## 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维 [[Back]](#toc)

多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行 reasoning、planning、visual thought 或 chain-of-continuous-thought。

### [1] [Latent Visual States for Efficient Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24233)
*Xiuwei Chen, Wentao Hu, Yongxin Wang, Zisheng Chen, Likui Zhang, Kun Xiang, Jianhua Han, Hui-Ling Zhen, Jingyuan Zou, Hang Xu, Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (78.3) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EVA 提出一种新框架，让多模态模型在推理过程中原生生成连续的潜视觉状态（Latent_slot tokens）作为中间视觉思维，替代传统依赖离散输出调用外部工具的方式，并通过 D-GSPO 解耦优化连续与离散组件，配合 EVA-230K 数据集训练，在多个基准上取得性能提升并提高推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言多模态模型在整合视觉证据时，主要依赖生成离散输出（如代码或框坐标）来调用外部工具，这引入了刚性的依赖关系和显著的延迟。作者希望克服这些限制，让模型能够原生地生成连续的潜视觉表征，作为推理过程中的中间视觉思维，从而提升效率和灵活性。

Method: 提出 EVA 框架，核心是让模型生成连续的 Latent_slot tokens（潜视觉状态）作为中间视觉思维，与离散文本 token 进行端到端联合训练。针对联合训练中 Latent_slot tokens 后"过渡窗口"出现的极端策略偏差问题，提出 D-GSPO（Decouple-GSPO）方法，将潜变量和离散组件的优化解耦。同时构建了 EVA-230K 高质量图文交错 CoT 数据集用于 SFT。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上，EVA 取得了显著的性能提升，同时提高了推理效率。

Conclusion: EVA 通过原生生成连续潜视觉状态作为中间推理思维，有效替代了传统依赖离散输出调用外部工具的方式，在提升多模态推理性能的同时降低了延迟，为多模态推理提供了一种新的高效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 EVA 框架，在推理过程中原生生成连续的 Latent_slot tokens 作为中间视觉思维（visual thoughts），属于多模态模型在连续潜空间中进行推理的典型工作，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "连续多模态推理与潜空间思维" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal reasoning with latent visual tokens, a top-priority subtopic, while improving inference efficiency. Signals: continuous latent visual tokens, multimodal reasoning without external tools, D-GSPO decoupled optimization, EVA-230K dataset, inference efficiency gain

Abstract: The integration of visual evidence has significantly enhanced the capabilities of large multimodal models. However, this integration predominantly relies on generating discrete outputs (etc., code or box coordinates) to invoke external tools, a process that introduces rigid dependencies and substantial latency. To overcome these limitations, we propose {EVA} (LatEnt Visual StAtes), a novel framework that natively generates continuous latent visual representations. These internal representations manifest as an adaptive sequence of Latent\_slot tokens, serving as intermediate visual thoughts during the reasoning process. These Latent\_slot tokens are then trained end-to-end with the discrete text tokens. This co-optimization, notably, causes extreme policy deviation in the 'transition window' following the Latent\_slot tokens. We develop D-GSPO (Decouple-GSPO) to target this root cause by decoupling the optimization of latent and discrete components. To support SFT, we construct EVA-230K, a high-quality text-image interleaved CoT dataset encompassing a diverse range of real-world scenes, documents, charts and OCR tasks. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks confirm that EVA achieves significant performance gains while enhancing inference efficiency.

</details>


### [2] [The Latent Bridge: A Continuous Slow-Fast Channel for Real-Time Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24470)
*Bojie Li, Noah Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续多模态推理与潜空间思维

Importance: 值得关注 (72.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent Bridge，一种在连续潜空间中连接慢速推理 VLM（Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking）与快速反应 VLM（MiniCPM-o 4.5）的通信通道，用于实时游戏智能体。相比传统的文本桥接（Text Bridge），Latent Bridge 在 7 个 Atari 游戏和 MetaDrive 驾驶域中匹配或超越文本桥接性能，在 MsPacman 和 RoadRunner 上分别提升 57% 和 28%，且增益与慢推理优于快反应的程度高度相关（r=0.93）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时游戏智能体需要在毫秒级响应（15Hz 控制循环）的同时具备秒级规划能力，而现有推理 VLM 延迟约 1.5 秒无法满足实时性，快速 VLM 则在规划密集型任务上表现不足。如何在不微调模型的前提下，将慢速推理能力高效注入快速反应模型，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 保持两个冻结 VLM（8B 推理模型 + 9B 反应模型）不变，仅训练通信通道。提出 Latent Bridge：将慢速推理模型的残差特征通过 LLaVA 风格的投影层映射到快速模型的输入嵌入空间，避免文本往返延迟。与 Text Bridge（慢模型写文本后缀供快模型读取）和 Fast-Only（无桥接）进行对比。在 7 个 Atari 游戏和 MetaDrive 上，每个通道独立调优动作解码器。

Result: Latent Bridge 在所有域中匹配或超越 Text Bridge：MsPacman 提升 57%，RoadRunner 提升 28%，其余游戏持平。两种桥接同时使用会破坏性干扰（RoadRunner -96%）。桥接增益高度可预测：当且仅当慢推理优于快反应时（T > F），Latent 和 Text 的增益高度相关（r=0.93）。MetaDrive 作为受控负例，Latent Bridge 无增益。

Conclusion: Latent Bridge 是一种安全、高效的连续潜空间通信通道，可在不微调模型的情况下将推理能力注入实时智能体。其增益可预测，且优于文本桥接。未来工作可探索更复杂的慢-快模型耦合策略及更广泛的应用场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Latent Bridge，将慢速推理 VLM 的连续残差特征通过 LLaVA 风格投影到快速反应 VLM 的输入嵌入空间，实现连续潜空间中的跨模型通信与实时游戏控制。这属于多模态模型在连续 latent 中进行推理与规划，与 canonical subtopic "连续多模态推理与潜空间思维" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous multimodal latent reasoning for real-time agents, a top-priority subtopic with strong empirical results. Signals: continuous latent bridge, real-time game agent, slow-fast VLM coupling, strong correlation r=0.93, matches top priority subtopic keywords

Abstract: A real-time agent for general computer use - with games as the most demanding case - must act within tens of milliseconds while still planning over seconds. These two regimes sit at opposite ends of the latency-quality tradeoff. A reasoning VLM (Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking) deliberates effectively but requires ~1.5 s per response - far too slow for a 15 Hz control loop. In contrast, a reactive VLM (MiniCPM-o 4.5) acts in milliseconds but underperforms on planning-heavy tasks. We couple two frozen models of matched scale (9B reactive, 8B reasoning), leaving the communication channel as the sole trainable component. The standard coupling is a Text Bridge (T): the slow model writes a suffix the fast model reads. We introduce a learned continuous Latent Bridge (L) that projects the slow model's residuals into the fast model's input-embedding space in a LLaVA-style manner, avoiding any text round-trip; both are compared against Fast-Only (F). On 7 Atari games and a driving domain (MetaDrive), tuning the action decoder per channel on held-out seeds, the Latent Bridge matches or beats the Text Bridge in every domain: it significantly improves two games (MsPacman +57%, RoadRunner +28%) and is a safe drop-in elsewhere. Combining both channels interferes destructively (RoadRunner -96%), so only one should be used. The benefit is highly predictable: the bridge helps if and only if slow reasoning already beats fast reaction (T > F) - the Latent and Text gains over Fast-Only move together at r=0.93. MetaDrive is the controlled negative, where the Latent Bridge is demonstrably inert because the Text Bridge adds no value. We release replay recordings and reproducible pipelines.

</details>

### [3] [Enforcing Human-like Kinematics in Dexterous Piano Playing via Adversarial Posture Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23848)
*Bin Qiu, Yanming Shao, Guanyu Cai, Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (51.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出对抗性姿态正则化（APR）方法，利用少量人类弹钢琴数据作为先验，通过对抗目标约束强化学习策略的姿态分布，使灵巧手在物理仿真中弹钢琴时呈现更自然、类人的手部姿态，避免关节过度伸展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可以训练灵巧手在物理仿真中弹钢琴并达到高音符准确率，但仅靠任务奖励或逆运动学往往导致不自然的姿态和关节过度伸展。现有方法需要昂贵的、与歌曲对齐的专家演示数据，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: 提出对抗性姿态正则化（APR），使用少量非对齐的人类弹钢琴数据（通过Meta Quest 3采集并重定向到Shadow Hand），通过对抗目标匹配策略姿态分布与人类先验分布，从而鼓励更类人的手部形状，无需歌曲对齐的专家演示。

Result: 在cPSI、BSE和FAC三个人类相似度指标以及视觉质量上均显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: APR能有效将人类姿态先验引入灵巧手强化学习策略，生成更自然、类人的弹钢琴姿态，且仅需少量非对齐的人类数据，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Classification reason: 该论文使用强化学习训练灵巧手在物理仿真中弹钢琴，属于机器人具身控制与策略学习，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on dexterous manipulation and human-likeness, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: robotics, dexterous manipulation, adversarial training, human-likeness, piano playing

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can train bimanual dexterous hands to play piano in physics simulation with high note accuracy, but for high-DoF dexterous hands, relying solely on task rewards or IK inversion often leads to unnatural postures and joint overextension. We propose \textit{Adversarial Posture Regularization (APR)}. It avoids expensive, song-aligned expert demonstration data and instead uses a small amount of casual human playing data. By matching the distribution of the posture of the policy with the human prior through an adversarial objective, APR encourages more human-like hand shapes. Meanwhile, we collect and release unstructured hand motion data of piano playing using a consumer-grade Meta Quest 3, and retarget the key motion information to the Shadow Hand. Finally, we achieve significantly better performance than prior methods on all three human-likeness metrics (cPSI, BSE, and FAC) as well as in visual quality. Project repository: .

</details>


### [4] [DynaWM: Dynamics-Aware Distillation with World Model and Momentum Targets for Smooth Locomotion over Continuous Stairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24089)
*Haidong Hou, Zhangguo Yu, Hengbo Qi, Jianlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (75.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DynaWM 提出一种动力学感知表示学习框架，通过引入世界模型作为正则化器增强地形几何编码能力，并利用动量目标编码器稳定知识蒸馏，使双足轮式机器人能够在连续楼梯上实现平滑运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教师-学生框架在处理连续楼梯场景时，存在动力学感知表示弱化以及地形几何编码不完整的问题，导致机器人难以在长序列楼梯上实现平滑运动。

Method: 提出 DynaWM 框架：(1) 引入世界模型作为正则化器，强制学习前向动力学感知表示，保留完整地形几何信息并支持层次化编码可视化；(2) 采用动量目标编码器提供一致的蒸馏目标，防止非平稳教师更新导致的维度坍塌。

Result: 通过 PCA 可视化和定量指标验证，编码器能够层次化捕获地形几何信息，具有更高的地形编码能力。仿真和真实硬件实验表明，该方法在多种连续楼梯场景下实现了优越的地形适应性和运动平滑性。

Conclusion: DynaWM 通过动力学感知表示学习和动量目标蒸馏，有效提升了双足轮式机器人在连续楼梯场景中的地形适应能力与运动平滑性，验证了世界模型作为正则化器在机器人控制中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DynaWM，利用世界模型作为正则化器增强双足轮式机器人在连续楼梯场景中的地形适应能力与运动平滑性，属于面向机器人控制与规划的世界模型方法，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Focuses on bipedal-wheeled robot locomotion, not directly aligned with user's priority on continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world model for terrain encoding, momentum target distillation, bipedal-wheeled robot, continuous stairs traversal, dynamics-aware representation

Abstract: Recent advances in control have enabled bipedal-wheeled robots to traverse slopes and single-step obstacles, yet long staircase traversal remains challenging as current teacher-student frameworks suffer from weakened dynamics-aware representations and incomplete terrain geometry encoding. To bridge this gap, we propose DynaWM, a dynamics-aware representation learning framework. To enhance terrain encoding capability and enable transparent assessment, we introduce a world model as a regularizer to enforce forward-dynamics awareness, preserving comprehensive terrain geometry while facilitating hierarchical encoding visualization. To stabilize knowledge transfer, we employ a momentum target encoder to provide consistent distillation targets, preventing dimensional collapse from non-stationary teacher updates. Evaluation of the learned representations through Principal Component Analysis (PCA) visualization and quantitative metrics reveals that our encoder hierarchically captures terrain geometry with higher terrain encoding capability, leading to enhanced terrain adaptability and motion smoothness. Experimental results in simulation and real hardware demonstrate that our method achieves superior terrain adaptability and motion smoothness, enabling bipedal-wheeled robots to overcome diverse continuous stairs, as shown in Fig. 1.

</details>


### [5] [Grounding Generative Policies in Physics: Optimization-Guided Diffusion for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24208)
*Sabrina Bodmer, René Zurbrügg, Tifanny Portela, Hao Ma, Alexandre Didier, Marco Hutter, Colin Jones, Melanie Zeilinger*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (83.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种推理时优化框架，将扩散模型的采样过程与约束优化相结合，在无需重新训练的条件下，使生成的任务空间机器人策略（抓取、轨迹等）满足物理可行性约束（可达性、避碰、可跟踪性），在灵巧抓取和动态操作任务上分别提升20pp和23pp的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能有效从高维多模态分布中采样，但生成的机器人策略（如抓取姿态、轨迹）可能在物理上不可行（违反可达性、碰撞约束等），导致不同机器人平台间零样本部署困难。现有方法需要重新训练或投影修正，难以兼顾生成质量与约束满足。

Method: 将扩散模型反向过程中的采样扰动替换为优化校正，通过约束优化问题施加硬约束或软惩罚，在保持样本接近学习先验的同时满足物理可行性约束。该方法无需重新训练扩散模型，仅在推理时进行优化引导的降噪。

Result: 在灵巧抓取（可达性与避碰约束）和动态操作（控制器级可跟踪性约束）实验中，优化引导降噪在保持抓取质量的同时，任务成功率相比投影法和梯度引导基线分别提升20pp（灵巧抓取）和23pp（视觉运动操作）。

Conclusion: 推理时优化引导的扩散模型能够有效弥合生成策略与物理可行性之间的差距，实现跨机器人平台的零样本部署，且无需重新训练模型，是一种实用且高效的机器人策略生成方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出优化引导的扩散模型用于机器人抓取和操作策略生成，解决物理可行性约束问题，属于具身智能中的机器人规划与控制，与[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics contribution but only tangential to user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: diffusion guidance for constraint satisfaction, inference-time optimization without retraining, dexterous grasping and dynamic manipulation, world model embodiment gap, no continuous language or video generation

Abstract: Diffusion models sample effectively from high-dimensional, multimodal distributions, but their outputs may violate deployment constraints. For task-space robot policies, generated grasps, waypoints, or trajectories can be distributionally valid yet infeasible, violating reachability, collision-avoidance, or closed-loop executability requirements. This embodiment gap limits zero-shot deployment across robots, even when the task-space behavior itself is transferable. We propose an inference-time optimization framework that couples the behavior generation to physical feasibility by formulating diffusion guidance as a constrained optimization problem. Our key insight is to replace the sampling perturbation in the backward process with an optimized correction, allowing hard constraints or soft penalties to be imposed during sampling without the need to retrain the diffusion model, while keeping samples close to the learned prior. We evaluate the method on dexterous grasp synthesis with reachability and collision-avoidance constraints, and dynamic manipulation with controller-level trackability constraints. Across settings and robot embodiments, optimization-guided denoising matches the feasibility of projection- and gradient-guidance baselines while better preserving grasp quality, and improving controller-level executability and task success, with task success improving by up to 20pp. on dexterous grasping and 23pp. on visuomotor manipulation over the best baseline.

</details>


### [6] [G$^3$VLA: Geometric inductive bias for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24472)
*Yue Peng, Yongzhe Zhao, Artur Habuda, Khuyen Pham, Yanheng Zhu, Tran Nguyen Le, Fares Abu-Dakka, Li Guo*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO

TL;DR: G³VLA 提出一种相机感知的几何模块，将标定后的相机几何结构（内参、外参）注入预训练 VLA 模型的视觉 token 流中，通过射线嵌入、投影位置编码和跨视图融合提升机器人操作性能，在 LIBERO、RoboCasa24 等基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型的视觉 token 基于 2D 图像坐标，忽略了机器人多相机系统中已知的内参和外参几何信息，导致多视图被独立处理而丢失了校准的几何结构，限制了空间敏感任务的表现。

Method: 提出 G³VLA，包含三个核心组件：(1) 内参条件射线嵌入（intrinsic-conditioned ray embeddings），将相机内参编码到视觉 token 中；(2) 投影位置编码（PRoPE），将 3D 空间位置投影到多视图共享的坐标系；(3) 双向跨视图融合（bidirectional cross-view fusion），实现多视图间的信息交互。几何监督来自 ground-truth 点图或置信门控的 π³X 教师预测，无需深度传感器或人工标注。

Result: 在 π₀ 上实例化后，在 LIBERO 套件、RoboCasa24、RoboTwin2.0 和真实机器人场景中均取得一致提升，在空间和物体敏感的任务上改进最大。在 π₀.₅ 和 GR00T 1.5 上也验证了有效性，表明几何感知 token 直接接入动作生成路径时迁移效果最佳。

Conclusion: 将校准的相机几何结构注入 VLA 模型的视觉 token 流是一种有效且通用的方法，能够显著提升机器人操作任务的表现，且不改变动作空间或模仿学习目标。

Classification reason: 该论文提出G³VLA，通过几何归纳偏置增强VLA模型的视觉token，面向机器人操作任务，属于具身智能中的视觉-语言-动作模型，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world_model and continuous multimodal representation priorities via geometric inductive bias injection into VLA visual tokens. Signals: world model, continuous visual token, geometric inductive bias, VLA, multi-camera fusion

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made rapid progress in generalist robot manipulation by harnessing semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language backbones, but their visual tokens remain grounded in 2D image coordinates rather than the calibrated geometry of the robot's cameras -- a mismatch especially pronounced in multi-camera setups, where views are coupled by known intrinsics and extrinsics yet processed as independent images. We propose G$^3$VLA, a camera-aware geometric module that injects calibrated structure into the visual-token stream of a pretrained VLA without altering its action space or imitation objective, combining intrinsic-conditioned ray embeddings, projective positional encoding (PRoPE), and bidirectional cross-view fusion. Geometric supervision is provided either from ground-truth point maps when available, or from confidence-gated $\pi^3$X teacher predictions, requiring no depth sensors or manual annotations. Instantiated on $\pi_0$, G$^3$VLA yields consistent gains across the LIBERO suites, RoboCasa24, RoboTwin2.0, and real-robot settings, with the largest improvements on spatially and object-sensitive tasks. We further validate on $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T 1.5, with results suggesting that geometric transfer is most effective when geometry-aware tokens have direct access to the action generation pathway. *Maggie Wang, Lars Osterberg, Stephen Tian, Ola Shorinwa, Jiajun Wu, Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.RO

TL;DR: InSight 是一个让 VLA 模型在原始动作层面可操控的框架，通过自动分割演示数据为带标签的原始动作基元，并利用 VLM 引导的数据飞轮自主获取缺失技能，使机器人无需人类演示即可学习新操作技能并组合完成长时任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽然能从演示中学习操作技能，但其能力受限于训练数据中的技能范围。现有方法无法自主获取训练数据中未包含的新技能，限制了机器人在开放环境中的适应能力。

Method: InSight 包含两个阶段：(1) 自动分割流水线，通过 VLM 计划分解和末端执行器位姿将演示数据分割为带标签的原始动作基元，赋予 VLA 原始动作可操控性；(2) VLM 引导的数据飞轮，识别完成新任务所需的缺失基元，自主尝试用 VLM 提出的低级控制执行缺失基元的演示，并自动标注、存储和集成成功演示到 VLA 训练集中。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的多种操作任务（如翻转方块、关闭抽屉、扫除、扭转、倾倒）中，InSight 无需任何人类演示即可学习这些目标技能。学到的基元可以组合执行新颖的长时任务，无需额外的人类演示。

Conclusion: 原始动作可操控性为 VLA 策略的持续技能获取提供了实用基础，使机器人能够自主扩展技能库并组合完成复杂任务。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 InSight 框架，通过 VLM 引导的自主技能获取使 VLA 模型在原始动作层面可控，实现机器人操作技能的持续学习与组合。这属于具身智能中机器人策略学习与规划控制的核心范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong robotics VLA skill acquisition but low alignment with user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: VLA steerability, primitive-action segmentation, VLM-guided data flywheel, autonomous skill acquisition, long-horizon task composition

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can learn manipulation skills from demonstrations, but their capabilities are bounded by the skills in the training data. We present InSight, a framework that unlocks autonomous skill acquisition by rendering VLAs steerable at the primitive-action level (e.g., "move gripper to the bowl", "lift upward", "pour the bottle"). InSight consists of two primary stages: (1) an automated segmentation pipeline that partitions demonstrations into labeled primitives via VLM plan decomposition and end-effector poses to enable VLA primitive steerability, and (2) a VLM-guided data flywheel that identifies missing primitives required to accomplish a novel task, autonomously attempts demonstrations of the missing primitives with VLM-proposed low-level control, and automatically labels, stores, and integrates successful demonstrations into the VLA training set. We evaluate InSight across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, including block flipping, drawer closing, sweeping, twisting, and pouring, without any human demonstrations of these target skills. Once learned, these primitives can be composed to execute novel, long-horizon tasks without additional human demonstrations. Our findings demonstrate that primitive steerability provides a practical foundation for continual skill acquisition in VLA policies. Project website: .

</details>

*Taekbeom Lee, Youngseok Jang, Jeonghwa Heo, Jeongjun Choi, H. Jin Kim*

TL;DR: ObsGraph 是一种以观察为中心的分层场景图，通过房间-视角-物体三层结构统一场景表示、检索与探索，在具身推理与探索任务中提升了成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在复杂陌生环境中执行任务时，需要自主探索并获取任务所需信息。现有方法在场景表示、信息检索与探索策略之间缺乏紧密耦合，导致探索效率低下。

Method: 提出 ObsGraph，一种以观察为中心的分层场景图，包含房间（粗粒度语义锚点）、视角（上下文物体共视关系）和物体（细粒度细节）三层。在此基础上进行粗到细的分层检索（受预算约束），并利用检索结果结构化探索候选空间，激活房间级探索、视角细化或前沿探索三种模式，实现表示、检索与自适应多尺度探索的紧密耦合。

Result: 在具身推理与探索基准上的实验表明，ObsGraph 相比基线方法在成功率和效率上均有显著提升，验证了结构化场景表示与基于信息缺口的目标导向探索的有效性。

Conclusion: ObsGraph 通过统一的分层场景图有效耦合了场景表示、检索与探索，为机器人在复杂环境中的自主信息获取提供了高效框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向具身推理与探索的分层场景图表示 ObsGraph，将场景表示、检索与探索统一，属于机器人具身智能中的世界建模与规划控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong in embodied world models but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Hierarchical scene graph, Embodied reasoning and exploration, Room-view-object layers, Coarse-to-fine retrieval, Adaptive multi-scale exploration

Abstract: Embodied reasoning and exploration are increasingly considered crucial abilities for robots operating in complex and unfamiliar environments. To accomplish tasks in such settings, an agent must identify and acquire the information necessary for the task through exploration. We propose ObsGraph, an observation-centric hierarchical scene graph that unifies scene representation, retrieval, and exploration. It retains visual evidence and organizes it into room-view-object layers: rooms provide coarse semantic anchors, views preserve contextual object covisibility, and objects store fine-grained details. On top of this representation, we perform coarse-to-fine hierarchical retrieval under a bounded budget, and crucially use retrieval outcomes to structure the exploration candidate space--activating room-level exploration, view refinement, or frontier exploration--thereby tightly coupling representation, retrieval, and adaptive multi-scale exploration. Experiments across embodied reasoning and exploration benchmarks demonstrate improved success and efficiency, highlighting the benefits of structured scene representation and more targeted information gathering driven by identified evidence gaps.

</details>

*Yuxin Zuo, Zikai Xiao, Li Sheng, Fei Huang, Jianhong Tu, Yuxuan Liu, Tianyi Tang, Xiaomeng Hu, Yang Su, Qingfeng Lan, Yantao Liu, Qin Zhu, Yinger Zhang, Bowen Yu, Haiquan Zhao, Haiyang Xu, Jianxin Yang, Jiayang Cheng, Junyang Wang, Lianghao Deng, Mingfeng Xue, Tianyi Bai, Yang Fan, Yubo Ma, Yucheng Li, Zeyu Cui, Zhihai Wang, Zhihui Xie, Zhuorui Ye, An Yang, Dayiheng Liu, Jingren Zhou, Ning Ding*

TL;DR: 本文提出Qwen-AgentWorld系列语言世界模型，通过三阶段训练（CPT、SFT、RL）在7个领域超过1000万条交互轨迹上训练，能够通过长思维链模拟智能体环境。同时提出AgentWorldBench评测基准，实验表明该模型显著优于现有前沿模型，并验证了世界模型作为解耦环境模拟器和统一智能体基础模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型是智能体推理与规划的核心认知机制，但现有语言模型在模拟复杂智能体环境方面能力有限。本文旨在探索基于语言模型的世界建模如何进一步推动通用智能体的能力边界。

Method: 提出Qwen-AgentWorld系列模型，采用三阶段训练流水线：(1) 连续预训练（CPT）注入通用世界建模能力；(2) 监督微调（SFT）激活下一状态预测推理；(3) 强化学习（RL）通过混合规则与评分奖励提升模拟保真度。训练数据涵盖7个领域超过1000万条真实环境交互轨迹。同时构建AgentWorldBench评测基准。

Result: Qwen-AgentWorld在AgentWorldBench上显著优于现有前沿模型。作为解耦环境模拟器，它支持可扩展可控的环境模拟用于智能体RL，效果超越仅使用真实环境训练。作为统一智能体基础模型，世界模型训练作为预热阶段可提升7个智能体基准的下游性能。

Conclusion: 基于语言模型的世界模型能够有效模拟多领域智能体环境，并通过两种互补范式（解耦环境模拟器与统一智能体基础模型）增强通用智能体能力，为智能体研究提供了新的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于语言模型的世界模型（Language World Model），用于模拟智能体环境、支持推理与规划，属于世界模型方向。其核心是构建能够模拟多领域智能体环境的基础模型，并探索世界模型在智能体强化学习和下游任务中的应用，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]（embodied_world_model）这一规范子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and continuous language model priorities with a novel language world model for agentic simulation. Signals: world model, continuous language model, agentic environment simulation, long chain-of-thought reasoning, RL training

Abstract: A world model predicts environment dynamics based on current observations and actions, serving as a core cognitive mechanism for reasoning and planning. In this work, we investigate how world modeling based on language models can further push the boundaries of general agents. (i) We first focus on building foundation models for agentic environment simulation. We introduce Qwen-AgentWorld-35B-A3B and Qwen-AgentWorld-397B-A17B, the first language world models capable of simulating agentic environments covering 7 domains via long chain-of-thought reasoning. Leveraging more than 10M environment interaction trajectories of 7 domains in real-world environments, we develop Qwen-AgentWorld through a three-stage training pipeline: CPT injects general-purpose world modeling capabilities from the state transition dynamics and augmented professional corpora, SFT activates next-state-prediction reasoning, and RL sharpens simulation fidelity through a tailored framework with hybrid rubric-and-rule rewards. To evaluate language world models, we present AgentWorldBench, a comprehensive benchmark constructed from real-world interactions of 5 frontier models on 9 established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that Qwen-AgentWorld significantly outperforms existing frontier models. (ii) Beyond foundation models, we further investigate two complementary paradigms through which world modeling enhances general agents. First, as a decoupled environment simulator, Qwen-AgentWorld supports scalable and controllable simulation of thousands of real-world environments for agentic RL, yielding gains that surpass real-environment training alone. Second, as a unified agent foundation model, world-model training acts as a highly effective warm-up that improves downstream performance across 7 agentic benchmarks. Code:

</details>

*Kuan-Yen Liu, Ren-Jyun Huang, Ti-Rong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: LaGO提出将预训练LLM作为潜在动作先验（latent action prior），通过软引导（soft guidance）的方式辅助在线强化学习策略优化，而非直接作为控制器。在CLEVR-Robot和Meta-World上，LaGO显著提升了PPO的成功率和奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将LLM直接用作规划器或控制器，要求LLM生成精确的动作，这在实践中不可靠。作者希望利用LLM的常识知识来引导策略学习，同时避免LLM直接控制带来的不稳定性。

Method: LaGO框架将预训练LLM编码为潜在动作先验（latent action prior），在在线强化学习过程中，该先验以软约束的形式引导PPO策略优化，而非直接输出动作。LLM的隐层表征被映射到动作空间，作为辅助损失项指导策略网络更新。

Result: 在CLEVR-Robot上，平均成功率从15.1%提升至27.2%；在Meta-World上，平均成功率从2.7%提升至15.2%。更强的预训练LLM提供更有效的引导。

Conclusion: LaGO验证了LLM作为潜在动作先验而非直接控制器可以有效提升在线强化学习的性能，为LLM与强化学习的结合提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文使用LLM作为潜在动作先验来引导在线强化学习策略优化，在CLEVR-Robot和Meta-World等具身/机器人控制基准上验证，属于具身智能中的规划与控制范畴，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]高度匹配。

Importance reason: Uses LLM as latent action prior for online RL, but focuses on embodied control rather than core continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: LLM as latent action prior, soft guidance for online RL, CLEVR-Robot and Meta-World benchmarks, improves PPO success rate, world model direction but not video world model

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for planning and sequential decision-making, but prior work often relies on using them as direct controllers, which requires precise action generation and can be unreliable in practice. This paper proposes Latent Action Guidance for Online Reinforcement Learning (LaGO), a framework that uses a pretrained LLM as a latent action prior to softly guide online policy optimization, rather than treating the LLM as an explicit planner or controller. Experiments on both a discrete-control benchmark, CLEVR-Robot, and a continuous-control benchmark, Meta-World, demonstrate that LaGO consistently improves both reward and success rate over Vanilla PPO. In particular, LaGO increases the average success rate from 15.1% to 27.2% on CLEVR-Robot and from 2.7% to 15.2% on Meta-World. Our analysis further shows that stronger pretrained LLMs provide more effective guidance, suggesting that LLM knowledge can improve planning and online decision-making.

</details>


### [11] [World Models in Pieces: Structural Certification for General Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24842)
*Yikai Lu, Yifei Wu, Xinyu Lu, Tongxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (80.66) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文证明通用智能体在大世界场景下无法做到普遍全能，标准最坏情况分析失效。为此提出结构性认证框架，通过深度组合目标过滤特定转移，给出世界模型逐项误差界 O(1/n)+O(δ)，实现可认证部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大世界（big-world）场景中，智能体无法在所有情况下都具备通用能力，其能力必然被特化到"碎片化"的世界模型中。标准的最坏情况分析无法区分关键瓶颈与无关失败，因此需要一种新的认证框架来定位智能体在哪些具体转移上具有可靠的长期规划能力。

Method: 首先形式化证明通用智能体不是全能的，标准最坏情况分析无信息量。然后提出结构性认证（structural certification），一种转移局部的框架，将有界目标条件性能映射到智能体内部世界模型的逐项保证。通过深度组合目标过滤特定转移，证明通用智能体在这些目标上具有结构性世界模型，误差界为 O(1/n)+O(δ)，且该界在小δ情况下是紧的。

Result: 给出了通用智能体结构性世界模型的误差界 O(1/n)+O(δ)，证明了该界在小δ情况下的紧性，并通过认证显式保证了该界的存在性。

Conclusion: 结构性认证框架使得通用智能体能够通过定位具体可靠的转移来实现可认证部署，为在大世界场景下安全使用智能体提供了理论基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通用智能体的世界模型结构性认证问题，关注世界模型在规划控制中的可靠性保证，与"具身世界模型与规划控制"这一规范主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong theoretical contribution to world model certification but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: world model certification, structural certification, general agent non-universality, transition-local framework, O(1/n)+O(δ) error bound

Abstract: In the big-world regime, agents cannot be universally capable and their ability is inevitably specialized across a world model in pieces. Consequently, standard uniform guarantees fail to distinguish between the understanding of critical bottlenecks and irrelevant failures. We first formalize this limitation by proving that general agents are not universal, rendering standard worst-case analysis uninformative. To overcome this, we introduce structural certification, a transition-local framework that maps bounded goal-conditioned performance to entry-wise guarantees on the agent's internal world model. Our main contribution is constructive. We provide algorithms that filter specific transitions using deep compositional goals and prove that a general agent on these goals has a structural world model with a $\mathcal{O}(1/n) + \mathcal{O}(\delta)$ error bound. Conversely, this bound is tight in the small-$\delta$ regime, whose existence is explicitly guaranteed by our certification. These results enable the certifiable deployment of general agents by localizing the specific transitions where long-horizon planning is reliable.

</details>

### [12] [Solving Inverse Problems of Chaotic Systems with Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24824)
*Peiyan Hu, Jian Zhang, Jiashu Pan, Ruiqi Feng, Tao Zhang, Zhi-Ming Ma, Yuan-Sen Ting, Gongjie Li, Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (91.14) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出双向条件流匹配（Bi-CFM）方法，用于求解混沌系统的逆问题（从最终状态推断初始状态），通过学习初始与最终状态分布之间的双向映射来捕捉混沌演化的随机性并缓解指数级误差累积，在经典混沌系统、三体行星散射以及真实球状星团观测数据上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混沌系统的逆问题（从最终状态推断初始条件）由于病态性、非唯一性、不稳定性以及时间反向动力学的混沌特性，长期以来难以解决。现有方法面临指数级误差累积和计算效率低下的挑战。

Method: 提出双向条件流匹配（Bi-CFM），学习初始状态分布与最终状态分布之间的双向映射，利用流匹配框架捕捉混沌演化的随机性并缓解误差累积。对于具有守恒律的系统，进一步提出守恒约束的 CBi-CFM，在流匹配过程中显式施加守恒约束。

Result: 在 Lorenz、Circuit 和高维 Lorenz 96 系统上，Bi-CFM 在五个分布级指标上优于基线方法，同时实现超过两个数量级的加速。在三体行星散射问题中，CBi-CFM 更好地遵守守恒律，守恒误差与真实轨迹相当。在真实球状星团观测上，该方法在精度上取得了显著进步。

Conclusion: Bi-CFM 为求解长时序真实世界混沌动力学的逆问题提供了一种可扩展的有效途径，在精度和效率上均优于现有方法，且能通过守恒约束进一步提升物理一致性。

Classification reason: 该论文研究混沌系统的逆问题求解，使用条件流匹配（flow matching）对混沌动力学进行双向建模，属于物理世界建模与动力学建模范畴，与 canonical subtopic "可交互与物理世界建模"（interactive_physical_world）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with world_model direction and chaotic system modeling, but lacks direct connection to priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: world model, chaotic system inverse problem, flow matching, conservation constraints, astrophysics application

Abstract: Modeling chaotic systems is crucial yet challenging. Inverse problems in chaotic dynamics, namely inferring initial conditions from final states, remain largely unsolved because of ill-posedness, non-uniqueness, instability, and potentially chaotic time-reverse dynamics. We address this open problem with Bidirectional Conditional Flow Matching (Bi-CFM), which learns bidirectional mappings between distributions of initial and final states to capture the stochasticity of chaotic evolution and mitigate exponential error accumulation over time. Furthermore, for systems with conservation laws, we extend it to Conservation-constrained Bi-CFM (CBi-CFM). Across the classic Lorenz, Circuit, and high-dimensional Lorenz 96 systems, Bi-CFM improves five distribution-level metrics over baselines while achieving a speedup of more than two orders of magnitude. In the three-body planet-planet scattering problem in planetary dynamics, CBi-CFM better respects conservation laws, with conservation errors comparable to those of the ground truth. Finally, on real observations of globular clusters, collisional million-body systems shaped by $\sim 10^{10}$ years (10 Gyr) of evolution, our method represents an advance in accuracy, establishing a scalable route to solving inverse problems of long-timescale real-world chaotic dynamics.

</details>

*Bingxuan Li, Yining Hong, Cheng Qian, Hyeonjeong Ha, Jiateng Liu, Zhenhailong Wang, Yue Guo, Yunzhu Li, Heng Ji*

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tailor-Bench，一个系统评估视觉世界模型在长尾物理交互中泛化能力的基准。通过设计常规、非常规和不可能三种场景模式以及预测生成和描述生成两种设置，揭示了当前图像和视频生成模型在模拟非常规物理交互时存在显著性能下降，表明模型依赖表面视觉模式而非真正的物理原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界模型在常见物理交互的基准上表现优异，但物理交互遵循长尾分布——大量罕见和不规则的交互未被充分研究。核心问题是：当前视觉世界模型是否真正内化和泛化了物理原理？

Method: 设计了 Tailor-Bench 基准，包含三种场景模式（常规、非常规、不可能）和两种评估设置（预测生成和描述生成），系统评估模型对物理交互的泛化能力。

Result: 从常规到非常规再到不可能场景，模型性能显著下降，揭示了物理世界建模中的长尾差距。图像模型无法实现正确的状态变化，视频模型还存在时序不一致问题。

Conclusion: 当前视觉世界模型依赖表面视觉模式而非真正的物理原理，在泛化到非常规物理交互时存在明显局限，需要更注重物理原理内化的建模方法。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Tailor-Bench，一个用于评估视觉世界模型在非常规物理交互中泛化能力的基准。这属于世界模型方向的评测工作，同时与多模态生成评测相关。现有 canonical subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）在 multimodal_generation 方向下，且 world_model 方向下没有专门的评测基准 subtopic，因此复用该 canonical subtopic 最为合适。

Importance reason: Directly evaluates world model generalization in long-tail physics, aligning with your high-priority world model and video generation subtopics. Signals: world model evaluation, long-tail physical interactions, video generation benchmark, generalization gap, Tailor-Bench

Abstract: Physical interactions follow a long-tailed distribution: a set of common and regular interactions dominates human experience and visual data, while a broad spectrum of rare and irregular interactions remains underrepresented. Although recent visual world models, including image and video generation models, achieve impressive realism on existing benchmarks, they primarily focus on simulating common physical interactions. This raises a central question: Do current visual world models internalize and generalize physical principles? In this work, we introduce Tailor-Bench, a benchmark that challenges world models to simulate irregular physical interactions. To enable systematic evaluation, we design three scenario modes that progressively challenge model reasoning: Regular scenarios reflect common tool-task pairs, Unconventional scenarios replace conventional tools with attribute-compatible substitutes to test affordance generalization, and Impossible scenarios introduce attribute-violating tools to probe constraint awareness. Additionally, we design two complementary settings under a unified evaluation protocol: predictive generation requires inferring outcomes without guidance, while descriptive generation specifies the target outcome for faithful realization. Our experimental results reveal a clear long-tail gap in physical world modeling: performance degrades from Regular to Unconventional and Impossible scenarios, indicating limited generalization beyond common interactions. Failure analysis further shows that models rely on superficial visual patterns: image models fail to realize correct state changes, while video models further suffer from temporal inconsistencies.

</details>

Result: 该方法可直接在真实世界观测数据上运行，生成可直接用于下游机器人规划和学习系统的数字孪生，无需仿真环境或人工标注，在铰接物体操作任务中展示了实用性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: ArtiTwinSplat 提供了一条实用、可扩展的数字孪生构建路径，降低了铰接物体操作在具身 AI 和人机协作中的集成门槛，为机器人系统在真实环境中的部署提供了有效支持。

Classification reason: 该论文通过3D高斯泼溅从RGB-D视频重建可交互的铰接物体数字孪生，属于3D场景/物体建模与动态场景中的世界建模，与[空间3D/4D世界模型]（spatial_3d_4d_world_model）高度匹配。同时其3DGS重建部分也与多模态生成下的3D生成与重建相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on articulated 3D reconstruction for robotics, which is not a top priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, RGB-D video, articulated digital twin, robotics, no CAD or annotation needed

Abstract: Deploying robots in unstructured real-world environments needs accurate, interactive models of the objects. Constructing these models at scale remains a critical bottleneck for robotic system integration. We present ArtiTwinSplat, a framework that automatically constructs articulated, photo-realistic digital twins of objects directly from RGB-D videos, requiring no CAD models, simulation assets, or manual annotations. Our method is built on 3D Gaussian Splatting that preserve geometric fidelity and photometric realism, coupled with an unsupervised articulation discovery pipeline that recovers part structure and joint kinematics from observed motion alone. With tracking and optimization stages our method provides stable, queryable digital twins that support real-time rendering, viewpoint control, and interactive manipulation. Unlike prior methods confined to simulation, ArtiTwinSplat operates directly on real-world observations and produces twins that are immediately usable by downstream robot planning and learning systems. This method offers a practical, scalable pathway toward digital twin construction, lowering the integration barrier for articulated object manipulation in embodied AI and human-robot collaboration contexts.

</details>

Conclusion: NavWM 通过统一潜世界推理、多模态动作预测与可控视觉生成，有效解决了传统导航策略的短视和模式坍缩问题，为具身导航中的世界模型应用提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 NavWM，一个统一的导航世界模型，融合潜空间世界推理、多模态动作预测与可控视觉生成，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）范畴，同时涉及多模态生成中的可控视觉生成。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model and navigation planning with latent tokens and multimodal trajectory forecasting, aligning with top priority subtopics. Signals: world model, navigation planning, latent world tokens, multimodal trajectory forecasting, visual foresight

Abstract: Conventional visual navigation policies often struggle with myopic decision-making and mode collapse in complex environments. While world models offer a promising alternative, existing paradigms typically isolate perception, generation, and control, failing to capture their shared spatio-temporal dynamics. In this paper, we propose NavWM, a unified navigation world model that seamlessly integrates latent world reasoning, multimodal action prediction, and controllable visual generation. At its core, NavWM leverages latent world tokens to distill geometric and semantic priors, endowing the agent with robust structural understanding. To overcome the limitations of deterministic policies, we introduce an anchor-based multimodal trajectory forecasting framework that generates a diverse action space. This inherent diversity explicitly empowers the generative world model to act as a robust closed-loop planner, utilizing visual foresight to evaluate and select the optimal path. Extensive experiments across diverse robotics datasets demonstrate that NavWM significantly advances the state-of-the-art, delivering remarkable improvements in both high-fidelity future state generation and zero-shot navigation success.

</details>


### [16] [Autonomous Video Generation with Counterfactual Controllability for Self-Evolving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24152)
*Xin Wang, Wenxuan Liu, Tongtong Feng, Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (97.15) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出新视角：具有反事实可控性的自主视频生成是实现自进化世界模型的关键路径。作者指出视频生成模型仅学到部分隐式时空世界模型，而非完全可接地或可控的模型，强调反事实可控性（询问在某个动作下会发生什么、测试生成未来是否满足具身约束）是决定性标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献声称视频生成本质上就是世界建模，但作者认为这一说法危险地依赖于"仅靠扩展视觉预测就能自动产生具身智能体"的信念。实际上，视频生成模型可能生成合理视频却不知道哪些变量可控、哪些约束属于特定身体、哪些未来在干预下仍然有效。因此需要超越预测真实感，追求反事实可控性以实现自进化世界模型。

Method: 本文是一篇观点/立场论文（perspective paper），并未提出具体技术方法，而是从概念层面论证了"具有反事实可控性的自主视频生成"作为自进化世界模型实现路径的合理性，分析了现有视频生成作为世界模型的局限性，并提出了反事实可控性作为决定性标准。

Result: 本文没有实验性结果，而是提出了一个概念框架和新的研究视角：将反事实可控性作为视频生成模型迈向真正世界模型的关键判据，并指出自主视频生成与反事实推理的结合是自进化世界模型的可行方向。

Conclusion: 视频生成模型学到的是部分、隐式的时空世界模型，而非完全可接地或可控的模型。反事实可控性——即询问在某个动作下会发生什么、测试生成未来能否满足具身约束、并将动作知识反馈到未来生成中——是实现自进化世界模型的关键。自主视频生成与反事实可控性的结合是值得探索的前沿方向。

Classification reason: 论文核心主张是"具有反事实可控性的自主视频生成是实现自进化世界模型的有前景途径"，直接讨论视频生成与世界模型的关系，属于世界模型方向下的视频世界模型（video_world_model）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。同时论文涉及视频生成方法，也匹配多模态生成方向。

Importance reason: Directly addresses the critical gap between video generation and grounded world models via counterfactual controllability, aligning with top-priority world model and continuous multimodal reasoning. Signals: world model, counterfactual controllability, self-evolving, video generation, autonomous

Abstract: Existing literature claims that video generation essentially is world modelling. On the one hand, the claim is productive because it pushes generative AI beyond static images and toward temporally extended physical scenes. On the other hand, this claim dangerously relies on the belief that scaling visual prediction alone will automatically yield physical agents. We prefer a more accurate statement: video generation models learn a partial, implicit spatiotemporal world model, but not a fully grounded or controllable one. The reason is as follows: a model may generate a plausible video of a drone crossing a forest or a robot arm manipulating a cup, yet still fail to know which variables are controllable, which constraints belong to a particular body and which futures remain valid under intervention. The frontier in essence is not predictive realism alone, instead it emphasizes a self-evolving generative nature that requires the decisive criterion to be counterfactual controllability: the capability of asking what would happen under an action, to test whether the generated future can survive embodiment constraints and to feed the resulting action knowledge back into future imagination (generation). Therefore, in this paper we present a new perspective, i.e., autonomous video generation with counterfactual controllability is one promising way to realize self-evolving world models.

</details>

Importance reason: Focuses on satellite-to-3D city asset generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative-keyword areas. Signals: 3D city generation, satellite image, textured mesh, structured-latent 3D foundation model, geometry flow

Abstract: Generating explicit 3D city assets from a single satellite image is important for digital twins, urban simulation, and geospatial intelligence. Unlike satellite-to-street-view synthesis, the task requires a reusable textured mesh with plausible geometry and controllable appearance rather than a 3D proxy optimized only for rendering a small set of images or videos. The ICCV Sat2City framework made a first step by conditioning cascaded sparse-voxel latent diffusion on satellite-derived height maps, but its appearance was random, its training data were synthetic, and its task-specific VAE did not scale well to noisy real-world reconstructions. We present Sat2City v2, a journal extension that adapts a pretrained native structured-latent 3D foundation model to weakly aligned satellite images and textured meshes. We build a real-world dataset with 16,241 satellite-mesh pairs across 24 regions in 9 cities. Instead of learning a 3D representation from noisy city meshes, Sat2City v2 encodes each mesh into a pretrained native 3D latent space, fine-tunes a satellite-conditioned geometry flow, and uses the decoded shape to anchor satellite-conditioned texturing. This retains Sat2City's geometry-to-appearance cascade while enabling appearance-controllable generation from the satellite input. Experiments on metric-scale DSM reconstruction and generative city-asset benchmarks for geometry and appearance show that Sat2City v2 achieves the best overall performance among evaluated baselines. Overall, Sat2City v2 advances satellite-to-city generation from rendering-oriented 3D proxies to explicit textured mesh assets, supported by, to the best of our knowledge, the first documented satellite-mesh paired dataset collected from matched geographic crops for this asset-level task. Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的风格化方法主要关注颜色外观的迁移，忽略了场景几何结构的适应性调整，导致风格化结果在结构层面不够自然。本文旨在同时迁移外观属性和几何结构，实现更完整的场景级风格化。

Method: 提出解耦优化方案，交替更新颜色参数和几何参数以避免两者相互干扰；设计几何感知对比特征匹配（GCFM），将RGB、深度和边缘线索整合到对比学习目标中，在两个优化阶段分别用于引导颜色和几何的迁移。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在定性保真度和定量指标上均优于现有基于3DGS的风格化方法，显著提升了风格迁移的质量。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在3DGS风格化中同时考虑颜色和几何结构迁移的有效性，解耦优化策略和GCFM模块为3D场景风格化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel geometry-aware style transfer framework for 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) that simultaneously transfers appearance attributes and geometric structures. Unlike prior works that primarily focus on color-based stylization and often overlook structural adaptation, our method explicitly incorporates geometry adaptation through a decoupled optimization scheme that alternately updates color and geometry parameters. This strategy alleviates potential interference between color and geometry updates, leading to stable and consistent scene-level geometry transformation. The decoupled optimization is enabled by the proposed geometry-aware contrastive feature matching (GCFM). GCFM integrates RGB, depth, and edge cues into a contrastive objective and is employed in both optimization phases to effectively transfer structural characteristics from style images to Gaussian primitives. Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves superior performance in both qualitative fidelity and quantitative metrics, significantly outperforming existing 3DGS-based stylization methods. Our code is available at \href{ }{ }.

Signals: 3D generation, compositional generation, multi-view consistency, Score Distillation Sampling, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in 3D generation have advanced notably with the development of text-to-image diffusion model. However, existing methods remain two practical challenges: (1) They primarily generate single 3D object, but struggle to generate multi-object compositional 3D assets due to the lack of the modeling for Gaussian primitives in reasonable interactions. (2) They often suffer from cross-view inconsistency during 3D optimization, as Score Distillation Sampling inherently performs on each single view, inevitably resulting in cross-view hallucinations. To solve above issues, we propose I2C-3D, a novel optimization-based method to generate multi-view consistent compositional 3D assets with reasonable interactions. Specifically, we propose an Inclusive Interactive Collisions strategy to guide Gaussian primitives appearing in reasonable interaction regions naturally, thereby ensuring objects in the compositional scene interact in a physically plausible and visually coherent way. Additionally, to enhance multi-view consistency, Multi-View Adaptive Score Distillation Sampling is devised to distill multi-view consistency prior and layout prior from pre-trained diffusion model by modulating attention map of instance token and spatial token across viewpoints. Benefiting from above elaborate designs, I2C-3D not only generates high-fidelity multi-view consistent compositional 3D assets but also supports 3D editing flexibly, facilitating complex scene generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate our I2C-3D outperforms existing methods in generation quality and multi-view consistency.

</details>

Abstract: High-quality 3D vehicle assets are essential for autonomous driving simulation. Although multi-view diffusion-based paradigms enable controllable single-image reconstruction, they typically produce limited viewpoints and exhibit cross-view geometric inconsistencies, thereby reducing reconstruction fidelity in real-world scenarios. In this work, we introduce 3DCarGen, a scalable single-view 3D car generation framework designed for real-world images by synthesizing an arbitrary number of 3D-consistent multi-view images. Specifically, given a single image as input, we first synthesize a set of images from fixed viewpoints. These images are then fed into a feed-forward reconstruction model, resulting in a coarse 3D representation based on 3D Gaussian Splatting. Conditioned on this explicit 3D prior, our multi-view diffusion model generates 3D-consistent images from arbitrary camera viewpoints. We further extend a fast mesh reconstruction algorithm by incorporating color-normal joint optimization to recover detailed and coherent 3D vehicle models from the synthesized dense views. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves robust geometric consistency and reconstruction fidelity compared to existing methods. Code and models will be released.

</details>

Abstract: Recovering realistic 3D vehicle models from autonomous driving scenes is crucial for synthesizing training data and building simulation environment. However, most existing vehicle generation methods fail to fully exploit multimodal sensors i.e. multi-view images and LiDAR point clouds) and rely on neural rendering based reconstruction, leading to low-quality mesh. Recently, native 3D generative models have made significant progress, yet they are not built for arbitrary multi-view inputs and often struggle with in-the-wild driving images. In this work, we present MM-TRELLIS, a multi-modal version of TRELLIS for in-the-wild 3D vehicle generation that integrates LiDAR and image sensors from autonomous driving datasets into native 3D generative models. Specifically, multi-view images are cycled as conditioning inputs, while LiDAR point clouds provide test-time guidance to ensure geometric accuracy and cross-view consistency. During denoising, we first align the guidance point cloud with the model priors, then enforce consistency between the generated geometry and the guidance point cloud. Finally, we introduce a voxel filtering strategy based on the opacity of 3D Gaussian Splatting to suppress floaters and produce clean meshes. Comprehensive experiments on Waymo dataset demonstrate our method outperforms existing methods in high-fidelity 3D vehicle generation. Code is available at .

Conclusion: 流匹配方法在医学点云补全中兼具生成质量和采样效率优势，PTv3 作为骨干网络进一步提升了性能与吞吐量，为医学点云生成提供了高效的新范式。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical point cloud completion, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords include medical. Signals: Medical point cloud, Flow matching, PTv3 backbone, Generative completion, SOTA on skull/mandible datasets

Abstract: Medical point cloud completion is important for anatomical reconstruction and downstream clinical workflows, yet generative modeling in this setting remains insufficiently studied. We investigate completion through continuous-time generative modeling and introduce PCFM, a PTv3-backed flow matching approach for medical point cloud completion. We evaluate on SkullFix and SkullBreak, and additionally on the more recent Mandibular Defect dataset. We build strong baselines by adapting PTv3 to a deterministic encoder-decoder completion model and by instantiating diffusion completion (PCDiff) with both PVCNN and PTv3 denoisers. PCFM with PTv3 is competitive with the deterministic PTv3 baseline and achieves state-of-the-art generative performance across datasets, while requiring substantially fewer sampling steps than diffusion. At the best operating points, PTv3 also yields clear throughput gains, providing up to a 7$\times$ speed-up for PCFM compared to a PVCNN backbone. Finally, we study empirical scaling trends by varying model size and point cardinality, showing consistent gains with higher point resolution and informative trade-offs across model scales.

</details>

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: OrbitForge 提出一种利用冻结的文生视频模型作为先验，结合可变形高斯泼溅重建优化，将单段文本生成视频转化为闭环轨道3D高斯泼溅场景的方法。通过重建锚定检测缺失视角并仅补全缺失视图，无需多视图微调或分数蒸馏，实现了全轨道覆盖的高质量3D场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频模型虽能生成高质量视频，但难以直接用于3D资产构建：相机运动难以控制、视角覆盖不完整、帧间存在时序不一致。现有方法要么需要多视图微调，要么依赖分数蒸馏优化，要么逐视图渐进生成，效率和质量受限。因此需要一种无需额外微调、能利用现成视频先验生成完整3D场景的方法。

Method: OrbitForge 包含以下步骤：(1) 从文本生成初始视频，使用可变形高斯泼溅（Deformable Gaussian Splatting）和鲁棒的 MedianGS 代理进行初步3D重建；(2) 从预设轨道渲染视图，检测缺失视角；(3) 仅对缺失视图，使用冻结的文生视频模型进行补全；(4) 将补全后的完整轨道视图重建为最终的高斯泼溅场景。整个过程无需任务特定的视频或多视图微调，也无需逐提示的分数蒸馏优化。

Result: 在冻结的300提示T3Bench测试集上，OrbitForge 重建的中位测量跨度达到359.0度；将原本不支持的Q10 ImageReward从MedianGS-only的8.07提升至16.36；在覆盖质量上与VideoMV保持竞争力。

Conclusion: OrbitForge 证明了利用冻结的文生视频模型作为先验，通过重建锚定的视角补全策略，可以有效生成高质量的全轨道3D场景。该方法避免了多视图微调和分数蒸馏，为文本到3D场景生成提供了一种简洁高效的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文利用文本生成视频作为先验，通过3D高斯泼溅重建优化实现文本到3D场景生成，核心贡献在3D场景生成与重建领域，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"。

Importance reason: Novel use of frozen video priors for 3D scene generation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Text-to-3D scene generation, Reconstruction-anchored video synthesis, Deformable Gaussian Splatting, No task-specific fine-tuning, Coverage-aware evaluation

Abstract: Generic text-to-video models can be used as rich open-world scene priors. Despite the high quality of today's generated videos, they do not directly yield reliable 3D assets: camera motion is difficult to control, view coverage is partial, and frames often contain inconsistencies across time. We introduce OrbitForge, an adapter built from frozen video priors and per-prompt Gaussian Splatting reconstruction optimization that converts a single text-generated video into a canonical closed-orbit 3D Gaussian Splatting scene. We use 3D reconstruction as an anchor to improve the 3D consistency of the generated video. We obtain a preliminary 3D reconstruction from a first generated video via Deformable Gaussian Splatting with a robust MedianGS proxy. We render views from a prescribed orbit to detect missing viewpoints. OrbitForge uses the text-to-video model to complete only the missing views, and reconstructs the completed orbit into a final Gaussian Splatting scene. This design requires no task-specific video or multiview fine-tuning, avoids per-prompt score-distillation optimization, and does not progressively generate views one step at a time. We further argue that this setting demands coverage-aware evaluation: local smoothness alone rewards methods that never attempt a full orbit. On a frozen 300-prompt T3Bench-derived audit, OrbitForge reconstruction attains a 359.0-degree measured median span, raises originally unsupported-bin Q10 ImageReward from 8.07 to 16.36 relative to MedianGS-only reconstruction, while remaining competitive with VideoMV on the coverage-quality.

</details>

*Haorui Ji, Weizhe Liu, Hongdong Li, Hengkai Guo*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: FLUX3D 提出了一种图像到 3DGS 的生成框架，通过 Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents (DA-SLAT) 和 Sparse-structure Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (SMDiT) 分别解决稀疏体素表示中的表征瓶颈和跨模态对齐瓶颈，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稀疏体素的图像到 3DGS 生成方法存在两个结构瓶颈：一是使用判别式 2D 特征（如 DINOv2）构建稀疏体素潜变量，这些特征优化于语义抽象而非重建，导致表征瓶颈；二是标准扩散 Transformer 缺乏有效机制将密集 2D 图像 token 与稀疏 3D 体素潜变量对齐，导致跨模态对应瓶颈。因此需要一种能同时提升表征学习和生成阶段跨模态对齐的方法。

Method: FLUX3D 包含两个核心设计：(1) Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents (DA-SLAT) —— 重新审视稀疏体素 3D 表示学习的 2D 特征选择，耦合解码器架构以提升 3DGS 重建保真度；(2) Sparse-structure Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (SMDiT) 和 Modal-Aware Rotary Positional Embedding (MARoPE) —— 实现几何无关的 2D-3D 对齐，使扩散模型能感知稀疏结构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，FLUX3D 在外观保真度上取得了显著提升，全面优于所有现有最先进方法，生成高质量的 3DGS 资产。

Conclusion: FLUX3D 通过 DA-SLAT 和 SMDiT 有效解决了稀疏体素 3DGS 生成中的表征瓶颈和跨模态对齐瓶颈，为高保真图像到 3D 生成提供了可扩展的框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FLUX3D，一种从图像生成 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 的高保真框架，属于 3D 物体生成与重建范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong 3D generation method but only tangentially touches user's priority on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: 3D Gaussian generation, diffusion transformer, sparse representation, image-to-3D, high-fidelity reconstruction

Abstract: Sparse voxel representation has emerged as a scalable foundation for image-to-3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) generation, yet current methods struggle to preserve high-frequency visual details of input images due to two structural bottlenecks. First, they adopt discriminative 2D features optimized for semantic abstraction to construct sparse voxel latents, which suppress reconstructive cues and induce a representation bottleneck. Second, in the generation stage, standard diffusion transformers lack effective mechanisms to align dense 2D image tokens with sparse 3D voxel latents, resulting in a cross-modal correspondence bottleneck. To address these issues, we propose FLUX3D, a scalable image-to-3DGS framework that boosts both representation learning and cross-modal alignment during generation. We first revisit 2D feature selection for sparse-voxel-based 3D representation learning, propose Diffusion-Aligned Structured Latents (DA-SLAT) and couple it with a decoder-only architecture to improve 3DGS reconstruction fidelity. We also design a sparse-structure-aware diffusion framework, which integrates the Sparse-structure Multimodal Diffusion Transformer (SMDiT) and Modal-Aware Rotary Positional Embedding (MARoPE) to achieve geometry-agnostic 2D-3D alignment. Extensive benchmark experiments demonstrate that FLUX3D yields substantial improvements in appearance fidelity and significantly outperforms all state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in generating high-quality 3DGS assets.

</details>


### [25] [FLAT: Feedforward Latent Triangle Splatting for Geometrically Accurate Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24876)
*Orest Kupyn, Goutam Bhat, Philipp Henzler, Fabian Manhardt, Christian Rupprecht, Federico Tombari*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 高优先级 (82.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FLAT 提出从视频扩散潜空间中直接解码三角形 splat（三角形面片）来生成几何精确的3D场景，通过射线中心旋转参数化和乘积窗口函数解决了三角形回归的梯度流动难题，在保持视觉质量的同时显著提升了几何精度，并支持轻量级测试时优化转换为游戏引擎就绪的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈潜空间场景解码器通常输出体素化的3D高斯（3DGS），缺乏明确定义的表面，难以用于仿真或标准图形管线。作者希望从压缩的视频扩散潜空间中直接解码出表面对齐的显式几何基元（三角形 splat），以获得既可渲染又接近显式几何资产的3D场景表示。

Method: FLAT 包含两个关键设计：(1) 射线中心旋转参数化（ray-centered rotation parameterization），用于三角形回归，降低对基元方向的敏感性；(2) 新颖的乘积窗口函数（product window function），在可微分三角形渲染过程中改善梯度流动。模型以视频扩散潜空间为输入，单次前馈即可解码出三角形 splat 场景表示。

Result: 在标准基准上，FLAT 相比最先进的前馈基线方法取得了显著更好的几何精度，同时保持了可比的视觉质量。轻量级测试时优化步骤可将预测的三角形 soup 转换为完全不透明的、支持实时渲染的游戏引擎就绪表示。作者还在相同训练设置下系统比较了3DGS、2DGS和三角形 splatting 变体的表示权衡。

Conclusion: FLAT 首次证明了可以从视频扩散潜空间中直接解码三角形 splat，实现了几何精度与视觉质量的良好平衡。其输出表示更接近显式几何资产，适用于仿真和标准图形管线，为前馈3D场景生成提供了新的表示范式。

Classification reason: 该论文从单张图像生成可探索的3D场景，使用三角形splat作为显式几何基元，属于3D场景生成与重建范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "3D 生成与重建"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel feedforward 3D scene generation from video diffusion latents with explicit triangle splats, boosting geometric accuracy for downstream use. Signals: 3D scene generation, feedforward decoding, triangle splatting, geometric accuracy, video diffusion latent

Abstract: Generating explorable 3D scenes from a single image requires strong generative priors and accurate geometric representations suitable for downstream use. Current video diffusion models offer high-quality generation and implicitly encode multi-view geometric structure in latent space. However, existing feedforward latent scene decoders typically output volumetric 3D Gaussians that lack a well-defined surface, limiting their use in simulation or standard graphics pipelines. This motivates decoding surface-aligned primitives that are not only renderable but also closer to explicit geometric assets. We ask whether compressed video diffusion latents can be mapped directly to explicit surface primitives in a single pass. To this end, we introduce FLAT and, for the first time, show that triangle splats can be decoded directly from video diffusion latents. Compared with decoding 3D Gaussians, predicting flat primitives is notoriously more challenging due to high sensitivity to primitive orientations, oftentimes leading to poor gradient flow. FLAT solves with two key ingredients: a ray-centered rotation parameterization for triangle regression and a novel product window function that improves gradient flow during differentiable triangle rendering. On standard benchmarks, FLAT achieves significantly better geometric accuracy while maintaining competitive visual quality compared to state-of-the-art feedforward baselines. We further show that a lightweight test-time refinement step converts the predicted triangle soup into a fully opaque, game-engine-ready representation that supports real-time rendering. By evaluating 3DGS, 2DGS, and triangle splatting variants under an identical training setup, we provide the first systematic analysis of representation tradeoffs in feedforward scene generation. The project page is available at

</details>

### [26] [DivRL: Disentangled Self-Similarity Rewards for Diverse Subject-Driven Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23950)
*Qian Wang, Zhenyu Li, Abdelrahman Eldesokey, Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DivRL 提出一种后训练框架，通过解耦的自相似性奖励（nSSM 衡量结构多样性，VSM 衡量身份一致性）和"探索-抑制"策略，在主体驱动图像生成中同时优化身份保持与结构多样性，缓解了"身份-多样性悖论"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主体驱动图像生成面临"身份-多样性悖论"：强身份保持往往导致输出僵化、多样性低。现有方法难以同时兼顾身份一致性与结构多样性。

Method: 提出 DivRL 后训练框架，引入负自相似性度量（nSSM）量化结构多样性，视觉语义匹配（VSM）评估身份一致性。采用"探索-抑制"策略：将 VSM 作为门控约束，模型自由探索多样化结构，仅对违反身份阈值的样本通过二次铰链损失进行惩罚，将身份保持从竞争目标转化为可行性约束。

Result: 实验表明，DivRL 在保持身份一致性的同时显著提升了生成图像的结构多样性，优于现有主体驱动生成方法。

Conclusion: 通过门控优化公式，身份保持与结构多样性可以联合提升，为缓解主体驱动生成中的身份-多样性矛盾提供了有效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究主体驱动（subject-driven）图像生成中的身份一致性与多样性平衡问题，属于多模态生成方向下的「主体与概念驱动生成」[CANONICAL] 子主题，直接匹配该子主题的关键词（subject-driven, customization, identity preservation）。

Importance reason: Addresses identity-diversity tradeoff in subject-driven generation, but falls outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous/autoregressive/diffusion video/image generation. Signals: Subject-driven generation, Identity-Diversity Paradox, Post-training framework, Disentangled self-similarity rewards, Explore-and-Suppress strategy

Abstract: Subject-driven image generation faces an "Identity-Diversity Paradox", where strong identity preservation often leads to rigid and low-diversity outputs. We propose a post-training framework called DivRL that jointly optimizes identity consistency and structural diversity simultaneously by leveraging disentangled visual features from a robust similarity model. Specifically, we introduce a Negative Self-Similarity Measure (nSSM) to quantify structural diversity, and Visual Semantic Matching (VSM) to evaluate identity consistency. We propose an "Explore-and-Suppress" strategy that treats VSM as a gated constraint: the model freely explores structurally diverse configurations, and only samples that violate the identity threshold are penalized via a quadratic hinge loss. This converts identity preservation from a competing objective into a feasibility constraint, allowing nSSM and VSM to improve jointly. Experiments demonstrate that our method effectively pushes the model to generate both consistent and diverse images and improves structural diversity while maintaining comparable identity consistency through a gated optimization formulation.

</details>


### [27] [Navigating User Behavior toward Personalized Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24196)
*Hengji Zhou, Yufeng Liu, Ye Liu, Yong Xu, Lianghao Xia, Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 主体与概念驱动生成

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NaviGen 提出利用用户交互历史（行为序列）进行个性化多模态生成，通过双标识符（协同编码+文本编码）统一表征行为与语义，并采用两阶段 SFT+RL 流水线实现偏好推理与指令写作，在商品、游戏、短视频三个领域提升了个性化图像/视频生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AIGC 流水线需要用户提供精细的创建指令，但终端用户很少能清晰描述视觉细节，导致生成结果与用户需求不匹配。因此需要一种方法，将用户的交互历史转化为可执行的生成指令，实现个性化内容生成。

Method: 提出 NaviGen 框架：(1) 为每个物品设计双标识符——协同编码（collaborative code）作为行为基底 + 文本编码（textual code）作为语义桥梁，统一在同一个 token 流中；(2) 两阶段 SFT+RL 流水线：第一阶段从进化搜索得到的监督信号中蒸馏偏好推理与指令写作能力，第二阶段通过层次化自一致奖励将生成与用户意图对齐。

Result: 在商品、游戏、短视频三个领域上的实验表明，NaviGen 提升了个性化图像和视频生成质量，增强了下一物品预测能力，生成了更具体、更相关、更可视觉生成的指令。

Conclusion: NaviGen 通过将用户行为历史编码为可被语言推理理解的 token 流，并设计两阶段训练流水线，有效弥合了用户行为与个性化生成之间的鸿沟，为个性化多模态生成提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于用户行为历史进行个性化图像/视频生成，属于主体与概念驱动生成（customization_generation）范畴，即利用用户交互历史（行为）作为个性化条件来引导生成，与"主体驱动、概念定制、个性化生成、ID保持、参考图引导生成"高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on personalized generation via user behavior, not on core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation methods. Signals: personalized generation, user behavior, instruction writing, reinforcement learning, not continuous language model

Abstract: Modern AIGC pipelines deliver high-fidelity images and videos but presuppose a well-formed creation instruction, while end users rarely articulate visual details, leaving generators misaligned with user demand. We study personalized content generation, which turns a user's interaction history into an executable instruction for downstream synthesis, and identify two obstacles: behavior must be encoded in a form legible to language reasoning, and the model must acquire instruction-writing skill absent from both pretraining and behavior data. We propose NaviGen, which represents each item with a dual identifier coupling a collaborative code and a textual code as a behavioral substrate and a semantic bridge in one token stream. On this representation, a two-stage SFT+RL pipeline first distills preference reasoning and instruction writing from evolutionarily searched supervision, then aligns generation with user intent through hierarchical and self-consistent rewards. Experiments across product, game, and short-video domains show that NaviGen improves personalized image and video generation, strengthens next-item prediction, and yields more specific, relevant, and visually generatable instructions. Our code is anonymously released at: .

</details>

### [28] [EPEdit: Redefining Image Editing with Generative AI and User-Centric Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24057)
*Hoang-Phuc Nguyen, Dinh-Khoi Vo, Trong-Le Do, Hai-Dang Nguyen, Tan-Cong Nguyen, Vinh-Tiep Nguyen, Tam V. Nguyen, Khanh-Duy Le, Minh-Triet Tran, Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (61.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EPEdit 是一个基于 Stable Diffusion 零样本图像编辑的应用，集成了后端框架与用户友好前端，支持掩码+提示词引导的多种图像编辑任务（对象替换/移除、背景修改、姿态变化、区域编辑等），无需额外微调，用户评估表明其优于现有解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像编辑工具（如Photoshop）需要大量专业知识，而现有生成式AI方案（如Stable Diffusion）往往需要昂贵的重训练和微调。作者希望开发一个既强大又易用、且无需额外微调成本的图像编辑应用。

Method: EPEdit 基于零样本（zero-shot）图像编辑算法，以 Stable Diffusion 为核心，通过掩码（mask）和文本提示词（prompt）引导编辑过程。系统包含后端框架和前端用户界面，用户可通过简单文本命令或标记区域进行精确调整，支持对象替换、移除、背景修改、姿态/视角变化、区域编辑和主题集合设计等多种功能。

Result: 用户评估表明，EPEdit 在图像编辑、主题设计和系统整体性能方面均优于现有解决方案（如Luminar Neo、Pixlr X、Canva等），提供了用户友好且成本效益高的综合图像编辑体验。

Conclusion: EPEdit 通过零样本 Stable Diffusion 方法成功实现了无需微调的高效图像编辑，验证了将生成式AI与用户友好界面结合的有效性，为图像编辑领域提供了一种低成本、易上手的实用解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出EPEdit图像编辑应用，支持对象替换/移除、背景修改、区域编辑等任务，属于图像编辑范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）。

Importance reason: Focuses on a user-friendly image editing application rather than advancing core generative AI methods or priority subtopics. Signals: zero-shot image editing, Stable Diffusion application, user-centric design, no fine-tuning, mask+prompt editing

Abstract: The demand for image manipulation has seen a significant increase recently. Traditional tools like Photoshop and Capture One, while powerful, require considerable expertise to use effectively. Generative AI has introduced alternative platforms, such as Luminar Neo, Pixlr X, and Canva. However, many of these solutions, including resource-heavy models like Stable Diffusion, often require substantial retraining and fine-tuning, leading to high costs for users. To address these challenges, we introduce Efficient Photo Editor (EPEdit), an application that integrates a robust backend framework with a user-friendly front-end interface. EPEdit supports a wide range of creative image editing tasks, including image generation, object replacement, object removal, background modification, changes in object pose or perspective, region-specific editing, and thematic collection design, all guided by masks and prompts. Users can interact with the system through simple text commands or by marking areas for precise adjustments, making it accessible even to those without technical expertise. At its core, EPEdit leverages zero-shot image editing algorithms based on Stable Diffusion model, removing the need for additional fine-tuning. This approach enables efficient image manipulation and thematic collection creation. User evaluations for tasks of image editing, thematic design, and overall system performance demonstrate that EPEdit outperforms existing solutions, offering a user-friendly, cost-effective solution for comprehensive image editing.

</details>


### [29] [Geometry-Instructed Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24225)
*Chirui Chang, Xiaoyang Lyu, Yi-Hua Huang, Haoru Tan, Shizhen Zhao, Yikang Ding, Jianmin Bao, Xin Tao, Pengfei Wan, Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

Importance: 普通相关 (53.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GIVE 是一个几何指令驱动的视频编辑框架，通过统一的物体状态公式表示编辑操作，利用深度框和方向框两个视频对齐的几何流来描述编辑前后物体的3D状态变化，并借助可扩展的图形引擎管线生成成对监督数据，实现了具有时间一致性和一致二次效果（如阴影、反射）的物体级几何视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物体级几何编辑（平移、旋转、缩放、复制、删除）是数字内容创作中的常规操作，但现有生成式视频编辑方法在这些操作上不可靠。核心挑战在于：如何在跨视角和时间维度上明确指定目标物体的3D状态变化，同时一致地更新与几何相关的二次效果（如阴影和反射）。

Method: 提出 GIVE 框架：(1) 通过统一的物体状态公式表示编辑操作；(2) 设计两个视频对齐的几何流——深度框（depth-box）编码粗略的3D位置和范围，方向框（orientation-box）提供与外观无关的方向线索，共同构成编辑前后物体状态变化的紧凑几何规范；(3) 构建可扩展的图形引擎管线，执行物体级编辑程序并渲染受控的编辑前后配对数据，隔离几何编辑的同时保持二次效果与变换一致。

Result: 实验表明 GIVE 能生成具有时间一致性和一致二次效果的忠实几何编辑，在统一框架下支持多种操作，并展现出向真实世界视频迁移的潜力。

Conclusion: GIVE 通过几何指令和图形引擎生成的配对数据，有效解决了视频中物体级几何编辑的难题，实现了时间一致且二次效果合理的编辑结果，为视频编辑提供了更可控、更可靠的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出几何指令驱动的视频编辑框架 GIVE，专注于视频中物体的几何级编辑（平移、旋转、缩放、复制、删除等），属于视频编辑范畴。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）。

Importance reason: Video editing with geometry control is relevant but not a top priority subtopic, and lacks direct connection to continuous models or world models. Signals: video editing, geometry instruction, object-level editing, graphics engine pipeline, temporal consistency

Abstract: Object-level geometric edits, including translating, rotating, scaling, duplicating, or removing an object, are routine operations in digital content creation (DCC) workflows, yet they remain unreliable in generative video editing. The key challenge lies in specifying the target object's 3D state change unambiguously across viewpoint and time, while consistently updating geometry-dependent secondary effects such as shadows and reflections. We introduce GIVE, a geometry-instructed video editing framework that represents edits through a unified object-state formulation. Two video-aligned geometry streams describe the target object before and after editing: a depth-box encoding coarse 3D placement and extent, and an orientation-box providing an appearance-agnostic orientation cue. Together, these streams provide a compact pre/post geometric specification for object-state transitions. To provide paired supervision for learning these edits, we build a scalable graphics-engine pipeline that executes object-level edit programs and renders controlled before/after pairs, isolating the intended geometric edit while keeping secondary effects consistent with the transformation. Experimental results demonstrate that GIVE produces faithful geometric edits with temporal coherence and consistent secondary effects across operators in a unified framework, and shows promising transfer to in-the-wild videos. Project page:

</details>


### [30] [UniRED: Unified RGB-D Video Frame Interpolation with Event Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24282)
*Yinuo Zhang, Guangshun Wei, Yuanfeng Zhou, Yiran Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: 本文提出UniRED，一个统一的多模态RGB-D视频帧插值框架，联合利用RGB外观、深度几何和事件相机时序线索，通过双向光流估计与软混合合成高帧率RGB-D视频，并构建了新的RGB-D-Event数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-D相机受硬件限制帧率较低，难以捕捉快速动态场景；现有RGB视频插值方法直接应用于RGB-D场景会产生模糊边界、伪影和几何一致性退化；仅靠两帧边界帧进行运动估计在复杂动态场景中约束不足。

Method: 提出统一多模态框架UniRED：1) 提取并融合RGB、深度和事件线索；2) 通过运动基元细化估计双向光流（RGB）和Z轴细化（深度）；3) 通过双向warping和软混合合成目标RGB-D帧。同时构建了RGB-D-Event三模态训练数据集。

Result: 在公开基准和自建数据集上的实验表明，该方法在RGB插值的光度保真度和深度插值的几何精度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 事件相机提供的超高时间分辨率异步测量能有效补充RGB-D帧插值中的运动线索，统一多模态融合框架可显著提升RGB-D视频帧插值的质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究RGB-D视频帧插值（video frame interpolation），属于视频编辑/增强范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on RGB-D video interpolation with event cameras, which is not aligned with the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: RGB-D video interpolation, event camera, multimodal fusion, depth geometry, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: High frame-rate RGB-D videos are crucial for a variety of downstream tasks, including motion analysis, dynamic scene understanding, and 3D reconstruction. However, due to hardware and sensing constraints, practical RGB-D cameras are typically limited to low frame rates, making it difficult to capture rapid scene dynamics. Existing video interpolation methods have achieved strong performance on RGB data, but they are not readily applicable to RGB-D scenarios, where they often yield blurry boundaries, visible artifacts, and degraded geometric consistency. Furthermore, motion estimation from only two boundary frames is inherently under-constrained in complex dynamic scenes. Event cameras, by contrast, provide asynchronous measurements with ultra-high temporal resolution, offering dense motion cues. In this paper, we propose a unified multimodal framework for RGB-D video interpolation that jointly exploits RGB appearance, depth geometry, and event-based temporal cues. Specifically, it first extracts and fuses RGB, depth and event cues, then estimates bidirectional flow with motion basis refinement for RGB and Z-axial refinement for depth, and finally synthesizes the target RGB-D frame via bidirectional warping and soft blending. In addition, we construct a new RGB-D-Event dataset to alleviate the scarcity of tri-modal training data. Extensive experiments on a public benchmark and the proposed dataset demonstrate that our method achieves superior photometric fidelity for RGB interpolation and stronger geometric accuracy for depth interpolation than existing approaches.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 大量实验表明，TIGER 在身份保真度和时序稳定性方面均达到最先进水平，实现了高质量、高效且身份一致的人脸视频修复。

Conclusion: TIGER 通过结构化三先验融合框架有效解决了人脸视频修复中的身份偏移、视角纠缠和真实感三大挑战，结合一步整流流实现了高效推理，为高质量人脸视频修复提供了新的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人脸视频修复（Face Video Restoration），属于视频质量增强与恢复任务，与图像与视频编辑（visual_editing）这一[CANONICAL]子主题高度相关，因为视频编辑范畴涵盖视频修复、增强等底层视觉任务。

Abstract: Face Video Restoration (FVR) aims to recover high-fidelity facial videos from degraded input while preserving identity and semantic consistency across frames. Existing methods often struggle to simultaneously address three key challenges: identity shift, viewpoint-entangled guidance, and perceptual realism. To tackle these issues, we propose TIGER, a structured tri-prior fusion framework that Tames Identity, Geometry, and gEnerative pRiors for high-quality FVR. Specifically, an Identity Prior is first established by injecting subject-discriminative embeddings into the latent space, effectively anchoring the subject's identity against severe degradations. Then, to provide temporally consistent structural guidance for dynamic videos, TIGER constructs a Geometry Prior by lifting 2D reference cues into a disentangled 3D parameter space, creating a geometric anchor through cross-source parameter fusion. Moreover, to achieve maximum efficiency without compromising realism, we harness the video generation model's Generative Prior through a one-step rectified flow. We further design a progressive three-stage training optimization strategy that refines structural fidelity, textural reconstruction, and distribution-level realism to ensure robust optimization. We also construct a large-scale FVR dataset to facilitate robust training and standardized evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TIGER achieves state-of-the-art performance in both identity fidelity and temporal stability, delivering a high-quality, efficient and identity-consistent FVR. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步扩散编辑器速度快，但固定的更新强度无法同时满足不同编辑类型下"激进实现目标提示"和"保守保留源图像"的双重需求，现有方法存在这一矛盾。

Method: 将问题建模为能量场传输上的后验候选选择问题。首先估计提示-增量场的局部时间曲率，将修正方向投影回原始一阶能量场传输估计的更新范数上，构建从源图像到强编辑的残差路径，保留原始一阶输出作为候选，最后通过最大化目标提示CLIP对齐度选择最终图像。

Result: 在PIE-Bench++的700样本、10种编辑类型上，该方法在所有单步更新算法中达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过后验候选选择策略而非设计新编辑模型，可以有效解决单步扩散编辑中保持与忠实性之间的权衡问题，为单步图像编辑提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种单步图像编辑方法（Riemannian Residual Line Search），属于图像编辑范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "visual_editing"（图像与视频编辑）。

Importance reason: Focuses on one-step image editing, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: One-step diffusion editing, Riemannian geometry, CLIP alignment, PIE-Bench++ SOTA, no inversion needed

Abstract: One-step diffusion editors are fast because they avoid inversion and iterative optimization, but a single transport update must be aggressive enough to realize the target prompt and conservative enough to preserve the source image--and no fixed update strength satisfies both demands across edit types. We treat this tension as a post-hoc candidate-selection problem on top of energy-field transport rather than as a new editing model. Our proposed method, Riemannian Residual Line Search, first builds a stronger edit by estimating the local time curvature of the prompt-delta field and projecting the corrected direction back onto the update norm of the original first-order energy-field transport estimation. It then forms a small residual path from the source image to this strong edit, retains the original first-order output as one candidate, and picks the final image by maximizing target-prompt CLIP alignment. On a 700-sample PIE-Bench++ evaluation across 10 edit type IDs, our method achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance among current one-step update algorithms.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce FiCA, a Feed-forward, instant Gaussian Codec Avatar generation pipeline that creates lifelike avatars from a single portrait image. Generating a photorealistic and drivable avatar from just a single image is significantly challenging due to the limited visual information available to accurately infer the 3D appearance and geometry of human heads. To address this, we develop a novel system that combines human-centric vision foundation models with a diffusion model. This system is designed to fully exploit partial visual observations to generate lifelike human avatars. Our proposed diffusion model learns a generative mapping from these partial observations to complete and authentic 3D mesh reconstruction. Additionally, we introduce a feed-forward mesh refinement network that enhances the fidelity and identity preservation of the generated avatars, eliminating the need for person-specific test-time optimization. By leveraging a universal prior model that decodes a generated mesh into a set of 3D Gaussians, we generate a photorealistic 3D Gaussian avatar, capable of being driven with novel expressions in real-time. Our experiments demonstrate that the avatars generated by our feed-forward approach faithfully represent diverse identities and surpass the visual quality of avatars produced by recent competing methods.

</details>

<details>
