Motivation: 文本到单人运动生成已取得显著进展，但扩展到文本驱动的3D人体交互生成仍具挑战，因为交互需要建模控制阶段进展、角色分工和双人协调的社会结构。现有方法缺乏对社会结构的显式建模。

Method: 提出"Think with LLM, Move with Motion Skill"的规划-执行范式。LLM规划器将隐式交互语义分解为阶段并分配角色，对齐到运动序列；运动执行器通过LoRA微调、前一阶段自条件（previous-phase self-conditioning）和自我-他人相对条件（ego-relative partner conditioning）将预训练的单人运动模型适配为协调的双人运动。

Result: 在InterHuman和InterX数据集上的实验表明，该方法在阶段一致性、角色对齐和双人协调方面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 社会结构建模对3D人体交互生成至关重要，LLM擅长推理交互结构但无法直接生成物理合理的运动，将LLM规划与运动技能执行相结合是一种有效的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本驱动的3D人体交互动作生成，属于人体动作生成范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Novel social structure reasoning for interaction generation, but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: 3D human interaction generation, LLM planner-executor, social structure modeling, text-to-motion, LoRA adaptation

Abstract: Although text-to-motion generation has achieved strong progress in synthesizing realistic single-person motions from language, extending it to text-driven 3D human-human interaction (HHI) remains non-trivial, as HHI requires modeling the underlying \textbf{social structure} that governs phase progression, actor roles, and inter-actor coordination. In this paper, we formulate HHI generation as a social structure modeling and grounding problem: the model must first infer how an interaction unfolds and how the two actors coordinate their roles, and then realize this structure as continuous, physically plausible, and partner-aware 3D motion. To study how such structure should be modeled, we first examine the capability boundary of large language models (LLMs) for HHI generation. Our analysis shows that LLMs can \textit{think} by recovering phase decompositions and partner-aware roles, but cannot directly \textit{move}, as they fail to generate dynamic, physically plausible, and interaction-aware motion. This motivates our planner-executor paradigm, \textbf{Think with LLM, Move with Motion Skill}. The LLM planner converts implicit interaction semantics into motion-aligned social supervision by decomposing interactions into phases, assigning partner-aware actor roles, and aligning them with motion sequence. The motion executor then grounds the planned social structure into coordinated two-person motion by adapting a pretrained solo motion model with LoRA, previous-phase self-conditioning, and ego-relative partner conditioning. Together, our Solo-to-Social framework bridges social organization and motion realization, producing 3D HHI with improved phase consistency, role alignment, and partner-aware coordination.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型中，token 序列空间缺乏一致的可访问结构：语义相似的图像可能对应不同措辞、顺序和概念位置的 token 序列，而相似的 token 序列可能编码截然不同的语义。这种结构缺失使得在该空间中进行平滑过渡变得困难，阻碍了图像混合和连续编辑等应用。

Method: 提出 Token-to-Token 对齐框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 结构对齐——将提示重写为共享的结构化形式（如 "A photo of [subject] with [attribute]"），使语义对应的概念映射到一致位置；(2) 嵌入级对齐——基于语义相似度匹配跨提示的 token 表示。对齐后，简单的线性插值即可产生有意义的语义过渡。

Result: 实验表明，对齐后的文本嵌入空间能够实现平滑连贯的语义过渡，支持图像混合和连续编辑等应用。结果表明文本到图像模型中的文本嵌入空间隐式编码了连续语义结构，只需对齐表示即可访问，无需修改生成模型本身。

Conclusion: 文本嵌入空间在适当对齐后具有可访问的连续语义结构，语义控制可以通过组织现有表示而非修改生成模型来实现。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本到图像生成模型中文本嵌入空间的对齐方法，以改进语义混合和连续编辑，属于文生图与图像扩散模型的基础方法范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "文生图与图像扩散"。

Importance reason: Focuses on text embedding alignment for semantic blending, which is not a core priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's high-weight directions like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Text embedding alignment, semantic blending, image editing, not autoregressive or video, no continuous token or world model keywords

Abstract: In modern generative models, images are specified and controlled through text prompts. In practice, images are generated from sequences of tokens derived from these prompts. However, the space of token sequences lacks a consistent accessible structure: semantically similar images may correspond to sequences that differ in wording, ordering, and placement of concepts, while similar token sequences may encode very different semantics. This apparent lack of structure makes it difficult to perform smooth transitions in this space, hindering applications such as image blending and continuous control of edits. We argue that this limitation stems not from the absence of semantic structure, but from misalignment between representations. To address this misalignment, we introduce Token-to-Token alignment, a framework that establishes explicit semantic correspondence between tokens across prompts. Our approach transforms prompts into a structured representation in which semantically corresponding concepts are mapped to consistent positions across prompts, and then aligns their token embeddings based on semantic similarity. Concretely, the method consists of two stages: a structural alignment that rephrases prompts into a shared structured form, followed by an embedding-level alignment that matches token representations across prompts. With this alignment in place, simple linear interpolation becomes a meaningful operation, producing smooth and coherent semantic transitions and enabling applications such as blending and continuous editing. Our results show that text embedding spaces in text-to-image models implicitly encode a continuous semantic structure that becomes accessible once representations are properly aligned, suggesting that semantic control can be achieved by organizing existing representations rather than modifying the generative model.

</details>

Result: 合成图像 MS-SSIM > 0.98，专家定性评估与结构相似性结果一致。合成数据有效支持了缺陷检测、分割和计量学等下游 ML 训练，同时保持了统计和物理真实性。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在极端数据稀缺条件下，DDPM 框架能够生成高保真 TEM 图像，满足半导体计量学的严格要求。该方法不仅解决了数据稀缺问题，还通过特征复用实现了分割能力，为半导体领域的 ML 应用提供了可行的数据增强方案。

Importance reason: Highly specialized TEM image generation with extreme data scarcity, but far from user's priority topics in continuous multimodal models, video generation, or world models. Abstract: Advanced semiconductor nodes drastically increased demand for Transmission Electron Microscopy (TEM), yet destructive sample preparation, slow imaging and high costs severely limit the availability of diverse datasets needed for downstream machine learning (ML). Synthetic data generation is becoming essential, but current generative models often miss TEM-specific noise, structural detail, and stochastic variability crucial for evaluation. We present a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) framework for synthetic TEM image generation under extreme data scarcity. A progressive patch-based training strategy scales from low-resolution patches to full images, enabling from-scratch training with only 15 samples. We integrate a custom TrivialAugment adaptation, cross-process domain transfer, classifier guidance, and RePaint-style inpainting, culminating in full-image generation that preserves global structural and spatial relationships in compliance with FAB metrology requirements. Beyond synthesis, we repurpose DDPM feature representations for segmentation, partitioning encoder feature maps to obtain coherent region masks. Our synthetic images achieve up to MS-SSIM > 0.98 and qualitative expert assessment consistent with structural similarity results, facilitating downstream ML training for defect detection, segmentation, and metrology while preserving statistical and physical realism.

</details>


### [37] [IV-CoT: Implicit Visual Chain-of-Thought for Structure-Aware Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24849)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出 Implicit Visual Chain-of-Thought (IV-CoT) 框架，将视觉条件查询分解为结构查询和语义查询的级联：结构查询先生成隐式视觉规划（latent visual plan），语义查询再基于该规划渲染外观。引入训练时草图监督（sketch supervision），通过草图损失引导结构查询捕获结构信息，推理时无需草图输入或中间解码，单次前向传播完成隐式 CoT 推理。

Result: 在 GenEval 和 T2I-CompBench 上取得优于现有方法的性能。可视化分析表明结构查询和语义查询在结构感知生成中扮演互补角色。

Conclusion: IV-CoT 通过隐式视觉思维链有效解耦结构规划与外观渲染，提升了文生图的结构感知提示遵循能力，且推理高效（单次前向传播）。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 IV-CoT，一种面向结构感知文生图的隐式视觉思维链方法，属于文本到图像生成方向。虽然涉及 MLLM 和视觉推理，但其核心任务是 text-to-image generation，因此主方向为多模态生成，匹配 [CANONICAL] 子主题"文生图与图像扩散"。

Importance reason: Proposes implicit visual chain-of-thought for structure-aware text-to-image generation, aligning with user's multimodal generation and continuous representation interests. Signals: structure-aware generation, implicit visual chain-of-thought, query decomposition, training-only sketch supervision, GenEval leaderboard

Abstract: Unified multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong text-to-image generation quality, but still struggle with structure-aware prompt following, where object counts, spatial relations, attribute bindings, and coarse layouts must be preserved. We attribute this limitation in part to the entanglement of structural planning and appearance rendering within a single conditioning stream. To address this issue, we propose Implicit Visual Chain-of-Thought (IV-CoT), a latent visual reasoning framework for query-conditioned image generation. IV-CoT decomposes the visual conditioning queries into a structural-to-semantic cascade, where structural queries first form a latent visual plan and semantic queries then render appearance conditioned on this plan. To guide the structural queries, we introduce training-only sketch supervision, which encourages them to capture structure from sketches without requiring sketch extraction or intermediate decoding at inference time. IV-CoT performs implicit CoT reasoning in a single forward pass and achieves superior results on GenEval and T2I-CompBench. Visualizations and analyses demonstrate that the learned structural and semantic queries play complementary roles in structure-aware generation.

</details>

Abstract: Short dramas, with their rapid shot rhythms, dialogue-driven focus shifts, and demanding cinematographic grounding, pose challenges that prompt-level or text-only video generation pipelines struggle to meet. We study plot-to-short-drama generation, where a global plot and local context are transformed into visually grounded multi-shot videos. We propose DramaDirector, a geometry-grounded framework that lets the planner borrow cinematographic geometry from a gallery of real short-drama shots indexed by depth and pose. DramaDirector decouples each shot into static visual and dynamic narrative conditions, trains the planner with schema-constrained SFT and GRPO under a learned text-visual alignment reward, and retrieves depth-pose references to guide first-frame generation and image-to-video synthesis. We also introduce DramaBoard, a benchmark built from 35 live-action dramas, 2.8K episodes, and 81K shots, with structured storyboards and multi-dimensional evaluation protocols. Experiments show that DramaDirector improves over representative multi-agent and video generation baselines on faithfulness, consistency, and controllability. Our code is released at:

*Yitong Li, Junsong Chen, Haopeng Li, Haozhe Liu, Jincheng Yu, Ligeng Zhu, Ping Luo, Song Han, Enze Xie*

TL;DR: Sol Video Inference Engine 是一个面向视频扩散模型的智能体驱动全栈推理加速框架，通过缓存、稀疏注意力、token剪枝、量化与算子融合五种技术的自动化组合优化，在64B Cosmos3-Super、22B LTX-2.3和2B SANA-Video三个模型上实现2倍以上端到端加速且保持生成质量几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型随规模扩大生成质量提升，但推理成本也随之增加。现有加速方法的最佳策略高度依赖于具体实例（模型、硬件、推理配置的组合），不同模型在架构、数值敏感性和注意力集中模式上差异巨大，推理设置（时空分辨率、视频时长）和硬件平台（内存层次、数值格式、kernel吞吐）也各不相同，导致手动调优成本极高。因此需要一种能自动为特定部署目标定制最优加速方案的框架。

Method: 提出Sol Video Inference Engine，一个无需训练的智能体驱动全栈加速框架。包含五个通用技术：缓存（cache）、稀疏注意力（sparse attention）、token剪枝（token pruning）、量化（quantization）和算子融合（kernel fusion）。对于给定的模型、硬件平台和服务配置，并行技能智能体（skill agents）优化每种技术的实现，一个智能体集成器（agent integrator）将它们组合成全局加速栈，最后由人工验证器（human validator）对生成质量提供反馈。该工作流在三个不同规模和架构的视频模型上实例化。

Result: 在64B Cosmos3-Super、22B LTX-2.3和2B SANA-Video三个视频模型上，全栈加速方案在仅需少量人工干预的情况下，实现了2倍以上的端到端加速，同时VBench评测质量几乎无损（near-lossless）。

Conclusion: Sol Video Inference Engine 证明了智能体驱动的自动化加速框架能够有效应对视频扩散模型推理加速中实例特定的优化挑战，在显著提升推理速度的同时保持生成质量，为视频生成模型的高效部署提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视频扩散模型的推理加速框架，涉及缓存、稀疏注意力、token剪枝、量化和算子融合等多项效率优化技术，属于[生成模型加速与高效化]这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference for video generation, a high-weight priority, with a practical agent-driven acceleration framework. Signals: efficient inference, video generation, diffusion model acceleration, agent-driven optimization, training-free acceleration

Abstract: Modern video diffusion models achieve higher generation quality through scaling, but this also increases inference cost. Although many acceleration methods have been proposed, a central challenge is that the most effective acceleration strategy is highly instance-specific: a recipe that works well for one combination of model, hardware, and inference configuration often does not transfer to another. Different models vary in architecture, numerical sensitivity, and attention concentration patterns. Inference settings differ in spatial and temporal resolution and video duration, while hardware platforms differ in memory hierarchy, supported numerical formats, and kernel throughput. These factors create a large tuning space, making manual performance engineering costly. We present Sol Video Inference Engine, an agentic, native, training-free acceleration framework for video diffusion models. It organizes five broadly applicable techniques, cache, sparse attention, token pruning, quantization, and kernel fusion, into an agentic acceleration stack for instance-specific optimization. For a concrete deployment target defined by a model, hardware platform, and serving configuration, parallel skill agents optimize the implementation of each technique, an agent integrator composes them into a global acceleration stack, and a human validator provides feedback on generation quality. We instantiate this workflow on three video models with different sizes and architectures: 64B Cosmos3-Super, 22B LTX-2.3, and 2B SANA-Video. With little human effort, the full stack achieves more than 2x end-to-end acceleration while maintaining near-lossless VBench quality, demonstrating the effectiveness of the agent framework for video diffusion acceleration.

</details>

TL;DR: ARIA 提出一种自适应区域级训练资源分配框架，用于条件扩散模型的蒸馏。通过在线估计教师-学生差异，ARIA 将训练集中在条件空间中差异最大的区域，从而提升蒸馏效果，尤其在未见和欠表示条件下表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散模型的蒸馏面临一个关键瓶颈：教师-学生之间的预测噪声高度依赖于条件信号，因此有效蒸馏需要探索大规模条件空间。然而，配对图像-条件数据有限，为每个条件生成合成图像计算成本高昂。现有方法通过切换条件来扩展条件空间，但未解决如何最优分配训练资源的问题。ARIA 旨在自适应地分配训练努力，聚焦于教师-学生差异最大的区域。

Method: ARIA 将条件空间划分为粗粒度区域，并为每个区域维护在线估计的教师-学生差异（discrepancy）。训练过程中，根据区域级差异动态调整采样概率，使训练资源更多地分配到差异大（即对齐不足）的区域。该方法保持原始蒸馏目标不变，仅改变训练数据的采样分布。作者还提供了理论分析，证明在有限方差和漂移假设下，该跟踪机制能有效追踪训练过程中不断变化的差异。

Result: 实验表明，ARIA 在多种架构和设置下均优于基线方法 RC（Random Condition），在未见条件和欠表示条件上的提升最为显著。该方法有效提升了蒸馏后学生模型在条件空间中的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ARIA 通过自适应区域级训练资源分配，有效解决了条件扩散模型蒸馏中的训练效率问题，为大规模条件空间下的知识蒸馏提供了一种实用且理论支持的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出条件扩散模型的蒸馏框架ARIA，自适应分配训练资源以提升蒸馏效率，属于扩散模型加速与高效化的核心范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation)。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference and model acceleration for conditional diffusion models, a high-priority subtopic with strong alignment to user's focus on generation efficiency. Signals: efficient inference, diffusion model distillation, adaptive training allocation, conditional generation acceleration, model efficiency

Abstract: Distilling conditional diffusion models aims to transfer the behavior of a large teacher to a smaller student while preserving alignment across conditioning inputs. Unlike recognition tasks, knowledge distillation in conditional diffusion often struggles to transfer knowledge beyond the training distribution, since the predicted noise strongly depends on the conditioning signal. As a result, effective distillation requires exploring a large conditioning space. In practical settings, this creates a major bottleneck. Paired image-condition data may be limited, and generating synthetic images for every available condition is often computationally infeasible, while the pool of conditions, such as text prompts, can be extremely large. Recent work addresses this issue by switching conditions during training, exposing the student to a broader conditioning space without changing the distillation objective. Yet this raises a complementary question: once a large conditioning corpus is available, how should the training effort be allocated? In this work, we introduce ARIA, a framework that adaptively allocates training effort across coarse regions of the conditioning space. By maintaining online estimates of teacher-student discrepancy at the region level, ARIA focuses updates where misalignment persists while preserving the original distillation objective. Empirically, ARIA improves over RC across most architectures and settings, with the clearest gains observed in unseen and underrepresented regimes. We also provide a theoretical analysis showing that the proposed tracking mechanism follows the evolving discrepancy during training under bounded variance and drift assumptions.

</details>


### [41] [The Geometry Behind Diffusion and Flow Matching: Gradient Flows and Geodesics in Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24157)
*Yian Yao, Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

TL;DR: 本文从 Wasserstein 空间的黎曼几何视角，严格证明了扩散模型对应自由能的梯度流（Fokker-Planck 方程/JKO 格式），而 Flow Matching 对应 Wasserstein 测地线（Benamou-Brenier 公式），两者在同一流形上沿不同路径到达相同端点，为两类生成模型提供了统一的几何理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和 Flow Matching 是当前最主流的两种生成范式，但两者之间的关系缺乏严格的数学统一理解。作者希望从 Wasserstein 空间的几何结构出发，揭示这两种方法的内在联系，为理解生成模型的采样效率差异提供理论依据。

Method: 利用 Otto 提出的 Wasserstein 空间黎曼流形框架，将扩散模型的去噪过程形式化为自由能 KL(ρ||π) 的梯度流（对应 Fokker-Planck 方程及其 JKO 隐式欧拉离散化），将 Flow Matching 的生成过程形式化为 Wasserstein 测地线（对应 Benamou-Brenier 公式的最小作用量路径）。通过将两者置于同一流形上，比较初始值问题（梯度流）与边值问题（测地线）的差异。

Result: 证明了 DDPM、DDIM、NCSN/SMLD、Energy Matching 等扩散变体均可统一为 JKO 格式的不同实现；Flow Matching 沿 Wasserstein 测地线进行确定性 ODE 采样，路径更直，因此所需采样步数远少于扩散模型。两类模型最终到达相同的目标分布，但路径不同。

Conclusion: 扩散模型和 Flow Matching 并非互斥的理论体系，而是 Wasserstein 流形上同一生成问题的两种变分原理——扩散是自由能梯度流（初值问题），Flow Matching 是 Wasserstein 测地线（边值问题）。这一统一视角为设计更高效的生成模型提供了理论指导。

Classification reason: 该论文从几何角度统一了扩散模型（梯度流）与 Flow Matching（测地线），揭示了 Flow Matching 沿 Wasserstein 测地线进行确定性 ODE 采样从而大幅减少采样步数的原理，属于生成模型加速与高效化的理论分析，因此归入 [CANONICAL] efficient_generation。

Importance reason: Provides a rigorous geometric unification of diffusion and flow matching, directly relevant to your high-priority continuous language/multimodal models and flow matching subtopics. Signals: Unifies diffusion and flow matching via Wasserstein geometry, directly supports flow matching and continuous language model priorities, strong theoretical foundation for efficient inference, no negative keywords, high relevance to multimodal generation direction

Abstract: The space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d$) of probability measures with finite second moment carries a natural geometry: the quadratic Wasserstein distance W_2 makes it a complete metric space and, following Otto, a (formal) Riemannian manifold whose geodesics are the optimal-transport interpolations. On this manifold, the gradient flow of the free energy F(rho) = KL(rho || \pi) is exactly the Fokker-Planck equation, and its implicit-Euler discretization is the JKO scheme. This is the geometry underlying diffusion models: the forward process descends the free energy, and each denoising step realizes one JKO step, which recovers DDPM, DDIM, NCSN/SMLD, and Energy Matching; this is one scheme, not separate theories. The same manifold supports a second variational principle. Its geodesics - the minimum-action curves of the Benamou-Brenier formula - are precisely the optimal-transport paths that Flow Matching learns. Fixing both endpoints and following the geodesic, generation becomes a deterministic ODE along a straight line, hence far fewer sampling steps. Placing both families of models on one manifold makes their relationship exact: diffusion follows a free-energy gradient flow, an initial-value problem; optimal-transport Flow Matching follows a Wasserstein geodesic, a boundary-value problem. The two reach the same endpoints along different paths.

</details>

*Feiyang Fu, Hehe Fan*

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

TL;DR: 本文提出时间重参数化累积强度外推(TR-CIE)采样器，通过调度时间重参数化和累积强度外推更新规则，在有限函数评估次数下提升离散流匹配(DFM)的采样质量，无需额外模型评估开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流匹配(DFM)为离散状态空间上的生成建模提供了理论框架，但现有采样方法（如τ-leaping）在有限函数评估次数(NFE)下的高效采样研究不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出在受限NFE下提升采样质量的采样器。

Method: TR-CIE包含两个组件：(1) 基于噪声调度的时域重参数化，重新缩放时间网格，吸收调度相关的增长项，缓解终端采样阶段的刚性；(2) 累积强度外推更新规则，通过复用上一步缓存的模型输出作为历史项，在非均匀时间网格上改进逐步骤累积强度的近似。每步仅需一次NFE，无额外模型评估开销。

Result: 在合成任务、文本生成和文生图基准上的大量实验表明，TR-CIE在有限NFE下显著提升了采样质量，优于标准τ-leaping采样器。

Conclusion: TR-CIE是一种高效且无需额外模型评估的DFM采样器，通过时间重参数化和累积强度外推有效改善了受限NFE下的采样质量，理论分析也证明了累积强度的局部近似误差有界并建立了收敛性结果。

Classification reason: 该论文提出TR-CIE采样器，旨在有限函数评估(NFE)下提升离散流匹配(DFM)的采样质量，属于生成模型采样加速与高效化的范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型加速与高效化" (efficient_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes a practical sampler for discrete flow matching, relevant to efficient generation but not directly aligned with top priority continuous multimodal or video generation subtopics. Signals: discrete flow matching, efficient sampling, time reparameterization, cumulative intensity extrapolation, text-to-image generation

Abstract: Discrete flow matching (DFM) provides a principled framework for generative modeling on discrete state spaces via continuous-time Markov chain dynamics. In practice, sampling for DFM commonly employs discretizations such as $\tau$-leaping, yet efficient sampling methods under a limited number of function evaluations (NFE) remain less studied. To address this gap, we propose the Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation (TR-CIE) sampler, which aims to improve sampling quality when function evaluations are restricted. TR-CIE consists of two components. First, a schedule-based time reparameterization rescales the time grid according to the noise schedule. Under standard factorized DFM rate parameterizations, this transformation of variables absorbs the schedule-dependent growth term and mitigates stiffness near the terminal sampling stage. Second, we introduce a cumulative-intensity extrapolation updating rule. By reusing cached model outputs from the previous step as a history term, this improves the approximation of stepwise cumulative intensities on the resulting non-uniform time grid. We provide a theoretical analysis that bounds the local approximation error of cumulative intensities and establishes convergence results. The resulting sampler requires one NFE per step and introduces no additional model evaluations compared to the standard $\tau$-leaping sampler. Extensive experiments on synthetic tasks, text generation, and text-to-image benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves sampling quality under limited NFE.

</details>

Conclusion: 循环去噪既是一种受物理启发的生成景观探测工具，也是一种实用的记忆审计方法，对隐私保护、版权合规和模型指纹识别具有重要意义。

Importance reason: Novel attack on diffusion models reveals memorization, but is tangential to user's focus on continuous models and autoregressive generation. Signals: diffusion model memorization, cyclic denoising, extraction attack, ultrastable attractors, privacy implications

Abstract: We introduce cyclic denoising -- repeated forward and reverse diffusion at controlled noise amplitudes -- as an extraction attack for image diffusion models. Inspired by random organization in disordered solids, cyclic denoising exposes regions of the learned distribution that are largely inaccessible to standard sampling. The dynamics drive samples toward attractors with a broad stability spectrum. The deepest attractors are ultrastable: they regenerate after near-total corruption and persist through thousands of noising-denoising cycles. Many of these attractors correspond to memorized training images, including stock photographs, brand watermarks, and web-crawl artifacts. The attack requires only sampler-level control, with no gradients, weight inspection, prompts, captions, or prior knowledge of the training data. Unlike generate-and-filter attacks, which rely on large-scale prompted generation and post-hoc similarity or membership-inference filtering, our main protocol is fully unconditioned. We demonstrate the phenomenon in Stable Diffusion v1.4 and in a pixel-space DDPM, showing consistent behavior across latent- and pixel-space diffusion models. Across noise amplitudes, we observe a yielding-like transition: low-amplitude cycling produces trivial absorbing fixed points or limit cycles, while larger amplitudes induce rearrangements, basin hopping, and long-lived trapping in structured memorized attractor basins. We also observe hierarchical partial absorption, prompt-stabilized basins, and cross-initial-condition universality of the recovered attractor set. Our results therefore show that cyclic denoising is both a physics-inspired probe of generative landscapes and a practical tool for memorization auditing, with implications for privacy, copyright compliance, and model fingerprinting.

</details>

Importance reason: Flow matching for reward-conditioned action distribution in driving planning is novel but peripheral to your core continuous multimodal and video generation priorities. Signals: flow-matching decoder, reward-conditioned action distribution, multimodal driving planning, NAVSIM SOTA, controllable sampling via reward guidance

Abstract: Multimodal driving planning faces a long-standing tension between two paradigms: scoring-based methods benefit from dense reward supervision but are confined to a fixed action vocabulary, while anchor-based methods generate proposals dynamically yet suffer from sparse supervision constrained to a single ground-truth trajectory. In this work, we propose FlowR2A, which resolves this tension by reframing simulation-based rewards from discriminative targets into generative conditions. By learning the reward-conditioned action distribution from dense trajectory-reward pairs with a flow-matching decoder, FlowR2A unifies the dense supervision of scoring-based methods with the proposal generation of anchor-based methods in a single generative model, forcing the model to internalize the correlation between an action and its outcomes in safety, progress, comfort, and rule compliance. To balance hard safety constraints against soft progress objectives, we introduce fine-grained per-timestep reward conditioning and reward noise augmentation. The generative formulation naturally supports controllable test-time sampling via reward guidance and anchored sampling, producing high-quality proposals. FlowR2A achieves state-of-the-art results on the NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks, with multimodal proposals of substantially higher quality than prior methods.

</details>

Signals: RL for diffusion generative LLMs, disaggregated training, throughput improvement, diffusion parallelism, trainer-assisted generation

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a dominant post-training paradigm, driving the emergence of high-performance RL systems such as veRL for autoregressive large language models (LLMs). In parallel, diffusion-oriented RL algorithms, e.g., DanceGRPO and FlowGRPO, have rapidly expanded the scope of RL from language reasoning to diffusion-based visual and flow-based generation. However, efficient RL systems for diffusion generative LLMs remain underexplored. Existing implementations, e.g., veRL-Omni, still rely on colocated execution, which simplifies synchronization but couples rollout and training resources, limits heterogeneous deployment, and constrains independent scaling. To this end, we introduce DigenRL, a disaggregated RL framework for diffusion-based generative LLMs that supports flexible resource allocation, accommodates heterogeneous GPUs, and facilitates efficient task scheduling. To maximally reduce the execution bubbles in the disaggregated architecture, we propose: 1) a generation-axis pipeline (GAP) and time-step parallelism (TSP) in the diffusion architecture to enable finer-grained pipelining between rollout and training; 2) an elastic trainer-assisted generation (TAG) approach to enable the trainer GPU resources to dynamically assist in executing rollout generations; and 3) a tightly one-step constrained asynchronous strategy to further utilize the tail bubble in the pipeline. Extensive experiments are conducted on three hardware testbeds with 16-32 GPUs using HunyuanVideo-13B, Wan2.1-14B, FLUX.1-12B, and QwenImage-20B generative models. Experimental results show that DigenRL achieves 1.56-2.10x throughput improvements over state-of-the-art diffusion RL systems, veRL-Omni and GenRL.

</details>

*Jian Xu, Delu Zeng, John Paisley, Qibin Zhao*

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文从后验传输（posterior-transport）视角系统分析了基于flow matching的逆问题求解器（FlowDPS、FLOWER、PnP-Flow等）。理论证明：对于确定性flow先验，贝叶斯条件化完全由源分布重加权实现，而非轨迹校正；现有轨迹引导求解器可视为最小动能校正场的不同近似。2D实验表明源重加权精确匹配真实后验，而轨迹引导方法误差大200-800倍且会坍缩后验模式。基于分析提出了一种廉价且有原则的速度校正求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于flow matching先验的逆问题求解器（如FlowDPS、FLOWER、PnP-Flow）通过添加测量引导项来修正概率流ODE轨迹，取得了良好的经验结果，但这些逐步校正项究竟近似什么、采样结果与真实后验p(x|y)的差距有多大，缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 1) 理论推导：证明对于确定性flow先验，贝叶斯条件化等价于对源分布进行重加权（reweighting），然后通过未修改的速度场前向传播即可得到精确后验样本。2) 将现有轨迹引导求解器统一解释为最小动能校正场的不同近似：FlowDPS为零阶近似、FLOWER为高斯近似、PnP-Flow为近端近似，并给出了Wasserstein距离下的后验偏差界。3) 基于理论分析提出了一种新的速度校正求解器。

Result: 在2D可控实验中，源重加权方法在所有指标上精确匹配真实后验（达到蒙特卡洛精度下限），而轨迹引导方法误差大200-800倍且会坍缩后验模式，且该问题与引导强度无关。在AFHQ、CelebA两个域内先验和两个域外设置上，提出的速度校正求解器具有竞争力，且能产生多样化的后验样本，其不确定性估计与重建误差相关。

Conclusion: 本文从后验传输视角统一了基于flow的逆问题求解器理论，揭示了现有轨迹引导方法的根本局限——它们近似的是最小动能校正场，而非真实后验。源重加权方法理论上更优但计算上更具挑战性。提出的速度校正求解器在实用性和理论保证之间取得了良好平衡。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于flow matching先验的逆问题求解器（如FlowDPS、FLOWER、PnP-Flow），核心关注生成模型在逆问题中的后验采样与guidance校正，属于生成模型的对齐/guidance/可控性范畴，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）。

Importance reason: Provides a principled theoretical framework for flow-based inverse solvers, but the application domain (imaging inverse problems) is not a user priority. Signals: flow matching theory, posterior-transport view, training-free inverse solvers, source reweighting vs trajectory guidance, 2D posterior validation

Abstract: A growing family of training-free solvers -- FlowDPS, FLOWER, PnP-Flow and their diffusion ancestors (DPS, DAPS) -- repurpose a pretrained flow-matching prior to solve imaging inverse problems by adding a measurement-guidance term to the deterministic probability-flow ODE. Despite strong empirical results, what these per-step corrections actually approximate -- and how far the resulting samples are from the true posterior $p(x\mid y)$ -- has not been characterized. We give a posterior-transport account of flow-based inverse problem solving. Our starting point is a simple but consequential fact: for a \emph{deterministic} flow prior, Bayesian conditioning is realized entirely by a \emph{reweighting of the source distribution}, not by a drift correction; pushing the reweighted source through the \emph{unmodified} velocity field yields exact posterior samples. From this we show that trajectory-guidance solvers can be read as the minimum-kinetic-energy \emph{correction} field needed to morph the unconditional source into the posterior, and that FlowDPS / FLOWER / PnP-Flow correspond to distinct zeroth-order / Gaussian / proximal approximations of this single object; we bound the resulting posterior bias in Wasserstein distance. A controlled $2$D study with a closed-form posterior confirms the theory decisively: source reweighting matches the true posterior to the Monte-Carlo floor on every metric, whereas trajectory guidance incurs $200$--$800\times$ larger error and collapses posterior modes, \emph{regardless of guidance strength}. Guided by the analysis we propose a cheap, principled velocity-correction solver that is competitive across two in-domain priors (AFHQ, CelebA) and two out-of-distribution settings while, unlike point-estimate source-space optimizers, producing diverse posterior samples with uncertainty that correlates with reconstruction error.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在11种代表性T2I越狱方法上（包括有代码和无代码论文），在原始设置下准确复现了先前结果，平均误差2.1%，中位数误差0%。

Conclusion: PixJail可作为未来T2I越狱复现和评测的统一基础框架，显著减少人工工作量，推动该领域更可靠和可比较的评测。

Importance reason: Focuses on T2I jailbreak evaluation reproducibility, not on core generative model architecture or efficiency. Signals: T2I jailbreak evaluation, paper-to-pipeline agent, reproducibility, safety alignment, not core generation method

Abstract: As Text-to-Image (T2I) jailbreak techniques evolve rapidly, existing benchmarks and reproduction workflows often struggle to keep pace. More importantly, T2I jailbreak evaluation is not a single prompt-level test, but a pipeline-level problem shaped by multiple stages, including prompt transformation, image generation, safety filtering, and multimodal judging. This makes results across papers difficult to reliably reproduce and fairly compare. To bridge this gap, we propose PixJail, a self-evolving paper-to-pipeline agent framework for reproducible T2I jailbreak evaluation. Given a T2I jailbreak paper and optional reference code, PixJail rapidly constructs a paper-specific attack module and a runnable evaluation pipeline under a unified contract, while faithfully reproducing the original experimental results. PixJail further maintains a memory bank that stores paper digests, attack evolution patterns, reusable templates, failure cases, and versioned artifacts, enabling future reproduction efforts to reuse prior experience. We reproduce eleven representative T2I jailbreak methods, including both code-available and code-unavailable papers. Under their original settings, our framework accurately recovers prior results with minimal error (2.1\% average, 0\% median). We hope that PixJail can serve as a unified foundation for future T2I jailbreak reproduction and evaluation, significantly reducing manual effort.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波卫星图像对监测热带气旋降水和强度至关重要，但重访时间长，可能错过快速风暴演化阶段。现有插值方法难以处理来自不同仪器的微波数据的高度异质性（多源、地理错位、时间不规则、仪器特性各异），亟需一种能统一处理这些挑战的生成模型。

Method: 提出 MotifGen 生成模型，采用自监督训练策略：随机掩码一个源（如某时刻的微波图像），利用其他源（其他时刻的微波和红外图像）进行重建。模型能够处理多源地理空间数据在样本间变化、时间间隔不规则、地理错位以及不同仪器特性差异的问题。结合微波和红外数据进行多源融合生成。

Result: 自监督训练相比监督训练显著降低了连续排名概率分数（CRPS）。结合红外与微波数据相比仅用微波数据有进一步提升。生成模型的集合均值与确定性模型相当，而生成的功率谱显著更接近真实观测。

Conclusion: MotifGen 是首个能够插值热带气旋微波图像并融合多个微波仪器和红外观测的生成模型，展示了多源异构地理空间数据生成建模的可行性，为气象遥感领域的时空插值提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出首个面向多源异构卫星图像（微波+红外）的时空插值生成模型，属于多模态生成方向。虽然论文聚焦于遥感/气象应用，但其核心贡献是生成模型在异构多源数据上的应用与评测（CRPS、功率谱等），与[CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）中涵盖的生成模型评测方法最为匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on satellite image interpolation for tropical cyclones, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Multi-source generative model, Spatiotemporal interpolation, Self-supervised masking, Tropical cyclone application, Not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Microwave satellite imagery plays a crucial role in monitoring tropical cyclone precipitation and intensity worldwide, but suffers from long revisit times, potentially missing rapid storm evolution phases. While this raises the need for an interpolation method, it is made challenging by the high level of heterogeneity of microwave data coming from different instruments. In this work, we introduce the first generative model that can be applied to multiple geospatial sources that change across samples, occur at irregular time intervals, are misaligned geographically, and come from instruments with varying characteristics. We apply this model to the case of spatio-temporal interpolation of tropical cyclone microwave images from other microwave and infrared instruments. We train using a self-supervised task in which a random source is masked and reconstructed, and show that it leads to a significant decrease in Continuous Ranked Probability Score over supervised training. We show a further improvement by combining infrared and microwave data compared to microwave only. Using these improvements, the generative model produces an ensemble mean on par with that of a deterministic model, while generating a power spectrum significantly closer to that of true observations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first generative model that interpolates microwave images of cyclones by combining multiple microwave instruments and infrared observations at irregular time intervals.

</details>

Conclusion: SignNet-1M 通过大规模数据增强有效提升了手语模型在真实世界分布偏移下的鲁棒性，为手语识别与翻译研究提供了重要的数据资源和评测基准。

Classification reason: 该论文发布了大规模多语言手语视频数据集 SignNet-1M，并提供了下游任务（翻译、识别）的统一评测基准。这属于生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）范畴，该 subtopic 在 multimodal_generation 方向下是 [CANONICAL] 的，且也在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下存在。论文核心贡献是数据集与基准，因此映射到此 subtopic。

Abstract: Sign language models are typically trained on datasets captured under constrained conditions, with limited viewpoint, background, and signer-identity diversity, leading to poor robustness under real-world distribution shifts. We introduce SignNet-1M, a large-scale augmented dataset spanning ASL, CSL, and German Sign Language (DGS). SignNet-1M synthesizes realistic variations along three axes: (i) novel-view rendering (rotation and zoom) via 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), (ii) scene/identity editing via diffusion models for background replacement and signer substitution while preserving sign motion and linguistic content, and (iii) post-rendering augmentations that emulate capture and compression artifacts (e.g., pose/temporal perturbations and video-level corruptions) to better match in-the-wild recordings. Beyond data release, we provide a unified benchmark suite across downstream tasks (e.g., translation and recognition) and ablations that isolate each augmentation component. Experiments across backbones show that training with SignNet-1M consistently improves generalization under cross-view, cross-background, cross-identity, and post-rendering shifts, while maintaining strong in-distribution performance. The dataset, full augmentation pipeline, and benchmark are available at .

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: MATCH作为首个基于Flow Matching的多视角异常检测方法，在性能和效率上均表现出色，证明了Flow Matching在工业异常检测领域的潜力。

Signals: Flow matching for anomaly detection, Multi-view industrial inspection, Real-time consumer hardware, SOTA on Real-IAD and MANTA-Tiny, ODE likelihood estimation

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in industrial objects is an important topic for increasing production efficiency. More complex objects often require the analysis of several view points, which has led to the field of multi-view anomaly detection. We present MATCH, the first multi-view anomaly detection method based on Flow Matching (FM). With the ODE formulation of Flow Matching, we can estimate likelihoods and thereby derive an anomaly score to detect anomalies in multi-view image data at object, image, and pixel-level. The architectural flexibility of FM models allows us to efficiently transform features of different spatial sizes to the normal distribution. We evaluate thoroughly on the already established Real-IAD data set and are also the first to provide a comprehensive evaluation of popular anomaly detection methods for the MANTA-Tiny data set. MATCH achieves state-of-the-art performance in both anomaly detection and segmentation, all while running on consumer-level hardware. By omitting the costly divergence term needed for likelihood estimation, we ensure that MATCH is usable in real-time production scenarios. Lastly, several ablation studies are conducted to validate the methodological choices.

</details>


### [51] [Are Text-to-Image Models Inductivist Turkeys? A Counterfactual Benchmark for Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24548)
*Jiayi Lei, Yuandong Pu, Xingyu Han, Rongpeng Zhu, Jing Xu, Jinyao Wang, Zijian Zhou, Bin Fu, Yuewen Cao, Yihao Liu, Yongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (76.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CF-World 基准，通过反事实场景（事实生成、显式反事实生成、隐式反事实生成）系统评测文生图模型的因果推理能力。实验发现所有模型在反事实设置下性能急剧下降，表明当前 T2I 模型依赖视觉共现模式而非真正的因果理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文生图模型在生成逼真图像方面取得了显著进展，但其成功是否反映了真正的因果理解，还是仅仅依赖于训练数据中的视觉-文本相关性模式匹配，这一问题尚不明确。受 Russell 的归纳火鸡悖论启发，作者希望系统性地评测 T2I 模型的反事实因果推理能力。

Method: 提出 CF-World 基准，包含三个递进层次：事实生成（基于常识）、显式反事实生成（直接视觉指令）、隐式反事实生成（需从改变规则中因果推导）。引入两个新指标：Prior Resistance Rate (PRR) 衡量模型克服先验的能力，Reasoning Retention Rate (RRR) 评估模型在无显式视觉线索时维持推理的能力。使用 VLM 作为评估器 (CF-Eval)。

Result: 所有模型从事实到反事实设置均表现出急剧性能下降。进一步分析表明，当前 T2I 模型将世界知识与视觉外观编码为紧密耦合的模式，严重依赖训练数据中频繁出现的视觉共现，因此在反事实任务中退回到熟悉的常识先验。

Conclusion: 当前文生图模型缺乏真正的因果推理能力，其成功主要源于对视觉-文本相关性的模式匹配。CF-World 基准为评估和推动 T2I 模型的因果推理能力提供了有效工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Counterfactual-World (CF-World) 基准，用于评测文生图模型的反事实因果推理能力，属于生成模型评测基准，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。同时该工作也涉及多模态理解（使用 VLM 评估），因此匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Probes causal reasoning limits of T2I models, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: counterfactual benchmark, causal reasoning evaluation, T2I model limitations, Prior Resistance Rate, Reasoning Retention Rate

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation models have achieved remarkable progress in producing visually realistic images from natural language prompts. Yet it remains unclear whether their success reflects genuine causal understanding or sophisticated pattern matching over visual-textual correlations. Inspired by Russell's inductivist turkey, we introduce Counterfactual-World (CF-World), a counterfactual benchmark designed to investigate whether text-to-image models can generate images under rules that systematically contradict real-world priors. CF-World organizes each scenario into three progressive levels: factual generation under ordinary world knowledge, explicit counterfactual generation with direct visual instructions, and implicit counterfactual generation requiring causal deduction from altered rules. We evaluate both open-source and closed-source T2I models using a Vision Language Model (VLM)-based evaluator (CF-Eval). Furthermore, we introduce two metrics: Prior Resistance Rate (PRR), which measures a model's ability to overcome entrenched real-world priors, and Reasoning Retention Rate (RRR), which assesses whether models can maintain reasoning-dependent counterfactual generation without explicit visual cues. Experiments show that all models exhibit sharp degradation from factual to counterfactual settings. Further analyses suggest that these failures arise because current T2I models encode world knowledge and visual appearances as tightly coupled patterns. Consequently, their heavy reliance on frequent visual co-occurrences within the training data forces them to default to familiar commonsense priors when tasked with rendering counterfactual worlds.

</details>

*Chenrui Fan, Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoT2V-Bench 是一个基于 3D 重建的诊断性基准，用于评测相机提示的文生视频模型生成的视频片段是否支持刚体 3D 场景重建。它通过估计相机参数、拟合可变形 3DGS、构建静态 MedianGS 代理并计算连续重建指标，揭示了可见运动、静态渲染误差、光流一致性和灵活vs静态拟合行为之间的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文生视频模型虽然能生成视觉上合理的相机运动视频，但这些视频是否真正对应一个一致的静态 3D 场景尚不清楚。缺乏一个系统性的基准来评测生成视频的 3D 几何一致性。

Method: 提出 GeoT2V-Bench 流水线：使用 VGGT 风格几何估计预测每帧相机内参和位姿，拟合 DeformableGS（可变形 3DGS），通过时序中值聚合导出静态 MedianGS 代理，沿估计相机路径渲染该代理。报告连续重建指标：表观图像运动、估计轨迹行为、MedianGS 静态渲染误差、静态渲染光流一致性、灵活vs静态拟合差距。

Result: 在 12 个开源模型配置和 80 个静态场景提示上进行了 3,840 次重建实验，发现可见运动、静态渲染误差、光流一致性和灵活vs静态行为往往不一致，表明 GeoT2V-Bench 能够捕获生成视频作为全局静态场景采集测试时出现的互补失败模式。

Conclusion: GeoT2V-Bench 提供了一个有效的诊断工具来评测文生视频模型的 3D 一致性，揭示了现有模型在生成几何一致的静态场景视频方面的不足，为未来改进提供了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 GeoT2V-Bench，一个用于评测文生视频模型 3D 一致性的重建基准，属于生成模型评测范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for 3D consistency in T2V, but not aligned with user's priority subtopics. Signals: 3D reconstruction benchmark, text-to-video evaluation, camera-prompted generation, continuous reconstruction profile, not autoregressive or diffusion video generation

Abstract: Camera-prompted text-to-video (T2V) models are increasingly used to synthesize virtual camera captures, such as orbiting objects or moving through static scenes. For these outputs, visual plausibility is insufficient: the generated frames should also provide coherent multi-view evidence for a single static 3D scene. We introduce GeoT2V-Bench, a reconstruction-based diagnostic benchmark for evaluating whether camera-prompted T2V clips can support explicit rigid 3D reconstruction. Our pipeline estimates per-frame camera intrinsics and poses with VGGT-style geometry estimation, fits DeformableGS, derives a static MedianGS proxy by temporal-median aggregation, and renders this proxy along the estimated camera path. Instead of producing a pass/fail label or a single scalar score, GeoT2V-Bench reports a continuous reconstruction profile covering apparent image motion, estimated trajectory behavior, MedianGS static rendering error, static-render flow agreement, and the gap between flexible and static fits. On a fair-format four-seed evaluation with 3,840 completed reconstructions from 12 open-weight model configurations and 80 GeCo-Eval static-scene prompts, we find that visible motion, static rendering error, flow agreement, and flexible-vs-static behavior often disagree. GeoT2V-Bench therefore captures complementary failure modes that emerge when generated videos are tested as global static-scene acquisitions.

</details>


### [53] [DiffusionBench: On Holistic Evaluation of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24888)
*Xingjian Leng, Jaskirat Singh, Zhanhao Liang, Ethan Smith, Martin Bell, Aninda Saha, Yuhui Yuan, Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (83.16) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 NanoGen 统一训练框架和 DiffusionBench 综合评测基准，发现 DiT 方法在 ImageNet 类条件生成和文生图任务上的排名相关性极弱（Pearson 相关系数为 -0.377 至 -0.580），表明仅用 ImageNet 评估不足以反映真实进展，建议同时报告两个任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 DiT 研究几乎全部集中在 ImageNet 类条件生成上，仅用 FID 等指标评估，但这种方法排名是否反映文生图等实际任务上的真实进展尚不明确。作者认为需要建立更全面的评测体系。

Method: 提出 NanoGen 统一训练框架，支持 RAE、VAE、pixel-space 和 MeanFlow 等多种扩散方法，仅需修改 12 行配置即可从 ImageNet 切换到 T2I 训练。在该框架下训练了 21 个潜扩散模型，系统比较了 ImageNet 和 T2I 两种设置下的方法排名。

Result: ImageNet 和 T2I 任务上的方法排名无强相关性（Pearson 相关系数 -0.377 至 -0.580），说明在 ImageNet 上提升 FID 的方法在 T2I 上可能没有对应提升。基于此，作者整合两个任务的结果构建了 DiffusionBench 基准。

Conclusion: 仅用 ImageNet 评估 DiT 方法具有局限性，建议采用 DiffusionBench 同时报告 ImageNet 和 T2I 结果，以更全面地反映生成建模的真实进展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 DiffusionBench，一个面向 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 的综合性评测基准，涵盖 ImageNet 类条件生成和文生图 (T2I) 两种任务，属于生成模型评测范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集" (generation_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Challenges single-benchmark evaluation in diffusion transformers, directly relevant to user's focus on multimodal generation and model efficiency. Signals: DiT evaluation gap, ImageNet vs T2I ranking correlation, unified training framework, holistic benchmark, efficient training for T2I

Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT) research on image generation has converged to a single evaluation setup: class-conditional generation on ImageNet. While methods improve the FID and related metrics, it is increasingly unclear whether they reflect real progress in generative modeling. The natural alternative, i.e., text-to-image (T2I) generation, is perceived as too costly or inconvenient to train and evaluate and is often skipped. We argue that this perception no longer holds. We introduce NanoGen, a unified DiT training and evaluation framework. NanoGen matches state-of-the-art DiT baselines on ImageNet and, with 12 lines of configuration change, also trains competitive text-to-image models. It currently supports RAE, VAE, pixel-space, and MeanFlow diffusion methods under both ImageNet and T2I setups. Under NanoGen, training T2I requires comparable compute to ImageNet. After training 21 latent diffusion models with NanoGen, we observe that method ranking shows no strong correlation between ImageNet and T2I generation: Pearson correlation is between -0.377 and -0.580 across three metrics. This suggests that a method which improves class-conditional ImageNet FID may show no corresponding improvement on T2I, clearly indicating the necessity of evaluating DiTs on both tasks. To this end, we summarize ImageNet and text-to-image results, which yields DiffusionBench, a holistic benchmark for DiT research. We recommend reporting DiffusionBench in place of ImageNet alone: methods that improve DiffusionBench are more likely to reflect broader progress.

</details>

### [54] [NeuroSonic: Conditional Flow Matching for EEG-to-Speech Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24087)
*Wenhao Gao, Yifan Wang, Yijia Ma, Carl Yang, Wen Li, Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 音频与多模态联合生成

Importance: 值得关注 (76.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: NeuroSonic 提出基于条件流匹配（conditional flow matching）的 EEG 到语音重建框架，通过学习确定性概率流速度场将噪声声学状态向干净语音传输，在 CineBrain 和 EAV 基准上显著优于 GAN、扩散和均值流基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号弱、空间弥散且高度可变，而语音具有强谐波和时序结构，两者之间的不匹配使得波形回归不稳定，随机多步生成对伪影依赖和受试者变异性敏感。现有方法（GAN、扩散、均值流）难以稳定重建高质量语音。

Method: 提出 NeuroSonic 框架，采用条件流匹配学习确定性概率流速度场，将噪声声学状态向干净语音传输。EEG 和音频嵌入到共享 token 空间，由时间条件门控 Transformer 参数化传输常微分方程（ODE），避免迭代随机采样。

Result: 在 CineBrain 和 EAV 基准上进行跨受试者评估，NeuroSonic 在分布真实性、频谱保真度和感知质量上均优于 GAN、扩散和均值流基线，整体感知质量提升高达 26.3%，在伪影密集段表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 确定性条件传输为 EEG 驱动的语音重建提供了一种稳定有效的方案，流匹配框架能够有效处理 EEG 条件变异性，在伪影重段仍保持高质量重建。

Classification reason: 该论文研究EEG到语音的重建，属于跨模态条件音频生成（EEG条件→语音输出），与[CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on EEG-to-speech reconstruction, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks the specified boost keywords. Signals: conditional flow matching, EEG-to-speech, deterministic transport, cross-subject evaluation, perceptual quality improvement

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous speech from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) remains fundamentally challenging. EEG provides a weak, spatially diffuse, and highly variable measurement of distributed cortical activity, whereas speech is organized as a coherent acoustic trajectory with strong harmonic and temporal structure. The resulting mismatch makes waveform regression unstable and causes stochastic multi-step generation to be sensitive to artifact-dependent conditioning and subject variability. We introduce NeuroSonic, a conditional flow-matching framework for EEG-to-speech reconstruction. Instead of predicting waveforms directly or refining them through stochastic denoising, NeuroSonic learns a deterministic probability-flow velocity field that transports a noise-corrupted acoustic state toward clean speech under EEG conditioning. EEG and audio are embedded into a shared token space and processed by a time-conditioned gated Transformer that parameterizes the transport ordinary differential equation. This formulation models trajectory evolution explicitly while avoiding iterative stochastic sampling. We evaluate NeuroSonic on the CineBrain and EAV benchmarks under cross-subject evaluation. Across both datasets, the proposed method improves distributional realism, spectral fidelity, and perceptual quality over representative GAN-, diffusion-, and mean-flow baselines, with up to a 26.3\% gain in overall perceptual quality. The performance gap is most evident in artifact-heavy segments, where conditioning variability is strongest. These findings indicate that deterministic conditional transport provides a stable and effective formulation for EEG-driven speech reconstruction. Code is available at .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的无监督视听语音增强方法虽然通过交叉注意力将视觉特征（如唇部运动）作为条件，但视觉信息的利用是否充分、跨模态对齐是否有效尚不明确。本文旨在显式增强视听模态间的对齐，以提升语音增强性能。

Method: 在扩散模型的训练目标中引入对比性视听对齐损失（contrastive audio-visual loss），使模型在训练过程中显式学习音频与视觉特征之间的对齐关系，同时保持后验采样框架不变。视觉特征通过交叉注意力注入扩散模型，对比损失则强制拉近匹配的视听对、推远不匹配的对。

Result: 在匹配与不匹配的测试数据上，该方法在干扰抑制、信号重建和感知质量指标上均取得一致提升，尤其在低信噪比（SNR）条件下增益最大。

Conclusion: 显式的对比性视听对齐损失能够有效增强扩散模型对视觉信息的利用，提升无监督视听语音增强的性能，且无需改变后验采样框架。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于扩散模型的视听语音增强（AVSE），属于音频与视觉跨模态条件生成，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "音频与多模态联合生成" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on audio-visual speech enhancement, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: diffusion-based AVSE, contrastive audio-visual loss, low SNR gains, cross-modal alignment, eess.SP domain

Abstract: Audio-visual speech enhancement (AVSE) exploits visual cues such as lip movements to recover speech in noisy environments. Recent work introduced diffusion-based unsupervised AVSE, where a speech diffusion model conditioned on visual features via cross-attention is trained and used as a data-driven prior for posterior sampling-based speech enhancement. Despite promising performance over its audio-only counterpart, the impact of explicitly enforcing cross-modal alignment in the fusion remains unclear. In this work, we propose to augment the diffusion training objective with a contrastive audio-visual loss to encourage stronger use of visual information while keeping the posterior sampling framework unchanged. Experiments across matched and mismatched test data show consistent improvements in interference suppression, signal reconstruction, and perceptual quality, with the largest gains at low SNRs. Code is available at cexauce/AV-CA-DiffUSE

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 蒸馏后的模型将生成步数大幅减少，实现了低延迟的实时流式生成，同时保持了音乐的结构连贯性和高保真度，支持用户在生成过程中动态干预和引导音乐轨迹。

Conclusion: 本文重新定义了生成式文本到音乐模型，将其从被动的"提示-等待"系统转变为响应式的交互乐器，为现场人机音乐共创开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出实时交互式音乐生成框架，属于文本到音乐生成（text-to-music），是音频生成的一个子领域。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "音频与多模态联合生成"（audio_av_generation）涵盖音乐生成、TTS、音频生成等，与该论文高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on music generation, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Real-time interactive music generation, streaming consistency distillation, data-free distillation, low-latency autoregressive generation, music-aware consistency objectives

Abstract: Interactive music and live performance relies on real-time human expression, but modern generative music AI remains largely absent from this domain due to its prohibitive inference latency and offline rendering paradigm. To provide pioneer musicians with a novel medium for interactive composition, we should fundamentally change these static models into dynamic, playable instruments. In this paper, we propose a framework that bridges this gap. To achieve the low latency required for live interaction without sacrificing structural coherence, we formulate distillation within a streaming autoregressive latent space. Our approach gets rid of the need for expensive paired audio-latent datasets by utilizing prompt-only inputs to synthesize teacher-guided, chunk-wise trajectories on the fly. Because live instruments require high acoustic fidelity, we introduce music-aware consistency objectives, which combine latent, spectral, and temporal-difference losses, to preserve crucial qualities like timbre, transients, and rhythmic stability during accelerated single-step streaming generation. Implemented via parameter-efficient adaptation, our distillation reduces generation steps to achieve a low real-time factor. Crucially, by operating as a continuous autoregressive stream, the system can seamlessly assimilate dynamic human inputs on the fly, allowing users to instantly steer the musical trajectory without interrupting the audio flow. Ultimately, this work recontextualizes generative text-to-music models not as passive prompt-and-wait systems, but as responsive instruments, opening new frontiers for live human-AI musical co-creation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: ZONOS2 8B 通过扩大规模、数据和改进训练，在 TTS 自然度、韵律和声音克隆方面达到了 SOTA 水平，并作为开源模型发布，推动了 TTS 领域的发展。

Importance reason: Focuses on TTS, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: TTS SOTA, MoE architecture, 6M hours data, open-source weights, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: We present ZONOS2 8B, our latest TTS model, which achieves state-of-the-art naturalness, prosody, and voice cloning fidelity. We improve upon Zonos-v0.1 across scale, data, and training recipe. We scale the model from 1.6B to 8B total parameters (900M active) with a novel mixture-of-experts (MoE) backbone, improving inference latency and throughput. We expand our training corpus from 200K to over 6M hours using a new data processing pipeline, and we simplify our post-training and conditioning recipes to improve naturalness and voice cloning fidelity. We evaluate ZONOS2 8B on quality, speaker similarity, WER, and ZTTS1-Eval, our novel TTS benchmark, where it performs competitively with state-of-the-art systems while maintaining good streaming latency. We release our model weights and example inference code under an Apache 2.0 license on GitHub and Hugging Face.

</details>

### [58] [REALM: A Unified Red-Teaming Benchmark for Physical-World VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23892)
*Yifei Zhao, Qian Lou, Mengxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (57.26) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: REALM是首个面向物理世界VLM的统一红队评测基准，整合12种红队攻击方法、3种防御和13个VLM，通过智能体目标生成管线实现公平比较，发现文本与字体注入攻击最有效，多模态联合优化产生最强视觉扰动迁移，单次攻击接近迭代方法且成本更低，模型规模本身不保证对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM红队评测分散在不同数据集、指标和威胁模型下，难以直接比较不同攻击方法的效果，且缺乏针对物理世界VLM（如具身智能系统）的标准化安全评测基准。

Method: 提出REALM基准，整合12种红队方法（文本攻击、视觉攻击、多模态攻击等）、3种模型无关防御和13个VLM，采用统一黑盒威胁模型和共享数据集/指标；引入智能体目标生成管线，为每个场景构建共享的、场景特定的物理接地攻击目标，实现不同攻击家族在一致对抗目标下的公平比较。

Result: 文本和字体注入攻击引发最多失败；多模态联合优化产生最强的视觉扰动迁移能力；单次攻击在更低成本下接近迭代方法效果；模型规模本身并不保证对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: REALM为物理世界VLM提供了首个统一红队评测框架，揭示了不同攻击方法的相对有效性，并为未来VLM安全研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Classification reason: 该论文提出首个面向物理世界VLM的统一红队评测基准REALM，整合多种攻击方法、防御和模型进行标准化评估，属于多模态安全与攻防（红队测试、对抗攻击、安全评测）范畴，因此复用[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark contribution for VLM safety but outside user's priority subtopics. Signals: Unified red-teaming benchmark, physical-world VLM focus, agentic target-generation pipeline, multimodal co-optimization analysis, model scale vs robustness insight

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as perception-reasoning backbones for embodied intelligence in safety-critical physical systems, where perception or reasoning errors can lead to unsafe decisions or actions. Although many red-teaming methods have been developed to probe VLM vulnerabilities, their evaluation remains fragmented across datasets, metrics, and threat models, making direct comparison difficult and obscuring whether observed differences arise from stronger attacks, more vulnerable models, or incompatible evaluation settings. Existing chatbot-centric red-teaming benchmarks mainly standardize jailbreak and content-safety evaluation, but they do not systematically capture physically grounded functional failures or cover red-teaming methods that target physical-world VLMs. This raises the key challenge of comparing diverse attack methods under a unified protocol while targeting the same scenario-specific failures. We introduce REALM, to our knowledge the first unified red-teaming benchmark for physical-world VLMs. REALM integrates 12 red-teaming methods, 3 model-agnostic defenses, and 13 VLMs under a practical black-box threat model with shared datasets and metrics. To align adversarial objectives across attack families, REALM introduces an agentic target-generation pipeline that constructs shared, scenario-specific, and physically grounded attack objectives for each scene, enabling fair comparison of diverse red-teaming methods under aligned adversarial goals. Our evaluation shows that text and typographic injection attacks induce the most failures, multimodal co-optimization yields the strongest visual-perturbation transfer, single-pass attacks approach iterative methods at much lower cost, and model scale alone does not confer adversarial robustness. Code is available at .

</details>

Method: 从多个现有基准中整合并扩展，构建了7,826个意图（覆盖10大类55子类有害意图），使用多种SOTA攻击策略生成47,524个对抗样本，形成标准化数据集。

Result: 发布了包含47,524个对抗样本的大规模开源数据集，覆盖广泛的有害意图类别，为VLM鲁棒性和安全性评估提供了统一资源。

Conclusion: PHANTOM数据集降低了对抗性研究的门槛，促进了VLM安全评估的可复现性、全面性和可比性，为防御机制开发和模型对齐研究提供了实用资源。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了面向VLM的大规模对抗攻击数据集，涵盖有害意图分类与多种攻击策略，属于多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on adversarial attack dataset for VLM safety, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to user's core interests in continuous models, generation, or world models. Signals: Large-scale adversarial dataset, VLM robustness evaluation, 10 harmful intent categories, 47,524 samples, open-source resource

Abstract: We introduce a large-scale, open-source dataset of pre-generated adversarial attacks for vision-language models (VLMs). The dataset is designed to be diverse, representative, and practical, extending existing benchmarks by covering 10 high-level categories and 55 subcategories of harmful intents. Our primary goal is to make adversarial data accessible to the research community, given the computational cost and complexity of generating large numbers of attacks. The dataset comprises 47 524 adversarial samples, generated using state-of-the-art attack strategies from recent literature. Our work complements existing efforts by consolidating and extending prior benchmarks from multiple established sources, resulting in 7 826 intents, and introduce an additional category to broaden coverage. This provides realistic evaluation resources for studying model robustness and alignment. Our dataset intends to enable researchers and practitioners to systematically evaluate the robustness and safety of VLMs, fine-tune attack-generation models, and develop or stress-test defensive guardrails under diverse adversarial conditions. By releasing this resource, we aim to lower the barrier to adversarial research and foster more reproducible, comprehensive, and comparable evaluations of VLM safety.

</details>


### [60] [Evaluating the Interpretability of Sparse Autoencoders with Concept Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24716)
*Jonas Klotz, Cassio F. Dantas, Pallavi Jain, Diego Marcos, Begüm Demir*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人类标注概念的可解释性评估框架，用于量化稀疏自编码器（SAE）潜变量与语义概念的对齐程度。作者构建了synCUB和synCOCO合成基准数据集，提出Fully-Binary Matching Pursuit（FBMP）匹配方法和Targeted Attribute Perturbation Alignment Score（TAPAScore）评估指标，实验表明中等字典大小的SAE可解释性最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE可解释性评估方法多依赖代理指标或定性检查，缺乏对语义对应关系的直接量化度量。需要一种无需用户研究即可衡量SAE潜变量与人类标注概念之间对齐程度的标准评估框架。

Method: 1) 构建synCUB和synCOCO合成数据集，包含仅在单一属性上不同的配对图像；2) 提出FBMP（Fully-Binary Matching Pursuit）算法，支持SAE潜变量与标注概念之间的多对多匹配；3) 提出TAPAScore（Targeted Attribute Perturbation Alignment Score），通过目标属性扰动验证匹配概念的选择性和方向正确性。

Result: FBMP和TAPAScore是唯一能在合理性检验中可靠区分训练SAE与未训练SAE的指标。在CLIP和DINOv2嵌入上训练的SAE中，过完备性增加会降低扰动对齐度，表明可解释性下降。中等字典大小提供了最佳的可解释性权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的评估框架为SAE可解释性提供了更可靠的量化标准，揭示了字典大小与可解释性之间的权衡关系，为未来多模态模型内部表征分析提供了方法论基础。

Classification reason: 该论文研究稀疏自编码器（SAE）在视觉与视觉语言模型中的可解释性评估，属于多模态模型的可解释性与可靠性范畴。虽然论文本身不直接涉及安全攻防，但"多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）是现有[CANONICAL]中与可解释性、可靠性最相关的宽泛主题，且该方向涵盖模型内部表征分析与可靠性评测。论文提出的评估框架用于衡量SAE潜变量与人类标注概念的对齐，本质上是对多模态模型内部表征的可靠性评估，因此归入此方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on SAE interpretability evaluation, not directly aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Sparse autoencoder interpretability, concept alignment evaluation, synthetic benchmarks, moderate dictionary size optimal, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly used to extract interpretable concepts from vision and vision language models, yet existing evaluation methods largely rely on proxy metrics or qualitative inspection rather than measuring semantic correspondence. We present a human-grounded evaluation framework that quantifies alignment between SAE latents and human-annotated concepts, without requiring user studies, and validate this matching through targeted attribute perturbations. To enable this intervention-style evaluation in vision, we construct synCUB and synCOCO, synthetic benchmarks of paired images that differ in exactly one attribute. We introduce Fully-Binary Matching Pursuit (FBMP), a coalition-based matching procedure that supports many-to-one mappings between SAE latents and annotated concepts, and consistently outperforms one-to-one baselines. For functional validation, we propose a Targeted Attribute Perturbation Alignment Score (TAPAScore), which tests whether matched concepts respond selectively and in the expected direction under targeted image-level attribute perturbations. Under sanity checks, our matching and TAPAScore are the only evaluated metrics that reliably distinguish trained SAEs from untrained ones. Across SAEs trained on CLIP and DINOv2 embeddings, we find that increased overcompleteness can reduce perturbation alignment, indicating a reduction in interpretability. Our evaluation framework suggests that moderate dictionary sizes provide the best trade-off, yielding the most interpretable SAEs. Code and datasets are available at .

</details>

### [61] [Listening makes Vision Clear for VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23763)
*Yiyang Chen, Yixin Tan, Binrui Shen*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文发现VLM中答案侧token的注意力分布存在解码漂移和模态边界标记干扰问题，导致视觉-语言一致性评估失真。为此提出基于提示侧语义的PV-TAM方法，通过注意力峰值分布衡量提示与视觉区域的对齐，在多个数据集上显著优于传统答案侧基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM视觉-语言一致性评估依赖答案侧token的注意力分布，但发现最高注意力区域并不总是对应目标语义token，原因是解码漂移（先前生成token的语言先验累积）和结构token（如模态边界标记）的全局注意力干扰。需要更可靠的评估方法。

Method: 提出Prompt-Vision Token Activation Map (PV-TAM)，采用提示侧语义而非答案侧token计算注意力激活图，并引入滤波器去除模态边界标记导致的系统性偏差。利用注意力峰值分布而非传统掩码重叠来衡量提示与视觉区域的对齐程度。

Result: 在多个数据集上，PV-TAM在注意力定位和IoU风格定位指标上一致优于答案侧基线方法，有效缓解了解码漂移和模态边界标记干扰问题。

Conclusion: 提示侧语义的注意力激活图比答案侧方法更可靠地评估VLM的视觉-语言一致性，PV-TAM为VLM的可解释性和忠实性评估提供了有效工具。

Classification reason: 该论文关注VLM视觉注意力与语言一致性评估，提出PV-TAM方法缓解解码漂移和模态边界标记导致的注意力失真，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性（忠实性、一致性）范畴，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Addresses VLM reliability but is not aligned with user's priority subtopics on continuous models or generation. Signals: VLM attention analysis, decoding drift mitigation, prompt-side evaluation, modality boundary markers, PV-TAM method

Abstract: Recent work typically assesses vision--language consistency using attention distributions of answer-side tokens. However, we observe that highest attention regions are not always consistent with the intended semantic token. This probably stems from decoding drift, where language priors from previously generated answer tokens accumulate and mismatch with visual attention. Besides the priors from previous answer tokens, we find that structural tokens, e.g., modality boundary markers, may encompass the entire context and generate high attention to areas unrelated to the target. To avoid these distortions and provide consistency evaluation for large VLMs, we adopt prompt-side semantics and propose Prompt-Vision Token Activation Map (PV-TAM). PV-TAM further incorporates a filter to remove systematic bias induced by modality boundary markers. Unlike traditional methods that evaluate overlap solely through masks while ignoring activation intensity, our metrics leverage the peak distribution of attention to measure the alignment between prompts and visual regions. In experiments, PV-TAM consistently improves both attention-based and IoU-style localization metrics over answer-side baselines on various datasets.

</details>


### [62] [A Benchmark for Hallucination Detection in VLMs for Gastrointestinal Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24115)
*Aminu Lawal, Niyoj Oli, Sachin Acharya, Prashnna Gyawali, Maria Carmen Romano, Binod Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (76.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文在胃肠内窥镜VQA数据集Gut-VLM上系统评测了9种幻觉检测方法，覆盖5个VLM。结果表明白盒方法ReXTrust在所有模型上显著优于其他方法，在MedGemma-4B上达到AUC 93.0。研究还发现了"自信幻觉"（confident confabulation）这一系统性失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在临床部署中面临幻觉问题，但现有幻觉检测方法主要评估于放射学基准，胃肠内窥镜领域尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估不同幻觉检测方法在GI内窥镜场景下的表现。

Method: 在Gut-VLM数据集（4,392个VQA对）上，对5个VLM（MedGemma-4B/27B、LLaVA-Med-7B、LLaVA-v1.6-7B、Lingshu-32B）评测9种幻觉检测方法，分为黑盒（RadFlag、SelfCheckGPT-NLI）、灰盒（AvgProb、AvgEnt、MaxProb、MaxEnt、Semantic Entropy、VASE）和白盒（ReXTrust）三类，使用AUC作为主要指标，采用配对置换检验进行显著性验证。

Result: ReXTrust在所有5个VLM上AUC均显著优于其他方法（p<0.001），在MedGemma-4B上达到最高AUC 93.0。白盒方法平均领先19.5 AUC点。在LLaVA-v1.6-7B上，黑盒和聚类灰盒方法接近随机水平，而ReXTrust仍达AUC 79.9。Token级灰盒统计（MaxEnt、MaxProb）是次优选择。发现"自信幻觉"模式——模型以高一致性或高token概率产生幻觉。

Conclusion: 白盒隐藏状态访问为幻觉检测提供了一致且显著的性能优势，ReXTrust是当前最有效的方法。Token级不确定性统计优于聚类方法。自信幻觉是现有方法面临的系统性挑战，需要新的检测策略。

Classification reason: 该论文研究VLM在胃肠内窥镜场景下的幻觉检测方法，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性方向，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"（multimodal_hallucination）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM hallucination detection, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics of continuous multimodal models and video/image generation. Signals: Medical domain, hallucination detection benchmark, white-box method advantage, confident confabulation failure mode, gastrointestinal endoscopy VQA

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are prone to hallucination, which remains a major barrier to their safe deployment in clinical practice. To date, most hallucination detection methods have been evaluated on radiology benchmarks such as MIMIC-CXR and VQA-RAD, while gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopy remains largely underexplored. In this paper, we benchmark nine hallucination detection methods on the Gut-VLM dataset, a GI diagnostic Visual Question Answering (VQA) dataset with 4,392 test VQA pairs, across five VLMs (MedGemma-4B, MedGemma-27B, LLaVA-Med-7B, LLaVA-v1.6-7B, and Lingshu-32B). The methods span three categories: black-box methods (RadFlag, SelfCheckGPT-NLI), gray-box methods (AvgProb, AvgEnt, MaxProb, MaxEnt, Semantic Entropy, and VASE), and a white-box method (ReXTrust). Our results show that ReXTrust, a white-box method, achieves the highest AUC across all five models, outperforming the strongest alternative method on each VLM by a statistically significant margin (paired permutation test, p < 0.001 in all cases), reaching a peak AUC of 93.0 on MedGemma-4B. White-box hidden-state access provides a consistent advantage of 19.5 AUC points on average (range: 9.5--33.5), with ReXTrust maintaining strong performance even on LLaVA-v1.6-7B (AUC 79.9), where black-box methods and clustering-based gray-box methods collapse to near-chance performance. Among non-white-box methods, token-level gray-box statistics (MaxEnt, MaxProb) are the strongest alternatives, outperforming both clustering-based gray-box methods (Semantic Entropy, VASE) and black-box approaches on average. We further identify confident confabulation, a failure mode in which models hallucinate with high inter-sample consistency or high token-level probability, as a systemic failure for both consistency and uncertainty-based methods.

</details>


### [63] [ActiveScope: Actively Seeking and Correcting Perception for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24292)
*Yajing Wang, Chao Bi, Junshu Sun, Shufan Shen, Zhaobo Qi, Shuhui Wang, Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 高优先级 (88.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ActiveScope是一个无需训练的框架，通过语义锚点定位（SAL）和干扰抑制细化（ISR）两个模块，主动搜索并纠正MLLM在高分辨率图像细粒度感知中的错误，有效缓解了上下文主导和语义偏差问题，在多个基准上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在高分辨率图像细粒度感知中仍存在困难，而现有的无训练方法常被干扰物误导，且难以定位多个目标。作者将失败归因于两个问题：上下文主导（显著干扰物压制目标注意力）和语义偏差（全局语义使模型聚焦于最显著概念导致多目标定位不完整）。

Method: 提出ActiveScope框架，包含两个模块：(1) 语义锚点定位（SAL），利用细粒度语义锚点独立定位关键目标，缓解语义偏差；(2) 干扰抑制细化（ISR），通过抑制对显著干扰物的注意力来克服上下文主导，细化定位结果。

Result: 在高分辨率图像理解基准上，ActiveScope优于现有无训练方法，例如在V* Bench上达到96.34%的准确率。

Conclusion: ActiveScope通过主动搜索和自纠正范式有效提升了MLLM的细粒度感知能力，验证了该范式的优越性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ActiveScope框架，旨在缓解MLLM在高分辨率图像细粒度感知中的"上下文主导"和"语义偏差"问题，通过主动搜索和自纠正提升感知准确性。这属于多模态幻觉缓解与可靠性增强方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态幻觉与可靠性"。

Importance reason: Addresses MLLM fine-grained perception with a training-free correction method, relevant to multimodal understanding but not a top priority subtopic. Signals: MLLM hallucination mitigation, training-free framework, high-resolution image understanding, semantic anchor localization, interference suppression

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive vision-language understanding, yet still struggle with fine-grained perception in high-resolution images. While existing training-free methods typically rely on attention-based localization or coarse-to-fine search, they are often misled by distractors and fail to locate multiple targets. Our investigation attributes these failures to Contextual Dominance, where salient distractors overwhelm target attention and cause inaccurate localization, and Semantic Bias, where global semantics cause the model to fixate on the most salient concept, resulting in incomplete localization in multi-object scenarios. Built on these insights, we propose ActiveScope, a training-free framework that enhances MLLMs by actively seeking and correcting perception. ActiveScope features two modules. The Semantic Anchor Localization (SAL) utilizes fine-grained semantic anchors to independently localize key targets, thereby mitigating semantic bias. The Interference-Suppressed Refinement (ISR) refines localization by suppressing attention on salient distractions to overcome contextual dominance. Extensive experiments on high-resolution image understanding benchmarks demonstrate that ActiveScope outperforms existing training-free methods (e.g., 96.34 percent accuracy on $V^{*}$ Bench), validating the superiority of the active search and self-correction paradigm. Our code is available at .

</details>
