### [64] [Mind the Heads: Topological Representation Alignment for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23885)
*Davide Caffagni, Alberto Compagnoni, Federico Melis, Sara Sarto, Pier Luigi Dovesi, Mark Granroth-Wilding, Marcella Cornia, Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (80.4) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 HeRA（Head-Wise Representation Alignment），一种在注意力头级别进行跨模态表征拓扑对齐的方法，通过 MKNN 度量和对比学习目标对齐 LLM 中各注意力头的局部邻域结构，有效提升 MLLM 在视觉任务上的性能并缓解幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征对齐方法通常对齐语言骨干网络的固定层，忽略了 Transformer 模型中注意力头的细粒度结构。本文旨在利用柏拉图表征假说，在注意力头级别进行跨模态拓扑结构对齐，以更精细地改善 MLLM 的视觉表征质量。

Method: 提出 HeRA，基于 Mutual K-Nearest Neighbor (MKNN) 度量，设计一个可微的对比学习目标来匹配跨模态的局部邻域结构。在训练过程中，根据 MKNN 对齐分数选择 LLM 中特定的注意力头进行对齐，并发现对齐最不对齐的头（least aligned heads）能带来最大收益。

Result: 在多个 MLLM 和 18 个基准上的广泛评估表明，HeRA 在视觉中心任务上持续提升性能，并有效作为视觉幻觉的正则化手段，自然抑制了对语言先验的过度依赖。

Conclusion: 注意力头级别的拓扑表征对齐是一种有效的 MLLM 改进策略，能够提升视觉理解能力并缓解幻觉，为跨模态对齐提供了更细粒度的新视角。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 HeRA 方法，通过对注意力头级别的表征拓扑对齐来改进 MLLM 的跨模态对齐，属于多模态对齐范畴，因此映射到 canonical 子主题"多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Addresses representation alignment in MLLMs with a novel head-wise topological approach, relevant to multimodal understanding and hallucination mitigation. Signals: multimodal LLM alignment, attention head topology, hallucination reduction, MKNN metric, Platonic Representation Hypothesis

Abstract: Representation alignment has emerged as an effective approach to improve Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by regularizing their internal representations toward those of an external vision encoder. However, existing methods typically align a fixed layer of the language backbone, overlooking the fine-grained structure of Transformer models. In this work, we propose Head-Wise Representation Alignment (HeRA), a method that enforces cross-modal alignment at the level of individual attention heads. Our approach is grounded in the Platonic Representation Hypothesis, focusing on preserving the topological structure of representations (i.e., their local neighborhood relationships) across modalities. Following the Mutual K-Nearest Neighbor (MKNN) alignment metric, we introduce a contrastive objective that acts as a differentiable proxy for matching local structures. HeRA applies this objective during multimodal training to specific attention heads in the LLM, selected by their alignment score according to the MKNN metric. Counterintuitively, we find that aligning the least aligned heads yields the largest gains. Extensive evaluations across multiple MLLMs and 18 benchmarks demonstrate that HeRA consistently improves performance on challenging vision-centric tasks and serves as an effective regularizer against visual hallucinations by naturally curbing the over-reliance on linguistic priors. Our code is publicly released.

</details>


### [65] [Towards Spec Learning: Inference-Time Alignment from Preference Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24004)
*Dhriti Krishnan, Tejas Goyal, Jaromir Savelka*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.79) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 spec learning 框架，通过少量偏好判断自动编译为自然语言规格说明（specifications），在推理时条件化 LLM 实现行为对齐，无需参数更新，在偏好信号密集的领域上超越 DPO，且生成的规格说明具有可读性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 行为对齐方法要么依赖繁琐的手工提示词工程（脆弱且易错），要么依赖基于偏好的微调（如 DPO）但成本高昂。本文希望找到一种轻量级、可解释且无需参数更新的对齐方法。

Method: 提出 spec learning 框架：用户提供简短指令和少量偏好判断（preference pairs），系统将这些偏好编译为自然语言规格说明（specifications），作为 LLM 的推理时条件提示。无需更新模型参数，规格说明是人类可读的文本形式。

Result: 在偏好信号密集的专门领域数据集上，基于规格说明生成的响应往往优于直接偏好优化（DPO）方法。规格说明具有可解释性和透明性，可作为偏好信号的可读体现。

Conclusion: Spec learning 提供了一种轻量级、可解释的 LLM 对齐替代方案，在特定场景下可超越 DPO，且避免了昂贵的参数微调，为推理时对齐提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 spec learning 框架，利用偏好对（preference pairs）在推理时对齐 LLM 行为，无需参数更新即可超越 DPO。这属于偏好学习与对齐的范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配，尽管论文主要针对 LLM 而非多模态，但其核心方法（偏好对齐、DPO 对比）完全属于该主题范畴。

Importance reason: Focuses on inference-time alignment via preference-based specification generation, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: inference-time alignment, preference-based specification, no parameter update, interpretable specifications, outperforms DPO on dense preference domains

Abstract: Steering a large language model (LLM) toward a desired behavior typically relies on an iterative process of hand-crafting a prompt based on a careful inspection of the model's responses. This is an involved, brittle, and error-prone process. Preference-based fine-tuning is a more rigorous but often prohibitively expensive solution. We propose spec learning, a framework that relies on a brief user instruction and a small set of preference judgments. These are compiled into specifications in the form of natural-language prompts for an LLM. Specifications condition LLMs at inference time, and no parameter updates to the underlying models are required. We show that the responses generated based on the compiled specifications often outperform direct preference optimization (DPO) on datasets from specialized domains whose preference signal is dense. Unlike opaque weight updates, the resulting specifications are human-readable and double as interpretable and transparent written embodiments of the preference signal that produced them.

</details>


### [66] [HANCLIP: A Family of Hyperbolic Angular Negation Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23843)
*Hoang-Bao Le, Aiden Durrant, Thai Son Mai, Binh T. Nguyen, Liting Zhou, Cathal Gurrin*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: HANCLIP 提出一种几何感知的 VLM 微调方法，通过双曲空间建模和角度三元组损失，在仅使用 2 万图像-文本四元组数据的情况下，显著提升 CLIP 系列模型对否定描述的敏感性，同时保持甚至提升标准检索和分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM（如 CLIP）对否定（negation）非常脆弱，容易依赖浅层词共现而被误导性文本干扰；直接微调否定数据又会破坏预训练知识，导致标准任务性能下降。因此需要一种不破坏原有表征结构、同时增强否定敏感性的方法。

Method: 提出 HANCLIP 框架，包含两个核心设计：(1) 双曲空间（hyperbolic）建模，利用双曲几何的层次结构特性编码语义不对称性；(2) 角度三元组损失（angular triplet objective），在嵌入空间中系统性地分离否定描述与其对应的肯定描述。训练使用 20,000 个图像-文本四元组（image, positive caption, negative caption, hard negative caption），模型无关，可即插即用于 CLIP、LongCLIP、SmartCLIP、HiMo-CLIP。

Result: 在 NegBench 否定基准上取得一致提升，同时在标准分类和图像-文本检索基准上保持或提升性能。多个 CLIP 变体均受益于该框架，验证了方法的通用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 精心设计的几何目标函数可以在仅使用少量额外数据的情况下显著扩展现有 VLM 的推理能力，为提升 VLM 的语言理解鲁棒性提供了一种高效、模型无关的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 对否定（negation）的敏感性，通过几何目标（双曲空间+角度三元组损失）重构嵌入空间来增强模型对否定描述的区分能力，属于多模态对齐与偏好学习范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Focuses on negation sensitivity in VLMs, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: negation sensitivity, hyperbolic embedding, angular triplet loss, CLIP fine-tuning, small data requirement

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are typically pre-trained on large-scale image-text datasets to capture semantic correspondences between visual content and natural language. However, they remain surprisingly brittle to negation: models often rely on shallow word co-occurrence and are easily distracted by misleading or irrelevant textual cues, even when their overall retrieval or classification performance is strong. Moreover, directly finetuning on negation data can interfere with previously acquired knowledge, causing noticeable degradation on standard vision-language benchmarks. To tackle these issues, this work introduces HANCLIP (Hyperbolic + Angular + Negation), a family of VLMs that explicitly restructures the embedding space to encode "what an image is not" alongside "what it is." HANCLIP is trained on a compact set of 20,000 image-text quadruplets and combines a hyperbolic formulation, which models hierarchical semantic relations and asymmetries, with an angular triplet objective that drives systematic separation between negated descriptions and their corresponding positives. This geometry-aware design strengthens negation sensitivity while preserving the global structure of pretrained representations, rather than overwriting them. Extensive experiments across multiple vision-language tasks show that HANCLIP delivers consistent gains on the negation-focused NegBench benchmark, while maintaining competitive or improved performance on standard classification and image-text retrieval benchmarks. The framework is model-agnostic and can be plugged into CLIP, LongCLIP, SmartCLIP, and HiMo-CLIP without large-scale retraining, demonstrating that a carefully designed geometric objective can substantially extend the reasoning capabilities of existing VLMs using only modest additional data.

</details>


### [67] [The Professor: Multi-Teacher Unsupervised Prompt Distillation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23897)
*Ahmad Algadhi, Ahmed Alzuhair, Omar Alkhulaif, Muzammil Behzad*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 TheProfessor，一种多教师无监督提示蒸馏方法，将两个 CLIP 教师模型（领域微调的 PromptSRC ViT-L/14 和零样本 EVA-CLIP-L/14）的集成知识蒸馏到轻量学生模型。在四个 base-to-novel 数据集上，置信度加权集成相比单教师 PromptKD 平均 HM 提升 1.77 点，在领域偏移场景（EuroSAT）上提升最大（+5.78）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PromptKD 等单教师提示蒸馏方法受限于单一教师的知识，在领域偏移场景下泛化能力有限。多教师集成可以提供互补的监督信号，但如何有效融合多个教师的知识进行蒸馏尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用固定双教师集成：一个领域微调的 PromptSRC ViT-L/14 教师和一个零样本 EVA-CLIP-L/14 教师（其 logits 可预计算）。探索两种集成策略：等概率平均和置信度加权平均，将集成后的 logits 作为软标签蒸馏到 ViT-B/16 学生模型。

Result: 在 Caltech-101、DTD、UCF101、EuroSAT 四个数据集上，置信度加权集成平均 HM 从 87.52 提升至 89.28（+1.77），等概率平均提升至 88.88（+1.37）。Caltech-101 提升微小（+0.16），UCF101 中等（+0.62），领域偏移最大的 EuroSAT 提升最显著（+5.78）。

Conclusion: 多教师提示蒸馏在领域偏移场景下最有效，当第二个教师提供互补监督时能显著提升学生模型性能。置信度加权集成优于等概率平均，但收益因数据集而异。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多教师无监督提示蒸馏（prompt distillation）方法，将大视觉语言模型（CLIP）的知识蒸馏到轻量学生模型，属于多模态模型的对齐与知识迁移范畴。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）中的偏好学习、对齐等主题高度相关，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Prompt distillation for VLMs is incremental and not aligned with your priority on continuous/multimodal models or generation. Signals: Multi-teacher distillation, PromptKD extension, domain shift gains, no generation or continuous token focus, low priority subtopic match

Abstract: Prompt distillation compresses large vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP into lightweight student models by matching teacher predictions on unlabeled domain images. PromptKD (CVPR 2024) established this paradigm with a single PromptSRC-finetuned ViT-L/14 teacher and a ViT-B/16 student. We propose TheProfessor, a multi-teacher extension that distills from a fixed two-teacher ensemble: a domain-finetuned PromptSRC ViT-L/14 teacher and a zero-shot EVA-CLIP-L/14 teacher whose logits are pre-computed per dataset. We evaluate single-teacher PromptKD, equal-probability ensembling, and confidence-weighted ensembling on four base-to-novel datasets: Caltech-101, DTD, UCF101, and EuroSAT. In a 12-run single-seed sweep, confidence-weighted ensembling improves average HM from 87.52 to 89.28 (+1.77 points), while equal averaging improves average HM to 88.88 (+1.37 points). Gains are dataset dependent: they are negligible on Caltech-101 (+0.16 HM for confidence weighting), modest on UCF101 (+0.62), and largest on domain-shifted EuroSAT (+5.78). These results update our earlier Caltech-only analysis and show that multi-teacher prompt distillation is most useful when the second teacher contributes complementary supervision under domain shift.

</details>


### [68] [Reinforcement Learning for Computer-Use Agents with Autonomous Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24515)
*Marta Sumyk, Oleksandr Kosovan*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.HC

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (54.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种使用自主视觉语言评估作为可扩展监督信号的强化学习微调框架，用于训练计算机使用智能体（CUA）。通过将VLM评估建模为带噪二元奖励通道并推导噪声校正的奖励估计器，在多个桌面环境基准上显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）在开放桌面环境中缺乏可扩展的机器可读奖励信号，任务成功与否通常依赖于视觉判断，难以用人工设计的奖励函数或密集标注来指定，这限制了强化学习在该领域的应用。

Method: 提出RL微调框架，使用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为自主评估器：给定最终截图和原始指令，VLM判断任务完成度并提供终端反馈。将VLM评估建模为带噪二元奖励通道，推导噪声校正的奖励估计器用于PPO优化，从而在策略优化过程中无需任务特定的启发式规则或人工标注。

Result: 在macOSWorld、Windows Agent Arena和OSWorld三个基准上，校正后的评估器奖励优于零样本基线和原始评估器奖励，平均成功率比零样本提升12.6个百分点，比原始评估器微调提升5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 自主视觉语言评估可以作为GUI环境中RL的实用奖励信号，前提是显式建模并校正评估器的噪声。该方法为CUA的强化学习训练提供了一种可扩展的替代方案。

Classification reason: 该论文使用视觉语言模型作为自主评估器，为GUI智能体提供奖励信号，并通过PPO进行强化学习微调，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）完美匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on RL for GUI agents, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: RL fine-tuning, autonomous VLM evaluation, noisy reward correction, GUI agents, desktop benchmarks

Abstract: Computer-Use Agents (CUAs) execute high-level user goals by perceiving and acting directly within graphical user interfaces. However, reinforcement learning for CUAs remains difficult because open-ended desktop environments rarely provide scalable, machine-readable reward signals: task success is often visually grounded and hard to specify with handcrafted reward functions or dense manual labels. We propose an RL fine-tuning framework that uses autonomous vision-language evaluation as a scalable supervision signal for GUI agents. Given a final screenshot and the original instruction, a Vision-Language Model judges task completion and provides terminal feedback without task-specific heuristics or manual labels during policy optimization. Because autonomous evaluators are imperfect, we model their feedback as a noisy binary reward channel and derive a noise-corrected reward estimator for Proximal Policy Optimization. Experiments across macOSWorld, Windows Agent Arena, and OSWorld show that corrected evaluator rewards outperform both zero-shot baselines and raw evaluator rewards, improving success rates by an average of 12.6 percentage points over zero-shot performance and 5.1 points over raw evaluator fine-tuning. These results suggest that autonomous evaluation can serve as a practical reward signal for RL in GUI environments when evaluator noise is explicitly modeled and corrected.

</details>


### [69] [VisCritic: Visual State Comparison as Process Reward for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24525)
*Jiachen Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisCritic 提出一种视觉过程奖励框架，通过孪生视觉 Transformer 比较操作前后截图，为 GUI 智能体提供步骤级视觉验证，无需额外人工标注即可训练批评模型，在多个基准上提升智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 智能体在长程任务中频繁失败，缺乏步骤级验证机制。已有的过程奖励模型仅通过文本推理验证动作，忽略了 GUI 状态变化的视觉本质。

Method: VisCritic 采用孪生视觉 Transformer 提取操作前后截图的"变化感知"表征，结合 Action-Aware Critic Head 联合评估动作成功性、任务进度和错误类型。通过弱监督数据构建流水线从现有轨迹中生成训练数据，无需额外人工标注。

Result: 在五个基准上的实验和离线分析表明，VisCritic 可作为即插即用模块提升多种 GUI 智能体的基准指标，并提供视觉诊断线索。

Conclusion: VisCritic 验证了视觉状态比较作为 GUI 智能体过程奖励的有效性，弥补了纯文本奖励模型的视觉盲区，为长程 GUI 任务提供了可靠的步骤级验证机制。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 VisCritic，一种用于 GUI 智能体的视觉过程奖励框架，通过比较操作前后截图来验证动作。这属于多模态对齐中的过程奖励建模（process reward），与多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）方向高度吻合。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI agent process reward, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: GUI agents, process reward, visual state comparison, Siamese vision transformer, weakly supervised training

Abstract: GUI agents powered by vision-language models show strong potential for automating digital tasks, yet frequently fail in long-horizon scenarios due to the absence of step-level verification. Existing process reward models verify actions through textual reasoning alone, missing the visual nature of GUI state changes. We introduce VisCritic, a visual process reward framework that verifies agent actions by directly comparing pre-action and post-action screenshots in visual feature space. VisCritic employs a Siamese vision transformer to extract change-aware representations, coupled with an Action-Aware Critic Head that jointly evaluates action success, task progress, and error type. A critic-training data construction pipeline generates weakly supervised samples from existing trajectories without additional human labels for critic training. Experiments and offline analyses across five benchmarks demonstrate that VisCritic serves as a plug-and-play enhancement for diverse GUI agents, generally improving benchmark metrics while providing visual diagnostic cues.

</details>

### [70] [Neuro-Symbolic Drive: Rule-Grounded Faithful Reasoning for Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23938)
*Xiangbo Gao, Xiukun Huang, Boyu Lu, Junge Zhang, Mengjie Mao, Jiachen Li, Wei Xiong, Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (63.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Neuro-Symbolic Drive，通过从经典规则基规划器中提取规则驱动的推理轨迹作为监督信号，微调 Qwen3.5-4B 作为驾驶 VLA 模型，使推理过程与运动生成在结构上因果耦合，显著提升了驾驶决策的推理忠实性和轨迹预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的驾驶 VLA 模型虽然结合了 CoT 推理，但其推理过程往往缺乏逐步决策语义，推理与最终运动规划之间缺乏因果联系。作者观察到规则基规划器本身就是可执行的符号推理引擎，其内部决策轨迹天然与最终动作因果相关，因此希望利用这些轨迹来监督 VLA 模型，使其推理过程与运动生成在结构上耦合。

Method: 在仿真环境中对规则基规划器进行插桩，捕获执行轨迹和每一步规则评估的内部决策轨迹。将每条轨迹序列化为结构化的规则驱动推理文本，与对应的轨迹配对，用于微调 Qwen3.5-4B 作为驾驶 VLA 模型。由于推理轨迹直接来源于决定最终动作的规划器状态，推理与运动生成在结构上天然耦合。

Result: 在仿真基准上，规则驱动推理将 ADE@3s 从 0.47 降至 0.26，miss rate 从 8.30% 降至 6.40%（三摄像头感知）；八摄像头感知下 ADE@3s 从 0.54 降至 0.26，miss rate 从 10.13% 降至 5.99%。

Conclusion: Neuro-Symbolic Drive 成功将神经符号规划逻辑转化为结构化监督信号，使驾驶 VLA 模型获得与运动生成因果耦合的推理能力，显著提升了轨迹预测的准确性和安全性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Neuro-Symbolic Drive，利用规则基规划器的决策轨迹生成结构化的规则驱动推理链（rule-grounded reasoning traces），用于监督驾驶 VLA 模型进行 Chain-of-Thought 推理。核心贡献在于多模态（视觉-语言-动作）场景下的结构化推理，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）范畴，该 subtopic 为 [CANONICAL] 条目。

Importance reason: Strong neuro-symbolic reasoning for driving VLAs, but outside core priority subtopics and lacks direct continuous/multimodal generation focus. Signals: neuro-symbolic driving, rule-grounded reasoning, VLA fine-tuning, faithful reasoning, trajectory prediction

Abstract: Driving VLA models incorporating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning are attractive because they leverage pretrained VLM representations and expose intermediate decisions in natural language, yet current rationales often lack the step-by-step decision semantics needed to keep the rationale causally connected to the planned motion. We introduce Neuro-Symbolic Drive, a neuro-symbolic driving framework that supervises a driving VLA with rule-grounded reasoning traces extracted directly from classical rule-based planners. Our key observation is that rule-based planners are symbolic AI systems that already function as executable reasoning engines: they reason about active safety constraints, search over candidate maneuvers, and select a final trajectory. We instrument these planners in simulation to capture both the executed trajectory and the internal decision trace at each rule-evaluation step. Each trace is serialized into structured rule-grounded reasoning and paired with the trajectory to fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B as a driving VLA. Because these traces are derived directly from the planner states that determine the action, they ensure reasoning is structurally coupled to motion generation by construction, rather than by post-hoc alignment. On our simulator-generated benchmark, detailed rule-grounded reasoning reduces ADE@3s from 0.47 to 0.26 and miss rate from 8.30% to 6.40% under three-camera perception, and from 0.54 to 0.26 and 10.13% to 5.99% under eight-camera perception. Neuro-Symbolic Drive thus converts neuro-symbolic planning logic into structured supervision. Code base: .

</details>


### [71] [PointVG-R: Internalizing Geometric Reasoning in MLLMs for Precise Pointing Localization via Visual Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24539)
*Ling Li, Bowen Liu, Zinuo Zhan, Jianhui Zhong, Ziyu Zhu, Bingcai Wei, Kenglun Chang, Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PointVG-R 提出了一种几何感知推理引导的 MLLM，通过视觉思维链、强化学习和自适应重要性加权策略，在指向式视觉定位任务上显著提升性能，mIoU 提升 15.86 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指向式视觉定位方法通常将输入图像编码为静态特征表示，主要在语言域中进行推理，忽略了图像中丰富的感知线索和显式空间几何信息，导致模型在解释手势空间关系时存在认知脆弱性。

Method: 1) 设计几何推理流水线，模拟人类解读指向手势的迭代认知过程；2) 构建 EgoPoint-CoT 高质量视觉思维链数据集，通过 SFT 和 RL 引导模型；3) 提出基于组方差的自适应重要性加权策略，动态调整奖励信号以优化学习过程。

Result: 在指向式视觉定位任务上达到 SOTA 性能，mIoU 相比基线提升 15.86 个点，消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: PointVG-R 通过将几何推理内化到 MLLM 中，结合视觉思维链和强化学习，有效提升了模型在指向定位任务中的空间理解能力，验证了推理引导策略在多模态定位中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PointVG-R，通过视觉思维链（Visual CoT）和强化学习增强 MLLM 在指向定位任务中的几何推理能力，属于多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Strongly advances MLLM geometric reasoning with visual chain-of-thought and RL, aligning with multimodal understanding and generation priorities. Signals: multimodal large language model, visual chain-of-thought, reinforcement learning, geometric reasoning, pointing localization

Abstract: Pointing-based visual grounding requires models to precisely locate target objects by deciphering complex spatial relationships between the visual scene and pointing gestures. Traditional methods typically encode input images into static feature representations and perform reasoning primarily within the linguistic domain, often overlooking the rich perceptual cues and explicit spatial geometry inherent in images. In this study, we aim to mitigate the cognitive vulnerability of models in interpreting gestural spatial relations by proposing PointVG-R, a reasoning-guided Multi-modal Large Language Model (MLLM). PointVG-R introduces geometric-aware reasoning for pointing-based grounding, enabling the model to think with images through the strategic integration of Reinforcement Learning (RL) and cold-start data. Specifically, we design a novel geometric reasoning pipeline that simulates the iterative cognitive process humans employ when interpreting pointing gestures. Furthermore, we construct EgoPoint-CoT, a high-quality visual Chain-of-Thought (CoT) dataset featuring detailed reasoning trajectories to guide the model via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and RL. To address the varying quality of learning signals encountered during training, we further propose an Adaptive Importance Weighting strategy based on Group Variance, which dynamically adjusts reward signals to optimize the learning process. Experimental results demonstrate that PointVG-R achieves SOTA performance, outperforming the baseline by $\textbf{15.86}$ points in mIoU. Extensive ablation studies further validate the efficacy of our proposed modules. Code: .

</details>


### [72] [SER: Learning to Ground Video Reasoning with Semantic Evidence Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24726)
*Sheng Xia, Zhengqin Lai, Tianxiang Jiang, Kanghui Tian, Shoujun Zhou, Bin Li, Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

TL;DR: 提出 Semantic Evidence Reward (SER)，将视频 MLLM 的时空证据定位重构为约束验证任务，使用裁判 VLM 评估证据的相关性与定位质量，替代传统 IoU 奖励，在 V-STAR 基准上提升 3.0 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频 MLLM 在细粒度时空推理中常基于无关帧或物体生成正确答案，缺乏可靠的证据定位能力。现有 RL 框架仅依赖几何 IoU 奖励，对边界扰动敏感且忽略语义对齐。

Method: 提出 Semantic Evidence Reward (SER)，将时空证据定位重构为约束验证任务。使用裁判 VLM 作为局部检查器，从相关性（relevance）和定位质量（localization quality）两个维度评估模型生成的证据，并结合时间惩罚项。无需密集框标注，可直接在标准视频 QA 数据上训练。

Result: 在 V-STAR 基准上达到 49.6% mLGM，比强基线 Open-o3-Video 提升 3.0 个百分点，同时提升了答案准确率和证据定位质量。

Conclusion: SER 通过语义对齐的奖励设计有效增强了视频 MLLM 的时空推理证据定位能力，减少了对密集标注的依赖，为视频推理的可解释性和可靠性提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文关注视频 MLLM 的细粒度时空推理与证据定位，属于多模态推理范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on video MLLM reasoning with semantic rewards, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: Video MLLM reasoning, Semantic evidence reward, Spatio-temporal grounding, V-STAR benchmark, Weak on user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Video MLLMs often struggle with fine-grained spatio-temporal reasoning, sometimes generating correct answers based on irrelevant frames or objects. Although outputting spatio-temporal evidence during reasoning is a promising direction, existing RL frameworks typically rely on geometry-only (IoU) rewards, which can be sensitive to boundary perturbations and overlook semantic alignment. To address this, we propose Semantic Evidence Reward (SER), which reformulates spatio-temporal evidence grounding as a constrained verification task. Instead of computing pixel-level overlap, SER uses a referee VLM as a local checker to evaluate model-generated evidence claims across two dimensions: relevance and localization quality, combined with a temporal penalty. This design reduces the reliance on dense box annotations and enables training directly on standard video QA data. On the V-STAR benchmark, SER achieves 49.6% mLGM, improving by 3.0 points over the strong evidence-grounded baseline Open-o3-Video, demonstrating its potential in enhancing both answer accuracy and evidence grounding.

</details>

### [73] [DREAM: Dense Retrieval Embeddings via Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24667)
*Yixuan Tang, Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (55.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DREAM提出利用自回归语言模型的next-token预测目标来训练密集检索嵌入模型，通过将检索器生成的查询-文档相似度分数注入冻结LLM的注意力头，使预测损失通过注意力机制反向传播以训练检索器，在BEIR和RTEB基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索器通常依赖对比学习训练，需要标注的正负文档对，成本高且难以获取。本文探索能否利用LLM的自回归next-token预测目标为密集检索提供监督信号，从而减少对人工标注的依赖。

Method: DREAM将检索器生成的查询-文档相似度分数注入冻结LLM的选定注意力头中，这些分数决定LLM预测目标输出时每个候选文档获得的注意力权重。通过注意力机制，预测损失为检索器训练提供梯度，实现端到端训练。

Result: 在BEIR和RTEB检索基准上，使用0.5B到3B参数的嵌入骨干网络，DREAM在不同模型规模下均一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DREAM证明了通过自回归建模训练密集检索器的可行性，为减少对比学习对标注数据的依赖提供了新思路，且方法在不同规模模型上均有效。

Classification reason: 该论文研究密集检索嵌入模型（dense retrieval embeddings），属于检索方向。虽然论文主要聚焦文本检索（BEIR/RTEB基准），但密集检索是多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）的基础技术，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic涵盖文档检索，因此映射到该现有分类。

Importance reason: Novel use of autoregressive LLM loss for dense retrieval training, but retrieval is not a priority subtopic. Signals: Dense retrieval, autoregressive modeling, next-token prediction, BEIR benchmark, embedding model training

Abstract: Dense retrieval embedding models are a fundamental component of modern retrieval-based AI systems. Most dense retrievers are trained with contrastive objectives, which require labeled positive and negative document pairs that are often costly and difficult to obtain. In this work, we investigate whether the autoregressive next-token prediction objective of a large language model (LLM) can provide supervision for dense retrieval. The intuition is simple: if a document contains information relevant to a query, conditioning on that document should make the target output easier for the LLM to predict. A key challenge is that the next-token prediction loss is computed inside the LLM, while the retriever is a separate embedding model. To address this challenge, we propose DREAM (Dense Retrieval Embeddings via Autoregressive Modeling), which injects retriever-generated query-document similarity scores into selected attention heads of a frozen LLM. During training, these scores determine how much attention each candidate document receives while the LLM predicts the target output. The resulting prediction loss provides gradients for retriever training through the attention mechanism. We evaluate DREAM on retrieval benchmarks BEIR and RTEB using embedding backbones ranging from 0.5B to 3B parameters. DREAM consistently outperforms existing baselines across different model scales. These results demonstrate that DREAM provides a promising approach for training dense retrievers through autoregressive modeling.

</details>


### [74] [Jolia: Concept-Level Vision-Language Alignment for 3D CT Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24570)
*Julien Khlaut, Charles Corbière, Baptiste Callard, Amaury Prat, Leo Butsanets, Antoine Saporta, Théo Danielou, Leo Machado, Korentin Le Floch, Tom Boeken, Pierre Manceron, Corentin Dancette*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ConQuer（Concept Queries），一种增强 CLIP 的医学影像-文本对比预训练方法。通过将放射报告拆分为概念级段落，并为每个概念（如器官）学习交叉注意力查询，实现局部对齐，无需分割掩码。训练得到的 Jolia 模型在胸部/腹部 CT 的发现分类、报告生成和跨中心迁移上超越 CLIP 基线，并在多个公开基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 风格的对比预训练将每张图像和整段文本压缩为单个全局 token，但医学影像包含数十个器官，放射报告结构复杂且篇幅长，全局对齐会丢失重要的局部细节。因此需要一种能同时进行全局和局部对齐的预训练方法。

Method: ConQuer 将放射报告按概念（解剖区域）拆分为多个段落，为每个概念学习一个交叉注意力查询（cross-attention query），从图像特征中池化出对应的局部特征，无需分割掩码或空间监督。然后对每个概念独立进行对比学习，同时保留 CLIP 的全局对齐损失。训练得到的模型称为 Jolia，基于 3D CT 数据。

Result: Jolia 在发现分类、报告生成和跨中心迁移任务上一致优于 CLIP 基线，并在多个公开基准上达到新的 SOTA。每个概念查询还学会了聚焦于对应解剖区域的注意力图，提供了内置的空间可解释性。

Conclusion: 概念级局部对齐能有效弥补 CLIP 全局对齐在医学影像-文本预训练中的不足，ConQuer 方法无需额外标注即可实现细粒度跨模态对齐，并带来可解释性收益。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ConQuer 方法，通过概念级查询增强 CLIP 的全局对齐，实现 3D CT 影像与放射报告的对比学习预训练。核心是跨模态（视觉-语言）对比学习与检索，属于多模态检索方向，因此映射到 canonical subtopic "多模态检索"。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical CT contrastive learning, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and includes negative keywords. Signals: Medical imaging, CLIP-style pretraining, concept-level alignment, CT foundation model, no segmentation masks

Abstract: Vision-language contrastive pretraining has become the dominant recipe for 3D medical foundation models, leveraging the large volumes of paired scans and reports produced in clinical practice. However, medical images usually span dozens of organs, and radiological reports are much longer than typical natural image captions and are composed of multiple structured sections. CLIP-style pretraining compresses this structure by encoding each modality into a single global token, at the risk of losing important details. We introduce ConQuer (Concept Queries), an image-text pretraining method that augments CLIP's global alignment with a set of localized alignments, one per concept. ConQuer splits the report into concept-specific sections and learns cross-attention queries that pool the matching image features without using any segmentation mask or spatial supervision. Contrastive learning is then applied independently for each concept. Concepts can be any unit of semantic localization; here, they are anatomical regions, one query per organ or gross body region. As a byproduct, each query learns attention maps focused on its concept, providing built-in spatial interpretability. We use ConQuer to train Jolia, a 3D CT foundation model on chest and abdominal CT. Jolia consistently outperforms a CLIP baseline on findings classification, report generation, and cross-center transfer, and sets a new state of the art across multiple public benchmarks. Jolia's weights will be released upon acceptance.

</details>

### [75] [MedBench v5: A Dynamic, Process-Oriented, and Hallucination-Aware Benchmark for Clinical Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24155)
*Ding Jinru, Jiang Chuchu, Lu Lu, Pang Wenrao, Bian Mouxiao, Gao Zhuangzhi, Chen Jiangyuan, Peng xinwei, Chen Ruiyao, Ren Sijie, Lu Renjie, Han Bin, Liu Meiling, and Xu Jie*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (71.41) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MedBench v5 是一个面向临床多模态模型的动态、过程导向、幻觉感知的评测基准。它从静态 QA 转向动态过程评估，包含双维框架（临床认知响应14子维度+医学原子技能4个智能体环境，共63个任务）、三种可切换信息流压力源（遗漏、矛盾、证据延迟）、五节点动态过程审计协议以及幻觉传播监控机制。实验表明，强整体任务性能不能保证过程稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI基准缺乏过程可见性、原子技能评估和集成幻觉检测，无法全面评估临床多模态模型在实际动态场景中的表现。

Method: MedBench v5 设计了：(1) 双维框架——临床认知响应（14个子维度）和医学原子技能（4个智能体环境），覆盖63个任务；(2) 三种可切换信息流压力源（遗漏、矛盾、证据延迟）用于分解式退化分析；(3) 五节点动态过程审计协议，生成模型特定的失败指纹；(4) 幻觉传播监控，追踪幻觉的发起、传播、锚定和矛盾交互，捕获静默幻觉。

Result: 在前沿模型上的实验表明，强整体任务性能不能保证过程稳定性。压力源主要破坏矛盾检测、诊断更新、幻觉传播和基于矛盾的自校正，而最终证据定位可能表面上保持稳定。

Conclusion: MedBench v5 为临床AI评估提供了统一的基础设施，支持能力画像、可控压力测试、过程审计和幻觉轨迹分析，揭示了现有模型在过程稳定性和幻觉管理方面的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MedBench v5，一个面向临床多模态模型（语言、视觉-语言、智能体系统）的动态、过程导向、幻觉感知的评测基准。这属于多模态评测与基准方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on clinical medical benchmarks, which are explicitly excluded by negative keywords, and lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: clinical benchmark, hallucination-aware, process-oriented, medical AI, negative keyword match

Abstract: Existing medical AI benchmarks lack process visibility, atomic skill evaluation, and integrated hallucination detection. We introduce MedBench v5, a redesigned benchmark for clinical multimodal models (language, vision-language, and agent systems) that moves from static QA to dynamic, process-oriented evaluation. MedBench v5 features: (1) a dual-dimensional framework combining Clinical Cognitive Responsiveness (14 sub-dimensions) and Medical Atomic Skills (4 agent environments), covering 63 tasks; (2) three switchable information-flow stressors (omission, contradiction, evidence delay) for factorized degradation analysis; (3) a dynamic process audit protocol with five reasoning nodes that produces model-specific failure fingerprints; (4) hallucination propagation monitoring across initiation, propagation, anchoring, and contradiction interaction-capturing silent hallucination. Experiments on frontier models show that strong overall task performance does not guarantee process stability: stressors mainly disrupt contradiction detection, diagnosis updating, hallucination propagation, and contradiction-based self-correction, while final evidence grounding can remain superficially stable. MedBench v5 provides a unified infrastructure for capability profiling, controllable stress testing, process auditing, and hallucination trajectory analysis in clinical AI evaluation.

</details>


### [76] [MMed-Bench-IR: A Heterogeneous Benchmark for Multilingual Medical Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24200)
*Junhyeok Lee, Han Jang, Hyeonjin Goh, Kyu Sung Choi*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MMed-Bench-IR 是一个多语言医学信息检索基准，覆盖6种语言和三个异构任务（跨语言医学QA检索、概念区分、多语言证据检索），评估了10个系统，发现生物医学编码器在英语上表现良好但在日语等语言上严重退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学检索基准仅孤立评估跨语言对齐、概念区分和证据检索中的某一项能力，缺乏同时衡量生物医学专业性与多语言覆盖度的综合评测，无法检测多语言场景下的检索退化问题。

Method: 构建了 MMed-Bench-IR 基准，包含三个异构任务：(1) 基于 UMLS 的跨语言医学QA检索（6,127条查询）；(2) 三级难度的概念区分（4,975个混淆集）；(3) 多语言证据检索（2,040条查询）。三个任务之间无概念和查询重叠，确保总分反映真实能力广度。评估了6个范式家族的10个系统。

Result: 生物医学编码器在英语上 nDCG@10 为0.818，但在日语上骤降至0.056，揭示了严重的跨语言失败。现有英语单语基准无法检测这一差距。

Conclusion: 多语言医学检索存在严重的跨语言性能退化，现有基准不足以评估这一挑战，MMed-Bench-IR 为多语言医学 RAG 提供了更全面的评测框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个多语言医学信息检索基准 MMed-Bench-IR，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）范畴。虽然该基准聚焦于检索而非视觉理解，但它是多语言医学场景下的跨模态检索评测基准，与 canonical 的 multimodal_benchmark 主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on multilingual medical retrieval, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: Multilingual medical retrieval, cross-lingual failure, biomedical encoders, RAG benchmark, low personal relevance

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in clinical settings increasingly requires multilingual retrieval against predominantly English evidence corpora. Multilingual medical retrieval demands three capabilities: cross-lingual alignment, concept discrimination, and evidence retrieval. However, existing benchmarks evaluate these only in isolation, leaving the interaction between biomedical expertise and multilingual coverage unmeasured. We introduce MMed-Bench-IR, a benchmark designed to disentangle these axes across 6 languages and three structurally heterogeneous tasks: (1) cross-lingual medical QA retrieval with 6,127 queries grounded in the Unified Medical Language System (UMLS), (2) concept discrimination over 4,975 confusion sets at three difficulty tiers, and (3) multilingual evidence retrieval for RAG with 2,040 quality-assured queries. The three tasks share zero concept and query overlap by design, ensuring that aggregate scores reflect genuine capability breadth. Evaluation of ten systems across six paradigm families reveals severe cross-lingual failure: biomedical encoders that score 0.818 nDCG@10 in English drop to 0.056 in Japanese, a gap that English-only benchmarks cannot detect.

</details>


### [77] [ReMMD: Realistic Multilingual Multi-Image Agentic Verification for Multimodal Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24112)
*Chenhao Dang, Dantong Zhu, Jun Yang, Conghui He, Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReMMD，一个面向真实场景的多语言多图像多模态虚假信息检测框架，包含 ReMMDBench 基准（500样本、2756张图像、5种语言、5类真实性标签）和 ReMMD-Agent 验证智能体（原子点分解、可复用证据集、结构化L1/L2/L3输出），在GPT-5.2上达到41.80%准确率和39.12% macro-F1，同时降低17.5%-79.9%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假信息检测基准和方法与真实场景严重不匹配：通常只处理短文本、单图像、二元标签或单一操纵来源，而真实网络帖子往往包含多语言长文本、多张图像、混合来源和微妙的图文框架错误。同时，智能体验证在真实证据搜索场景下成本高昂。

Method: ReMMD 包含两部分：1) ReMMDBench：从真实社交媒体收集500个样本、2756张图像，覆盖5种单语和2种跨语言设置、3种文本长度层级、多图像帖子、5类真实性标签、8类失真标签、证据来源和推理依据。2) ReMMD-Agent：一个具有持久记忆的验证智能体，将帖子分解为原子点，构建可复用的证据集，并预测结构化的L1（真实性）/L2（失真类型）/L3（证据来源）输出。

Result: 在多个闭源系统、开源LVLM、MMD-Agent和T2-Agent的对比中，ReMMD-Agent在5类真实性分类上取得最佳性能，使用GPT-5.2达到41.80%准确率和39.12% macro-F1，同时相比MMD-Agent降低17.5%成本，相比T2-Agent降低79.9%成本。

Conclusion: ReMMD填补了真实场景多模态虚假信息检测的空白，证明了多语言多图像设置的重要性，以及结构化智能体验证在准确性和成本效率上的优势。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 ReMMDBench，一个面向多模态虚假信息检测的真实世界多语言多图像评测基准，并提出了 ReMMD-Agent 验证框架。核心贡献是构建了新的多模态评测基准，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准" (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal misinformation detection, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Multimodal misinformation detection, Multi-image verification, Agentic verification, Cost reduction, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation detection is increasingly important because viral posts now combine long multilingual narratives, several images, mixed provenance, and subtle text--image framing errors. Existing benchmarks and methods remain poorly matched to this setting: they usually isolate short captions, single images, binary labels, or one manipulation source, while agentic verification remains costly under realistic evidence search. We present ReMMD, a realistic multilingual multi-image agentic verification framework for multimodal misinformation detection. ReMMD includes ReMMDBench, a real-world multimodal misinformation detection benchmark with 500 samples, 2,756 images, five monolingual languages, two cross-lingual settings, three text-length tiers, multi-image posts, five-way veracity labels, eight distortion labels, evidence provenance, and rationales. It also includes ReMMD-Agent, a persistent-memory verifier that decomposes posts into atomic points, builds a reusable evidence set, and predicts structured L1/L2/L3 outputs. Across proprietary systems, open LVLMs, MMD-Agent, and T2-Agent, ReMMD-Agent obtains the best five-way veracity performance, with 41.80% accuracy and 39.12% macro-F1 using GPT-5.2, while reducing cost by 17.5% relative to MMD-Agent and 79.9% relative to T2-Agent. The project is available at .

</details>

*Honglin Guo, Qi Zhang, Yu Zhang, Weijie Li, Rui Zheng, Zhikai Lei, Qiyuan Peng, Zhiheng Xi, Tao Gui, Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AGORA，一个基于真实工作文档的档案文档推理基准，包含 362 个问题、8 个领域共 9,664 份文档（3.72 亿 token），要求 agent 在远超上下文窗口的文档集合中定位稀疏证据、协调不一致的术语/单位/时间格式并计算答案。评测 8 个模型，最强模型仅达 59.4% 准确率，任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅覆盖档案文档推理的部分方面（如单文档 QA、检索），缺乏同时强调档案基础性（archive-grounded）、agentic 探索和跨领域覆盖的联合评测。随着 LLM 越来越多地被部署为在文档上进行推理的 agent，需要一个能真实反映这种复杂场景的评测基准。

Method: AGORA 基准构建采用 agentic pipeline：(1) 从 8 个领域收集 9,664 份真实工作文档（共 3.72 亿 token）；(2) 通过跨文档任务合成生成 362 个问题；(3) 应用防泄漏混淆处理；(4) 进行难度过滤。评测时让模型作为 agent 在文档集合中自主探索和推理。

Result: 评测 8 个模型（包括 GPT-4o、Claude 等），最强模型仅达 59.4% 准确率，且不同领域间表现差异显著。任务远未解决，表明当前 LLM 在档案文档推理方面仍有巨大提升空间。

Conclusion: AGORA 基准揭示了当前 LLM 在档案文档推理任务上的显著不足，为未来研究提供了有价值的评测平台和方向指引。该任务要求 agent 具备跨文档探索、信息协调和复杂推理能力，是 LLM 作为文档 agent 的重要能力评估。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 AGORA 基准，用于评测大语言模型在档案文档推理场景中的 agentic 能力，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然论文主要关注文本文档推理而非视觉多模态，但该子主题涵盖各类评测基准，且论文涉及跨域文档理解与推理，与多模态理解与生成方向中的评测任务高度相关。

Importance reason: Strong benchmark for agentic document reasoning but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: agentic document reasoning, cross-domain benchmark, far from solved, 59.4% accuracy, 372M tokens

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as agents that reason over documents rather than answer from parametric knowledge. We study archive-grounded reasoning: locating sparse evidence across a large, messy collection of workplace files, reconciling inconsistent terminology, units, and time conventions, and computing an answer. Existing benchmarks address only parts of this setting and none jointly stresses archive-groundedness, agentic exploration, and cross-domain coverage. We introduce Agora, a benchmark pairing 362 questions with eight domain collections of 9,664 authentic documents and 372M tokens, far exceeding any model's context window, so agents must explore deliberately rather than scan exhaustively. Agora is built by an agentic pipeline combining cross-document task synthesis, leakage-preventing obfuscation, and difficulty filtering. Evaluating eight models, we find the task far from solved: even the strongest reaches only 59.4% accuracy, with notable variation across domains.

</details>


### [79] [Age of LLM: A Strategic 1v1 Benchmark for Reasoning, Diplomacy and Reliability of Large Language Models under Fog of War](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24391)
*Arnaud Ricci*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.GT, cs.MA, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Age of LLM，一个基于回合制的1v1策略对战基准，用于评测LLM在迷雾战争、外交和可靠性压力下的推理、策略与可信度。15个推理模型在54场比赛中被评测，发现核武器rush主导战术、军事征服罕见但更快、外交活跃但几乎不达成、非法动作反映信念追踪能力，以及可靠性可能与获胜存在弱关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评测基准存在数据污染风险，且缺乏对LLM在对抗性不确定性（如迷雾战争、外交博弈、严格格式约束）下推理能力的系统评测。作者希望构建一个动态、私密、可复现的评测环境，以揭示LLM在战略推理、信念追踪和可靠性方面的真实能力。

Method: 设计了一个13x7网格上的回合制1v1策略游戏基准，包含迷雾战争、完整外交系统（消息、停火、最后通牒）和严格JSON格式约束。使用私有引擎、随机地图种子和随机对手来缓解数据污染。对15个推理模型进行了54场比赛（5,258个动作）的系统评测，分析了战术选择、外交行为、非法动作率和胜率等指标。

Result: (1) 核武器rush战术占主导（规则一致子集78%，全集85%）；(2) 军事征服罕见但更快（12.3回合 vs 18.9回合）；(3) 外交活跃但几乎从不达成协议；(4) ~58%的非法动作是迷雾/状态错误，非法动作率可作为信念追踪的度量；(5) 可靠性（格式合规）与获胜之间存在弱关联。

Conclusion: Age of LLM提供了一个独特的视角来观察LLM在对抗性不确定性下的推理行为，包括信念追踪、自发欺骗和每模型的认知"人格"。当前排名仅是初步描述性视图，但逐回合的动作和消息轨迹为未来研究LLM在复杂博弈环境中的推理提供了丰富的数据集和分析框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向LLM的1v1策略对战评测基准（Age of LLM），包含迷雾战争、外交、可靠性等维度，属于LLM在复杂对抗环境下的评测。虽然不直接涉及多模态，但该基准评测框架与"多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）在评测协议、基准设计上高度契合，且该方向是[CANONICAL]条目，优先复用。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for LLM reasoning under adversarial uncertainty, but focuses on game strategy rather than user's priority subtopics. Signals: LLM benchmark, fog of war, diplomacy, reliability, reasoning

Abstract: We introduce Age of LLM, a turn-based 1v1 benchmark in which two LLMs face off on a 13x7 grid to destroy the enemy base. Three stressors are deliberate: fog of war, full diplomacy (messages, ceasefires, ultimatums; uranium kept secret), and a reliability dimension where every turn must follow a strict JSON schema and an illegal action is silently discarded. The engine is private and each match uses a fresh random map seed and opponent, mitigating the data contamination that affects public benchmarks. Models receive a (near) rule-only prompt with no build-order advice (two tactical seed phrases were present during data collection; see Section 2.7). We benchmark 15 reasoning models across 54 matches and 5,258 actions. Findings: (1) the nuclear rush dominates (78% on the rules-coherent v0.11+ sub-corpus; 85% corpus-wide) with a sole-launcher signature that is largely mechanical under secret-simultaneous launch rules, not a cognitive deterrence failure; (2) military conquest is rare but faster (12.3 vs 18.9 turns); (3) diplomacy is prolific yet almost never consummated; (4) ~58% of illegal actions are fog/state errors, making the illegal-action rate a measure of belief-tracking; (5) -- the least established, and the only one we label exploratory -- a weak link associates reliability with winning. The corpus is small, unbalanced and not side-swapped, so the ranking is a preliminary descriptive view, not a contribution. Beyond ranking, the turn-by-turn traces of actions and messages make the corpus a lens on how LLMs reason under adversarial uncertainty -- their belief-tracking, spontaneous deception, and per-model cognitive "personas" -- which we frame as a future research direction. We release the replay format, an isometric viewer and all replays; engine source on request.

</details>


### [80] [EgoSAT: A Comprehensive Benchmark of Egocentric Streaming Interaction Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24422)
*Yijia Lei, Jinzhao Li, Yichi Zhang, Jiacheng Hua, Yin Li, Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: EgoSAT是首个面向第一视角（egocentric）视频流式交互理解的综合评测基准，包含1997个视频、约4800个QA对，统一了回顾推理、在线理解和前瞻预测三类任务，系统评估了多种VLM在流式设置下的表现，发现现有模型在前瞻和回顾建模上存在困难，且置信度校准严重不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一视角视频理解评测多聚焦于单一任务（如回顾性问答或未来预测），缺乏一个统一的流式交互理解基准来评估VLM在视频帧逐帧到达、模型需持续理解动态视觉上下文这一真实场景下的能力。

Method: 构建EgoSAT基准：从Ego4D等数据源筛选1997个第一视角视频（共165小时），设计约4800个QA对，将问题分为回顾推理（已完成事件）、在线理解（正在进行的活动）和前瞻预测（未来动作）三类。在流式设置下（仅可见已观测帧）评估多种开源和闭源VLM，并引入可回答性区分和置信度诊断分析。

Result: 现有VLM在流式交互理解中表现不佳，尤其在前瞻预测和回顾推理任务上困难显著；模型置信度与可回答性之间存在严重错位，出现大量"自信但错误"的行为。

Conclusion: EgoSAT揭示了当前VLM在流式第一视角视频理解中的关键短板，特别是置信度校准问题，为未来研究提供了重要的评测基准和分析方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了EgoSAT，一个面向第一视角视频流式交互理解的多模态评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题，因此直接复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Novel egocentric streaming benchmark but outside user's priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous/multimodal generation or world models. Signals: Egocentric video benchmark, Streaming interaction understanding, VLM evaluation, Confidence calibration, Retrospective/prospective reasoning

Abstract: We introduce EgoSAT, the first comprehensive benchmark for egocentric video reasoning in streaming settings, designed to evaluate the capabilities of modern vision-language models (VLMs). The benchmark targets streaming interaction understanding, where video frames arrive sequentially and models must continuously interpret evolving visual context. EgoSAT unifies several previously distinct tasks within a single streaming framework. In this formulation, queries about completed events correspond to retrospective reasoning, queries about ongoing activities require online understanding, and queries about future actions involve prospective anticipation. This unified setting requires models to reason about the past, present, and future while operating under the constraint that only previously observed frames are available. EgoSAT contains 1,997 unique videos spanning 165 hours of egocentric footage and around 4,800 high-quality question-answer pairs, carefully designed to probe reasoning across varying temporal contexts. Using this benchmark, we evaluate a diverse set of both open-weight and closed-weight VLMs, providing a systematic assessment of their ability for streaming interaction understanding. By distinguishing answerability and conducting diagnostics on confidence of models, we find existing models not only struggle with prospective and retrospective modeling, but also exhibit severe mis-calibration: confidence often fails to track inherent answerability, leading to dangerous "confidently wrong" behaviors. Project page:

</details>


### [81] [Legal Reasoning Is Not Lawyering: Rethinking Legal Benchmarks for Pro Se Access to Justice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23716)
*Andrew Lou, David Shin*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CY

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出当前法律AI基准仅评估模型在专家预处理输入上的表现（上限），而忽略了pro se诉讼人（无律师当事人）提供的含噪声、不完整、有错误的输入（下限）。作者通过连接pro se文献与机器学习中长上下文敏感、欠规范、幻觉、拼写扰动等退化现象，并在LEXam基准上进行小规模扰动实验，揭示了上下限之间的差距，呼吁构建能直接衡量模型在pro se类输入下鲁棒性的法律基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前法律AI基准评测假设LLM能改善司法可及性（access to justice），但这些基准的输入由法律专家预处理，仅衡量模型的上限性能。而真正需要司法帮助的pro se诉讼人（无律师当事人）提供的输入包含噪声叙事、隐藏事实、遗漏、民间法律假设和表面错误，模型在这些条件下的表现（下限）未被充分评估。作者希望揭示这一差距并推动更贴近实际场景的基准设计。

Method: 作者首先系统梳理pro se文献中关于非专业人士法律输入的特征（噪声、遗漏、错误等），并将其与机器学习文献中已知的LLM退化条件（长上下文敏感、欠规范、幻觉、拼写扰动）进行对应。然后在LEXam法律基准上设计小规模扰动实验，模拟pro se类输入退化，对比扰动前后的模型性能差异，以量化上下限之间的差距。

Result: 在LEXam基准上的小规模扰动实验表明，模型在模拟pro se类输入下的性能显著下降，验证了当前基准仅测量上限而忽略下限的问题。作者指出如果模型开发继续聚焦于仅测量上限的基准，这一差距可能被隐藏甚至扩大。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁法律AI社区设计能够直接衡量模型在pro se类输入下鲁棒性的基准，使关于法律AI改善司法可及性的主张变得可实证检验。当前基准范式需要从仅关注专家预处理输入的上限评估，转向同时关注真实用户输入下的下限评估。

Classification reason: 该论文讨论法律AI基准评测的局限性，提出当前基准仅衡量专家预处理输入的上限性能，而忽略了pro se诉讼人（非专业人士）输入下的鲁棒性。这属于多模态/LLM评测与基准设计范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on legal AI benchmarks, which is outside the user's core priority subtopics and lacks technical novelty in continuous/multimodal models or generation. Signals: Legal AI benchmark critique, pro se robustness gap, small perturbation experiment, not aligned with user's priority subtopics, no autoregressive or diffusion generation focus

Abstract: Legal AI benchmark research frequently invokes the assumption that large language models can improve access to justice, including for people who cannot access lawyers in order to understand and exercise their legal rights. We argue that current benchmarks are not equipped to support this assumption because they evaluate legal reasoning over inputs that have already been preprocessed by legal experts, which measures the upper bound of model performance. Access to justice depends on a lower bound: how models perform when inputs come from pro se litigants, whose prompts may contain noisy narratives, buried facts, omissions, folk-legal assumptions, and surface-level errors. These degradations are comparable to conditions under which LLMs are known to degrade in the general machine learning literature, including long-context sensitivity, underspecification, hallucination, and typographical perturbations. We connect evidence from pro se literature with this body of machine learning research and present a small perturbation experiment on LEXam, a legal benchmark, to illustrate the gap between these two bounds. If model development continues to focus on benchmarks that measure only the upper bound, this gap may remain hidden or even widen. We conclude by calling for legal benchmarks that directly measure robustness under pro se-like inputs so that access-to-justice claims about legal AI can become empirically testable.

</details>


### [82] [EG-VQA: Benchmarking Verifiable Video Question Answering with Grounded Temporal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24797)
*Linpeng Huang, Weixing Chen, Zexin Chen, Yang Liu, Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (57.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出EG-VQA基准，用于评估视频大语言模型在视频问答中的证据定位能力，并引入EG-F1指标联合衡量时间对齐与语义一致性。实验表明现有模型在答案正确性与证据定位之间存在显著差距，为此提出EG-Reasoner模型，通过显式证据监督训练，在开源模型中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VideoQA基准主要评估答案正确性，忽略了预测结果是否真正基于视频中的相关证据，导致答案生成与证据理解之间存在脱节。

Method: 构建EG-VQA基准（2067个视频、11838个QA对，含细粒度时间证据标注），提出EG-F1指标联合衡量时间对齐与语义一致性，并训练EG-Reasoner模型进行显式证据监督学习。

Result: 即使强闭源模型也难以准确定位证据；EG-Reasoner在开源模型中达到SOTA，在反事实等推理密集型任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 仅靠规模扩展不足以实现鲁棒视频理解，结构化证据监督对于开发更可靠、可解释的VideoQA系统至关重要。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了EG-VQA基准，包含2067个视频和11838个QA对，并引入EG-F1指标用于评估视频问答中的证据定位能力，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一[CANONICAL]子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on video QA evidence grounding, not directly on user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: VideoQA benchmark, evidence grounding, EG-F1 metric, open-source model, reasoning-intensive tasks

Abstract: Recent advances in Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have yielded promising performance on video question answering (VideoQA). Nevertheless, existing benchmarks are predominantly evaluated through answer correctness, while the grounding of predictions in relevant video evidence remains largely unexamined. This disconnect between answer generation and evidence understanding motivates the construction of the Evidence-Grounded Video Question Answering Benchmark (EG-VQA), an open-ended evaluation protocol in which each QA pair is explicitly annotated with supporting temporal evidence, thereby requiring joint reasoning and precise evidence localization. EG-VQA is comprised of 2,067 videos and 11,838 QA pairs with fine-grained evidence annotations. To evaluate predicted evidence, Evidence-Grounded F1 (EG-F1) is introduced as a unified metric in which temporal alignment and semantic consistency against ground-truth evidence are jointly measured. Experimental evaluation reveals that even strong proprietary models struggle to accurately ground their predictions, exposing a fundamental discrepancy between answer correctness and faithful evidence localization. To bridge this gap, EG-Reasoner, an evidence-grounded reasoning model trained with explicit supervision, is proposed. State-of-the-art performance is achieved among open-source models, with results competitive against proprietary systems, particularly pronounced gains are observed on reasoning-intensive tasks such as counterfactual questions. These findings demonstrate that scaling alone is insufficient for robust video understanding and that structured evidence supervision is essential for the development of more reliable and interpretable VideoQA systems.

</details>

### [83] [AVOC: Enhancing Hour-Level Audio-Video Understanding in Omni-Modal LLMs via Retrieval-Inspired Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24286)
*Yijing Chen, Wenhui Tan, Xiaoyi Yu, Yuyue Wang, Xin Cheng, Kaisi Guan, Hao Jiang, Xiangyang Li, Guojie Zhu, Ruihua Song*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: AVOC 提出一种检索启发的 token 压缩框架，通过相关性、重要性和多样性三个检索准则对音频-视频 token 进行压缩，使全模态大语言模型能够高效处理长达一小时的音视频输入，在多个长视频基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态大语言模型在短音视频理解上表现优异，但在长音视频理解中受限于有限的上下文窗口和严重的信息冗余，亟需有效的 token 压缩方法。

Method: AVOC 在模态编码器与 LLM 主干之间引入可学习的 token 压缩模块，将多模态 token 压缩重新定义为 top-K 检索问题。借鉴信息检索中的相关性、重要性和多样性三个经典准则，分别设计针对性机制，并整合为统一的检索式压缩流程。

Result: 在 OmniVideoBench 和 LVOmniBench 上分别超越第二名模型 4.9 和 5.5 个平均准确率点；在长达一小时的 Audio-Video Needle-in-a-Haystack 任务中保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: AVOC 通过检索启发的 token 压缩有效解决了长音视频理解中的上下文窗口和冗余问题，为全模态大语言模型处理长时序多模态输入提供了高效可行的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AVOC 框架，通过检索启发的 token 压缩模块解决长音频-视频理解中的上下文窗口和冗余问题，属于多模态大语言模型的高效化方法，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient multimodal token compression for long-form video understanding, aligning with user's priority on model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: token compression, long-form video understanding, retrieval-inspired, omni-modal LLM, efficient inference

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have achieved remarkable progress in short-form audio-video understanding, yet long-form audio-video comprehension remains challenged by limited context windows and severe information redundancy. To address these bottlenecks, we propose AVOC, a framework for long-form audio-video understanding in Omni-modal Large Language Models. AVOC introduces a learnable token compression module between the modality encoders and the LLM backbone. We reframe multimodal token compression as a top-$K$ retrieval problem: given a fixed context budget, the module must retrieve a compact subset of tokens that best supports answering the user query. We draw inspiration from three classical Information Retrieval criteria for selecting informative units from a large candidate pool: relevance, importance, and diversity. AVOC instantiates each criterion as a tailored mechanism for audio-video understanding, and integrates them into a unified retrieval-style compression pipeline. Experiments show that AVOC achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-form audio-video benchmarks, surpassing the second-best model by 4.9 and 5.5 points in average accuracy on OmniVideoBench and LVOmniBench, respectively. Moreover, AVOC maintains robust performance on Audio-Video Needle-in-a-Haystack task at durations up to one hour.

</details>


### [84] [Accelerating Multimodal Large Language Models with Prior-Corrected Token Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24156)
*Zengjie Chen, Yuxiang Cai, Jingcai Guo, Taotao Cai, Jianwei Yin, Zhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (85.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Prior-Corrected Token Reduction (PriorTR)，一种无需训练的视觉 token 缩减方法，通过分离模型固有先验与任务条件注意力来避免关键 token 被误剪枝，在保持精度的同时加速 MLLM 推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力分数的视觉 token 剪枝方法存在缺陷：注意力分数往往被模型固有的先验（即使没有文本指令，模型也会关注某些与任务无关的区域）所主导，导致任务条件 token 的注意力被抑制，从而在剪枝时被错误丢弃，影响模型性能。

Method: 提出 PriorTR，通过引入一个空 token（null token）作为与指令无关的探针，在单次前向传播中同时估计模型先验注意力图和任务条件注意力分布，然后通过对比两者来度量每个视觉 token 的额外可用信息量，据此进行 token 剪枝。

Result: 在多个多模态基准和多种 MLLM 上的实验表明，PriorTR 在精度与效率的权衡上持续优于现有的无训练基线方法，尤其在激进的 token 预算下优势更为明显。

Conclusion: PriorTR 通过显式分离模型先验与任务条件注意力，有效缓解了注意力主导先验导致的误剪枝问题，为 MLLM 的 token 缩减提供了一种更精确、无需训练的高效方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PriorTR，一种无需训练的视觉 token 缩减方法，旨在加速多模态大模型（MLLM）的推理效率。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题的范畴，具体涉及 token 压缩与稀疏推理。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in MLLMs, a high-priority subtopic, with a novel training-free token reduction method. Signals: training-free token reduction, MLLM acceleration, prior-corrected attention, strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off, matches efficient inference priority

Abstract: Visual token reduction has emerged as an effective strategy for accelerating Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Many existing methods prune tokens by ranking text-visual attention scores. However, we show that attention is often dominated by a model-induced prior: even without textual instruction, MLLMs tend to focus on certain task-agnostic regions. Consequently, the attention scores of instruction-conditioned tokens are suppressed, increasing the risk that these tokens are discarded during pruning. To address this issue, we propose Prior-Corrected Token Reduction (PriorTR), a training-free token reduction method that explicitly separates task-conditioned attention from the model-induced prior. PriorTR estimates the attention map of the prior, and contrasts it with the task-conditioned attention distribution to measure the additional usable information contributed by each visual token. Importantly, PriorTR computes both the model-induced prior and the task-conditioned posterior within a single forward pass by introducing a null token that serves as an instruction-agnostic probe in the attention block. This design avoids duplicated propagation. Extensive experiments across multiple multimodal benchmarks and MLLMs demonstrate that PriorTR consistently improves the trade-off between accuracy and efficiency over strong training-free baselines, particularly under aggressive token budgets.

</details>


### [85] [Spectral Evolution-Guided Token Pruning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24165)
*Bin Chen, Yuxiang Cai, Yadan Luo, Yi Zhang, Jianwei Yin, Zhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (85.2) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLSE（Cross-Layer Spectral Evolution），一种训练无关的 token 剪枝框架，通过分析视觉 token 在 Transformer 层间的频谱演化来评估其重要性，从而在 MLLM 中高效减少冗余视觉 token，在图像和视频基准上实现了效率与精度的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM token 剪枝方法通常依赖单层信号（如注意力分数或 token 相似度），忽略了视觉表征在跨层变换中的演化，且在多模态 token 序列中可能存在位置偏差。作者希望利用跨层频谱演化来更稳定地衡量 token 重要性，从而在不牺牲推理性能的前提下加速 MLLM。

Method: 提出 Cross-Layer Spectral Evolution (CLSE) 框架：通过分析视觉 token 表示在 Transformer 各层之间的频域演化（从高频结构细节到低频语义抽象的转变），量化 token 的频谱重分布程度。频谱重分布越强的 token 被认为语义越活跃，应予以保留。该方法无需训练，直接应用于预训练 MLLM 的推理阶段。

Result: 在多个图像和视频基准上，CLSE 在激进 token 缩减下实现了优于现有方法的效率-精度权衡。具体地，CLSE 减少了 FLOPs、KV 缓存内存和延迟，同时保持或提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: 跨层频谱演化是一种有效的 token 重要性度量方式，能够缓解位置偏差，为 MLLM 的高效推理提供了一种训练无关的 token 剪枝方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于跨层频谱演化的训练无关 token 剪枝框架，旨在减少 MLLM 中的视觉 token 冗余以加速推理，属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient inference in MLLMs via training-free token pruning, aligning with user's model efficiency priority and boosting efficient inference keyword. Signals: training-free token pruning, cross-layer spectral evolution, MLLM efficiency, KV cache reduction, multimodal understanding generation

Abstract: Reducing visual token redundancy is critical for accelerating Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) without degrading cross-modal reasoning performance. Existing token pruning methods typically rely on single-layer signals, such as attention scores or token similarities, which overlook the cross-layer transformation of visual representations and may exhibit positional bias in multimodal token sequences. To address this limitation, we propose a training-free token pruning framework based on Cross-Layer Spectral Evolution (CLSE). Instead of measuring token importance from single-layer feature magnitudes, CLSE quantifies how token representations evolve across Transformer layers in the frequency domain. This evolution reflects the transition from high-frequency structural details to low-frequency semantic abstractions. We observe that tokens with stronger spectral redistribution across layers are more likely to be semantically active and should therefore be preserved. By modeling cross-layer token dynamics, CLSE provides a stable importance criterion that mitigates positional bias. Extensive experiments on both image and video benchmarks demonstrate that CLSE achieves a superior trade-off between efficiency and accuracy under aggressive token reduction. Across multiple MLLMs, CLSE reduces FLOPs, KV cache memory, and latency while maintaining competitive or improved performance.

</details>


### [86] [Towards Fast and Effective Long Video Understanding of Multimodal Large Language Models via Adaptive Quasi-Gaussian Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24187)
*Kun Zhang, Chenxin Fang, Tao Chen, Baiyang Song, Yunhang Shen, Yiyi Zhou, Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 AdaQ，一种无需训练的自适应准高斯采样方法，通过为不同查询自适应选择最优 3-σ 区间来高效选取关键帧，显著降低 MLLM 长视频理解的计算开销并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解中 MLLM 面临巨大的计算和内存开销，现有关键帧选择方法（硬采样）灵活性和鲁棒性不足，且对噪声敏感。

Method: 受高斯分布 3-σ 规则启发，将视频帧选择定义为准高斯采样问题，提出 AdaQ 方法：对局部查询使用较小 3-σ 区间（聚焦关键细节），对全局查询使用较大 3-σ 区间（覆盖全局信息），实现自适应、无训练的关键帧采样。

Result: 在四种 MLLM 和三种嵌入模型上的实验表明，AdaQ 显著优于默认 MLLM 和 SOTA 关键帧选择方法。例如，仅用 64 帧即可使 Qwen3-VL-8B 平均超越 GPT4o 15.8%，且仅需设置 1 个超参数，鲁棒性强。

Conclusion: AdaQ 是一种高效、鲁棒且无需训练的长视频关键帧采样方法，能有效提升 MLLM 在长视频理解任务上的性能与效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AdaQ 自适应准高斯采样方法，用于高效选择关键帧以降低 MLLM 处理长视频的计算开销，属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）范畴，因此复用该 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Proposes a training-free adaptive keyframe sampling method for efficient long video understanding in MLLMs, aligning with user's priority on model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: Training-free keyframe selection, Adaptive quasi-Gaussian sampling, Long video MLLM efficiency, Strong performance with few frames, Single hyperparameter simplicity

Abstract: Long video understanding remains a daunting challenge for \emph{Multimodal Large Language Models} (MLLMs) due to the excessive computation and memory footprint. Thus, \emph{keyframe selection} is often adopted to mitigate this shortcoming, which however still suffers from low flexibility and high noise due to its hard sampling principle. In this paper, we define video frame selection as a problem of \emph{Quasi-Gaussian Sampling}, and propose an adaptive and training-free approach termed \textbf{\emph{AdaQ}}. Inspired by the $3$-$\sigma$ rule of Gaussian distribution, the objective of AdaQ is to achieve the optimal $3$-$\sigma$ interval for different examples, \emph{i.e.}, a smaller $3$-$\sigma$ interval for the local query and a larger one for the global query, thereby facilitating robust and adaptive frame sampling. To validate AdaQ, we apply it to four MLLMs with three embedding models. The extensive experimental results not only show its obvious performance gains over the default MLLMs and the SOTA keyframe selection methods, \emph{e.g.}, helping Qwen3-VL-8B outperform GPT4o by 15.8\% on average by using only 64 frames, but also confirm its superior robustness and high efficiency for long-video understanding, \emph{e.g.}, \textbf{only 1 hyper-parameter} needs to be set. \textbf{Our code project} is given at \href{ }{ }.

</details>

### [87] [ParaPairAudioBench: Paralinguistic Pairwise Audio Benchmark for LALM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24648)
*Jisu Jeon, Seungyeon Jwa, Joosung Lee, Jinhyeon Kim, Woojin Chung, Hwiyeol Jo, Jeonghoon Kim, Jonghyun Choi, Soyoon Kim*

Main category: eess.AS, cs.SD, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ParaPairAudioBench是一个包含5,175对音频样本的成对评测基准，覆盖风格、语速、重音、年龄和性别五个副语言维度，用于评估大型音频语言模型（LALM）作为裁判模型在细粒度副语言区分上的可靠性。实验表明当前LALM裁判平均落后人类判断32%，且在平局（Tie）情况下存在严重的校准失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LALM裁判模型主要关注整体自然度评估，忽略了细粒度的副语言（paralinguistic）特征区分能力。缺乏一个系统性的、多维度的基准来评估LALM在副语言属性上的判断可靠性。

Method: 构建了包含5,175对音频样本的成对评测基准，覆盖风格、语速、重音、年龄和性别五个维度。设计了同文本（same-transcript）和跨文本（cross-transcript）两种条件，以分离词汇与声学线索的影响。采用成对比较范式，包含平局（Tie）选项以评估校准能力。

Result: 当前LALM裁判模型在副语言维度上平均落后人类判断32个百分点，尤其在平局情况下校准失败严重。不同模型在不同维度上表现差异显著，且同文本与跨文本条件下的表现揭示了模型对词汇与声学线索的不同依赖程度。

Conclusion: ParaPairAudioBench为LALM-as-a-Judge提供了多维度的、校准感知的评估框架，揭示了现有模型在细粒度副语言判断上的显著不足，为未来改进LALM的音频理解与评估能力指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向语音副语言属性的成对音频评测基准（ParaPairAudioBench），属于多模态（音频-语言）生成模型的自动评测。该论文属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"生成模型评测与数据集"（generation_benchmark）子主题，该子主题为CANONICAL条目，涵盖生成模型评测基准、新数据集与自动评测指标。

Importance reason: Focuses on LALM-as-a-judge for paralinguistic audio, which is outside the user's core priorities in continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: Paralinguistic audio benchmark, LALM-as-a-judge, calibration failures, same/cross-transcript conditions, not aligned with user's priority subtopics

Abstract: Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have been widely used as judge models for the automatic evaluation of generated speech. However, prior approaches predominantly focus on holistic naturalness, leaving fine-grained paralinguistic distinctions underexplored. We introduce ParaPairAudioBench, a pairwise benchmark of 5,175 audio pairs across five paralinguistic dimensions: Style, Rate, Emphasis, Age, and Gender. Our experiments show that current LALM judges still lag behind human judgments by 32%p on average and exhibit severe calibration failures, particularly in Tie cases where the correct decision is to abstain. To further analyze lexical versus acoustic reliance, the benchmark includes both same-transcript and cross-transcript conditions. ParaPairAudioBench enables multi-dimensional, calibration-aware assessment of the reliability of LALM-as-a-Judge for paralinguistic speech evaluation.

</details>

### [88] [Agentic Collaborative Cognition for Zero-Shot 3D Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24649)
*Wenxin Wang, Bo Zhang, Feng Chen, Zixuan Wang, Wen Li, Changsheng Li, Yinjie Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种协作式多智能体框架，通过规划智能体（Planning Agent）进行视角规划与补充，感知智能体（Perception Agent）构建结构化认知地图，两者闭环迭代实现零样本3D场景理解，在6个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的零样本3D理解方法将3D场景转化为视频关键帧，受限于视频的有限观测视角和对3D场景的隐式感知，存在固有瓶颈。

Method: 提出协作多智能体框架：1) Planning Agent负责高层视角规划，分析认知地图确定查询相关视角并补充缺失关键视角；2) Perception Agent从这些视角中记录物体级属性，通过跨视角一致实例标识符将碎片化观测整合为结构化认知地图，同时提供反馈过滤不匹配候选物体并指导后续视角规划。两智能体通过闭环迭代协作，直到收集足够信息完成任务。

Result: 在6个基准上达到SOTA：ScanRefer上Acc@0.5提升11.1%，3D辅助对话上BLEU-1提升14.6，SQA3D上EM提升2.1。

Conclusion: 协作多智能体框架通过显式视角规划与结构化认知地图构建，有效克服了视频有限视角和隐式感知的瓶颈，显著提升了零样本3D场景理解性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究零样本3D场景理解，使用多智能体协作框架进行3D视觉定位与问答，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的"空间与3D多模态推理"（spatial_3d_reasoning）规范子主题。

Importance reason: Novel multi-agent framework for zero-shot 3D understanding, but outside core priority subtopics. Signals: Multi-agent collaboration, Zero-shot 3D understanding, Cognitive map, Viewpoint planning, SOTA on benchmarks

Abstract: Recent advancements have explored agentic zero-shot 3D understanding by reformulating it as video keyframe understanding with Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, existing methods face an intrinsic bottleneck due to the finite observation perspectives inherent in videos and the implicit perception of 3D scenes. In this paper, we propose a collaborative multi-agent framework that assigns a Planning Agent to handle high-level viewpoint planning and supplement novel perspectives, and a Perception Agent to explicitly summarize the 3D scene into a structured holistic cognitive map. Specifically, Planning Agent first analyzes this cognitive map to determine query-relevant viewpoints and supplements missing critical perspectives to ensure comprehensive observation. Subsequently, Perception Agent documents object-level attributes from these views by assigning consistent instance identifiers across viewpoints, thereby integrating fragmented observations into the holistic cognitive map. In parallel, it provides feedback to filter out mismatched candidate objects and guide subsequent viewpoint planning. Through this closed-loop iterative process, two agents collaboratively figure out candidates until Perception Agent determines that sufficient information has been captured to complete the task. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on 6 benchmarks, with improvements of 11.1\% Acc@0.5 on ScanRefer, 14.6 BLEU-1 on 3D-assisted dialog, and 2.1 EM on SQA3D.

</details>

### [89] [ABACUS: Adapting Unified Foundation Model for Bridging Image Count Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23835)
*Anindya Mondal, Sauradip Nag, Anjan Dutta*

Main category: eess.IV, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ABACUS 是一个基于 3B 统一基础模型适配的统一视觉语言模型，通过密度感知自适应缩放、边界感知计数策略和循环一致性 GRPO 三大创新，在无需基准特定训练的情况下，同时在目标计数理解（目标计数、人群计数、指代表达计数）和计数忠实图像生成任务上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在计数任务上表现不佳，且理解和生成之间存在差距——理解模型难以准确计数，生成模型难以生成指定数量的物体。作者希望构建一个无需基准特定训练的统一模型，同时解决计数理解和计数忠实生成问题。

Method: 基于 3B 统一基础模型（如 Show-o），提出三项创新：1）密度感知自适应缩放（Density-Aware Adaptive Zooming with Objectness Maps），通过密度图引导的缩放和显著性图实现空间定位；2）边界感知计数策略（Boundary-Aware Count Policy via GRPO），消除裁剪边界误差；3）循环一致性 GRPO 策略（Cycle-Consistent GRPO），让理解分支自我批评生成输出，弥合理解-生成差距，无需外部标注。

Result: 在七个基准上达到 SOTA，包括 FSC-147、CARPK、CrowdHuman、QNRF、RefCOCO 等计数理解任务，以及计数条件图像生成任务，超越了任务专用模型和更大的通用模型。

Conclusion: ABACUS 证明了通过精心设计的适配策略（自适应缩放、边界感知、循环一致性 GRPO），统一基础模型可以同时胜任计数理解和计数忠实生成，无需任务特定训练，为统一多模态模型在细粒度视觉任务上的应用提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出ABACUS，一个统一视觉语言模型，同时处理目标计数理解（VQA类任务）和计数忠实图像生成（生成任务），属于理解与生成一体化模型。该论文与现有 subtopic "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）高度匹配，该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 和 multimodal_generation 两个方向下均有存在，因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with unified multimodal model and continuous visual token priorities, with novel GRPO-based counting and generation bridging. Signals: unified multimodal model, continuous visual token, autoregressive generation, efficient inference, controllable generation

Abstract: ABACUS is a unified vision-language model that handles object counting, crowd counting, referring-expression counting, and count-faithful image generation without any benchmark-specific training required. Our model is built on existing 3B-parameter unified foundation model and is adapted for object localization tasks using three key innovations: density-aware adaptive zooming with objectness maps for spatial grounding; a boundary-aware count policy via GRPO to eliminate crop-boundary errors; and a cycle-consistent GRPO strategy where the understanding branch self-critiques generated outputs, closing the understanding-generation gap without any external annotations. ABACUS achieves state-of-the-art results across seven benchmarks, outperforming both task-specific specialists and larger generalist models.

</details>


### [90] [UniTranslator: A Unified Multi-modal Framework for End-to-end In-Image Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24333)
*Jiahao Lyu, Pei Fu, Zhenhang Li, Shaojie Zhang, Jiahui Yang, Yu Zhou, Can Ma, Zhenbo Luo, Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (83.9) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniTranslator 是一个统一多模态框架，用于端到端图像内机器翻译（IIMT），通过理解-生成对齐模块（UGAM）和空间掩码解码器（SMD）解决理解-生成冲突和空间位置错位问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型直接应用于图像内机器翻译时存在两个关键问题：(1) 理解-生成冲突——理解阶段推断的翻译与生成阶段使用的文本监督不一致；(2) 空间位置错位——渲染的文本不能准确匹配目标文本区域。因此需要专门设计统一框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出UniTranslator统一多模态框架，包含：(1) Understand-Generation Alignment Module (UGAM)——桥接理解与生成之间的表征差距，促进翻译内容预测与文本渲染之间的语义一致性；(2) Spatial Mask Decoder (SMD)——在文本区域上施加像素级监督，提升空间定位、几何对齐和布局可控性。

Result: 在多个基准上，UniTranslator在多种语言方向和复杂真实世界布局下均达到SOTA性能。实验还揭示了翻译理解与图像生成之间存在强烈的相互增强效应。

Conclusion: UniTranslator通过统一多模态框架有效解决了图像内机器翻译中的理解-生成冲突和空间错位问题，验证了统一翻译多模态学习的优势，为端到端IIMT提供了新的有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出UniTranslator，一个统一多模态框架用于端到端图像内机器翻译，将视觉文本理解与图像生成紧密结合。这属于"统一多模态模型"（理解与生成一体化）范畴，该subtopic在multimodal_understanding_generation和multimodal_generation两个方向下均有存在，优先复用。

Importance reason: Strong technical contribution for IIMT but only tangentially touches priority subtopics like unified multimodal models without core focus on continuous representations or autoregressive generation. Signals: Unified multimodal framework, Understanding-generation alignment, Spatial mask decoder, End-to-end IIMT, SOTA on benchmarks

Abstract: In-Image Machine Translation (IIMT) aims to translate scene text in an image and render the translated text back into the original regions while preserving the overall visual appearance. Recent unified multimodal models provide a promising solution by combining visual-text understanding and image generation within a single framework. However, directly adapting such models to IIMT remains challenging. In particular, they often suffer from understanding-generation conflicts, where the translation inferred during understanding is inconsistent with the text supervision used in generation, and spatial position misalignment, where the rendered text does not accurately match the target text regions. To address these issues, we present UniTranslator, a unified multimodal framework for IIMT that tightly couples translation understanding and text editing. Specifically, we introduce an Understand-Generation Alignment Module (UGAM) to bridge the representation gap between understanding and generation, encouraging semantic consistency between translated content prediction and text rendering. We further propose a Spatial Mask Decoder (SMD) with pixel-level supervision over text regions to improve spatial grounding, geometric alignment, and layout controllability during generation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that UniTranslator achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse language directions and complex real-world layouts. Moreover, our results reveal a strong mutual reinforcement effect between translation understanding and image generation, highlighting the advantage of unified translation multimodal learning. Code is available at .

</details>


### [91] [S1-Omni-Image: A Unified Model for Scientific Image Understanding, Generation, and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24441)
*Qingxiao Li, Zikai Wang, Qingli Wang, Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: S1-Omni-Image 是一个面向科学图像的开源统一多模态模型，基于 S1-VL-32B 推理骨干，采用 think-before-generate 范式，先产生推理轨迹和任务特殊 token，再注入生成模块进行图像生成或编辑。支持科学插图生成、文本渲染、多轮编辑、医学/地理图像分割、医学图像翻译和超分辨率等任务，在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用图像生成模型缺乏对科学语义、结构关系、领域知识和任务意图的鲁棒理解能力，而科学图像任务需要同时具备高保真合成和科学语义理解能力。现有模型难以在统一框架中同时支持科学图像的理解、生成和编辑。

Method: 基于 S1-VL-32B 科学多模态推理骨干，耦合图像生成模块。用户指令输入后，模型首先生成任务导向的推理轨迹、文本答案和任务特殊 token；这些隐藏状态被注入生成模块以条件化图像生成或编辑。构建了 314K 样本的 SciGenEdit 训练数据集。

Result: 在 GenExam 和 TechImage-Bench 上优于开源模型；在 MSD、cigRockSEM、SynthRAD2025 和 IXI 四个编辑基准上达到 SOTA；在科学图像理解评测上保持稳定性能。

Conclusion: S1-Omni-Image 证明了 think-before-generate 范式在科学图像领域统一理解、生成与编辑的有效性，为科学多模态任务提供了一个开源统一框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 S1-Omni-Image，一个统一科学图像理解、生成与编辑的多模态模型，采用 think-before-generate 范式将理解与生成耦合。这属于"统一多模态模型"（理解与生成一体化）范畴，该 subtopic 在 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下已存在（非 canonical 但已建立），且与 multimodal_generation 方向也相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on scientific domain rather than general continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: unified multimodal model, think-before-generate, scientific image tasks, not aligned with priority subtopics, no autoregressive or video generation

Abstract: We present S1-Omni-Image, an open-weight unified multimodal model for scientific image understanding, generation, and editing. Unlike general-purpose image generation models, scientific image tasks require not only high-fidelity synthesis, but also robust understanding of scientific semantics, structural relations, domain knowledge, and task intent. To this end, S1-Omni-Image builds on the scientific multimodal reasoning backbone S1-VL-32B and couples its understanding capability with an image generation module under a unified think-before-generate paradigm. Given a user instruction, the model first produces a task-oriented reasoning trace, a textual answer, and a task special token; their hidden states are then injected into the generation module to condition image generation or editing. S1-Omni-Image supports scientific image understanding, generation, and editing in a unified framework. For generation, it focuses on scientific illustrations and text rendering, including logical diagrams, relational comparisons, data charts, and realistic scientific visualizations. For editing, it casts segmentation and other domain-specific vision tasks as native image editing problems, enabling multi-turn illustration editing, medical and geographic image segmentation, medical image translation, and scientific image super-resolution. We construct SciGenEdit, a 314K-sample training dataset, and release the model weights, inference code, and SciGenEdit-10K. Experiments show that S1-Omni-Image substantially improves scientific image generation and editing while preserving the scientific image understanding capability inherited from S1-VL-32B. It outperforms open-source models on GenExam and TechImage-Bench, achieves state-of-the-art results on four editing benchmarks including MSD, cigRockSEM, SynthRAD2025, and IXI, and maintains stable performance on scientific image understanding evaluations.

</details>
