### [92] [Vision-Language Model Reasoning for Contextual Semantic Mapping in Intralogistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24814)
*Marvin Rüdt, Hao Pang, Constantin Enke, Zäzilia Seibold, Kai Furmans*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (65.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上下文语义地图构建流程，结合SLAM几何建图、SAM实例分割、实例聚类和VLM多视角推理，在零样本开放词汇设置下推断物体可移动性等上下文属性，生成包含几何结构、物体类别和可移动性的语义地图。在物流环境中达到98.93%语义分类mIoU和89.17%可移动性估计mAcc。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主移动机器人在物流环境中依赖几何地图进行定位和导航，但缺乏对物体及其上下文属性（如可移动性）的语义理解，限制了其在动态环境中的鲁棒导航能力。

Method: 提出一个上下文语义地图构建流程：(1) 使用SLAM进行几何建图；(2) SAM进行实例分割；(3) 跨多视角聚类实例；(4) 使用VLM（零样本、开放词汇）对聚类后的实例进行多视角推理，推断物体类别和可移动性等上下文属性。

Result: 语义分类mIoU达98.93%，物体可移动性估计mAcc达89.17%。组件分析表明VLM推理是上下文理解的主要瓶颈，实例聚类是全景性能的主要限制。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义地图支持上下文感知过滤和动态物流环境中的鲁棒导航，验证了VLM在机器人语义建图中的有效性，并指出了VLM推理和实例聚类是当前流程的主要瓶颈。

Classification reason: 该论文使用VLM进行多视角推理以构建语义地图，属于视觉语言模型在机器人场景中的应用，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics and logistics, not on core continuous language/multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Robotics, SLAM, VLM reasoning, Semantic mapping, Intralogistics

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots operating in intralogistics environments rely on geometric maps for localization and navigation, but lack semantic understanding of objects and their contextual properties. We present a contextual semantic mapping pipeline that combines SLAM-based geometric mapping, SAM-based instance segmentation, instance clustering, and VLM multi-view reasoning to produce a contextual semantic map representation encoding geometric structure, object class, and object movability. By aggregating observations across multiple viewpoints and querying a VLM in a zero-shot, open-vocabulary setting, the pipeline infers contextual object properties--here demonstrated through movability--without requiring task-specific training or predefined object categories. We evaluate three VLMs under two prompting strategies and conduct a component-wise analysis of the pipeline. The proposed pipeline achieves 98.93 % mIoU for semantic classification and 89.17 % mAcc for object movability estimation. Component analysis identifies VLM reasoning as the primary bottleneck for contextual understanding and instance clustering as the main limitation for panoptic performance. The resulting semantic map supports context-aware filtering and robust navigation in dynamic intralogistics environments.

</details>


### [93] [DriveStack-VLA: Render-Teacher Alignment for BEV-Based DeepStack Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24051)
*Jingke Wang, Zhenru Zhao, Shuangming Lei, Hao Su, Yuehao Huang, Yijia Xie, Kai Tang, Guanglin Xu, AiXue Ye, Yukai Ma, Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DriveStack-VLA 提出了一种基于BEV表示的视觉-语言-动作驾驶模型，通过DeepStack连接将BEV注入LLM解码器，并引入Render-Teacher Alignment对齐真实图像与栅格化图像的感知焦点，以及基于头的自批判模块优化轨迹选择，在多个驾驶基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶模型缺乏驾驶导向的空间智能，其策略主要基于透视图像token和语言先验，缺乏度量几何、俯视场景结构和安全关键感知线索，导致在专家演示中弱视觉几何建模和感知覆盖不足。

Method: 1) 构建双视觉建模组件：通过DeepStack式连接将BEV表示注入LLM解码器；2) 提出Render-Teacher Alignment，对齐真实图像与栅格化图像的感知焦点；3) 引入基于头的自批判模块，对采样轨迹进行排序并有条件地优化最佳轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIMv1上达到91.6 PDMS，NAVSIMv2上达到91.0 EPDMS（启用人类惩罚过滤），在闭环Bench2Drive上驾驶得分为79.49，成功率为56.36%。

Conclusion: DriveStack-VLA通过BEV注入和对齐机制有效增强了VLA驾驶模型的空间感知能力，在多个驾驶基准上取得了领先性能，验证了将结构化空间表示融入VLM驾驶策略的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文将预训练VLM转化为驾驶策略（VLA驾驶模型），属于视觉语言模型在自动驾驶领域的应用，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on driving-specific VLA with BEV and alignment, not on core continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: BEV-based VLA driving model, Render-Teacher Alignment, DeepStack connection, self-critique trajectory selection, closed-loop driving benchmarks

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action driving models convert a pretrained Vision-Language Model into a driving policy, allowing them to use world knowledge and follow language guidances. However, existing VLA driving models still lack driving-oriented spatial intelligence: their policies are mainly grounded on perspective image tokens and language priors, while precise motion planning requires metric geometry, top-down scene structure, and attention to safety-critical perceptual cues. This limitation makes current models vulnerable to weak visual geometry modeling and perceptual coverage in expert demonstrations. In this paper, we present DriveStack-VLA, a framework built upon a large VLM backbone. To strengthen the spatial grounding of VLA driving, we develop dual visual modeling components. We inject a Bird-Eye-View representation into the Large Language Model decoder through a DeepStack-style connection, and propose Render-Teacher Alignment to align the perceptual focus of real images with that of rasterized images. Furthermore, to bridge the gap in multimodal trajectory selection, we introduce a head-based self-critique module that ranks sampled trajectories and conditionally refines the best one. DriveStack-VLA achieves 91.6 PDMS on NAVSIMv1, 91.0 EPDMS on NAVSIMv2 (with the human penalty filter enabled), and a driving score of 79.49 with a success rate of 56.36\% on the closed-loop Bench2Drive. More visualizations are available on our project page: .

</details>


### [94] [BioMedVR: Confusion-Aware Mixture-of-Prompt Experts for Biomedical Visual Reprogramming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24740)
*Jiaxiang Liu, Tianxiang Hu, Juwei Guan, Yujie Wu, Yusong Wang, Yao Mu, Zuozhu Liu, Mingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: BioMedVR 提出首个基于 Visual Reprogramming 的生物医学图像适配框架，通过混淆最小化机制和混合提示专家模块，在少量样本下高效适配预训练 VLM，在18个数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练 VLM（如 CLIP）适配到生物医学图像领域面临挑战：全模型微调计算成本高，医学数据稀缺且类别间差异细微。现有 Visual Reprogramming 方法仅关注正类提示而忽略混淆负类，导致细粒度医学场景中预测校准不佳。

Method: 提出 BioMedVR 框架，包含：(1) 混淆最小化机制，利用 LLM 生成混淆感知属性并结合混淆抑制损失减少假阳性对齐；(2) 混合提示专家模块，包含正类专家（主类判别）和负类专家（混淆抑制），通过自适应门控平衡两者。

Result: 在18个数据集（11个生物医学 + 7个自然图像基准）上，BioMedVR 在准确率和泛化性上均优于现有方法，有效桥接了 VR 与 VLM 在生物医学领域的应用。

Conclusion: BioMedVR 是首个将 Visual Reprogramming 应用于生物医学图像的框架，通过显式处理类别混淆问题，实现了预训练 VLM 在医学领域的少样本高效适配，为参数高效迁移学习提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究如何将预训练VLM（如CLIP）通过Visual Reprogramming适配到生物医学图像领域，属于视觉语言模型的参数高效适配方法，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on biomedical visual reprogramming, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: biomedical imaging, visual reprogramming, confusion-aware, mixture-of-prompt experts, few-shot adaptation

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP have demonstrated strong generalization across natural-image domains. However, adapting these models to biomedical imaging is non-trivial: full-model fine-tuning is computationally expensive, while medical data are often scarce and exhibit subtle, fine-grained inter-class differences, making parameter-efficient adaptation particularly critical. Visual Reprogramming (VR) offers a parameter-efficient alternative by injecting learnable perturbations into the input space, but existing VR approaches for VLMs mainly focus on positive class prompts and overlook confusing negatives, leading to miscalibrated predictions in fine-grained medical scenarios. We present BioMedVR, the first VR-based framework for biomedical imaging, enabling few-shot adaptation of pretrained VLMs through compact learnable VR modules. To mitigate class confusion, we introduce a Confusion Minimization Mechanism that leverages LLM-generated confusion-aware attributes together with a Confusion-Suppression Loss to explicitly reduce false-positive alignment. Moreover, the designed Mixture-of-Prompt Experts combines a positive expert for main-class discrimination and a negative expert for confusion suppression, balanced via adaptive gating. Extensive experiments on 18 datasets, including 11 biomedical datasets and 7 natural image benchmarks, demonstrate that BioMedVR achieves superior accuracy and generalization, effectively bridging VR and VLMs in biomedical domains.

</details>


### [95] [UniDrive: A Unified Vision-Language and Grounding Framework for Interpretable Risk Understanding in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24759)
*Xiaowei Gao, Pengxiang Li, Yitai Cheng, Ruihan Xu, James Haworth, Stephen Law, Yun Ye*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.81) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniDrive 是一个统一的视觉语言与定位框架，结合时序推理分支（多帧视觉输入建模场景动态）和高分辨率感知分支（保留最新帧的细粒度空间细节），通过门控交叉注意力融合模块整合两者，联合生成自然语言风险描述和风险目标的边界框输出。在 DRAMA-Reasoning 基准上优于图像和视频基线方法，在小目标定位、零样本泛化及可解释性方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在自动驾驶场景理解中面临时序推理与空间精度之间的根本性权衡：单帧或低分辨率输入容易遗漏小、远或部分遮挡的危险目标，而语言为中心的驾驶模型往往缺乏对解释的定位证据。

Method: UniDrive 包含两个分支：时序推理分支从多帧视觉输入建模场景动态，高分辨率感知分支从最新帧保留细粒度空间细节。两者通过门控交叉注意力融合模块整合，使动态上下文与精确空间证据对齐。基于融合表示，联合生成自然语言风险描述和风险目标的边界框。

Result: 在 DRAMA-Reasoning 基准上，UniDrive 在描述生成和风险目标定位任务上均优于代表性图像基线和视频基线方法。在小目标定位、零样本泛化到 NuScenes 和 BDD100K 数据集以及人工评分的可解释性和可信度方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 显式结合时序语义和高分辨率感知为可解释且面向安全的自动驾驶系统提供了更坚实的基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UniDrive，一个用于自动驾驶场景风险理解的统一视觉语言与定位框架，属于通用视觉语言模型（MLLM）在自动驾驶领域的应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Strong in autonomous driving MLLM grounding but outside user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: MLLM for driving, temporal-spatial fusion, risk grounding, zero-shot generalization, DRAMA-Reasoning benchmark

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for autonomous driving scene understanding, yet existing methods still face a fundamental trade-off between temporal reasoning and spatial precision. Models that rely on single-frame or low-resolution inputs often miss small, distant, or partially occluded hazards, while language-centric driving models frequently provide limited grounded evidence for their explanations. To address this gap, we propose UniDrive, a unified visual-language and grounding framework for interpretable risk understanding in autonomous driving. UniDrive combines a temporal reasoning branch that models scene dynamics from multi-frame visual input with a high-resolution perception branch that preserves fine-grained spatial details from the latest frame. The two branches are integrated through a gated cross-attention fusion module, enabling dynamic context to be aligned with precise spatial evidence. Based on the fused representation, UniDrive jointly generates natural-language risk descriptions and grounded bounding-box outputs for risk objects. Experiments on the DRAMA-Reasoning benchmark show that UniDrive outperforms representative image-based and video-based baselines in both captioning and risk-object grounding. In particular, UniDrive achieves the best overall performance on the validation split and demonstrates clear advantages in small-object localization, zero-shot generalization to NuScenes and BDD100K, and human-rated interpretability and trustworthiness. These results suggest that explicitly combining temporal semantics and high-resolution perception provides a stronger foundation for interpretable and safety-oriented autonomous driving systems. The code is available at .

</details>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

VQA、文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能。

### [96] [Ground Then Rank: Revisiting Knowledge-Based VQA with Training-Free Entity Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23881)
*Qian Ma, Qiong Wu, Zhengyi Zhou, Yao Ma*

Main category: cs.IR, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 IBA（Identify-Before-Answer）框架，将知识型 VQA 中的实体识别与证据排序解耦，利用 MLLM 从候选名称中选择高置信度实体，再通过文本重排序器进行证据选择，无需训练即可在 Encyclopedic-VQA 和 InfoSeek 上超越微调的多模态重排序基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识型 VQA 的多模态检索增强生成（MM-RAG）方法将实体判别和段落级证据排序耦合在单一重排序阶段，导致计算成本高且泛化能力有限。作者观察到 MLLM 在开放命名场景下表现不佳，但在小候选集中选择实体时准确率更高，因此希望解耦这两个步骤以提升效果和效率。

Method: 提出 IBA（Identify-Before-Answer）框架，分为两步：(1) 实体识别阶段：利用 MLLM 从候选实体名称列表中选择高置信度实体；(2) 证据排序阶段：使用现成的文本重排序器（off-the-shelf textual re-ranker）对检索到的段落进行证据选择。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在 Encyclopedic-VQA 和 InfoSeek 两个基准上，IBA 方法一致优于经过微调的多模态重排序基线，同时降低了训练和推理复杂度。进一步分析表明，性能提升不仅来自更好的实体识别，还来自在正确实体固定后能够选择更具信息量的证据。

Conclusion: 解耦实体识别与证据排序是提升知识型 VQA 性能的有效策略，MLLM 在受限候选集上的实体选择能力足以替代复杂的端到端多模态重排序方法，为训练高效的 MM-RAG 系统提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 Knowledge-Based VQA（知识型视觉问答），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答（VQA）范畴，与 canonical 子主题 "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on KB-VQA entity grounding, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Training-free entity identification, Decoupled entity and evidence ranking, Outperforms fine-tuned baselines, Multimodal retrieval-augmented generation, No priority subtopic alignment

Abstract: Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) requires grounding visual queries to external knowledge beyond directly observable content in images. While recent multi modal large language models (MLLMs) show strong perceptual abilities, they struggle on KB-VQA tasks requiring groundings from both fine-grained entity and evidence levels. Most existing multi-modal retrieval augmented generation (MM-RAG) methods tightly couple entity discrimination and section-level evidence ranking into a single re-ranking stage, leading to high cost and limited generalization. In this work, we revisit existing MM-RAG solutions from a workflow perspective and argue both entity-level and fact-level groundings are key bottlenecks. We observe that although MLLMs often fail under open-ended entity naming, they can better identify the correct entity when selecting from a small set of candidate names. Based on this insight, we propose a simple and training-free identify-before-answer IBA framework that decouples entity identification from section-level re-ranking. Our approach prompts an MLLM to select high-confidence entities using only candidate names, followed by an off-the-shelf textual re-ranker for evidence selection. Experiments on Encyclopedic-VQA and InfoSeek show that our method consistently outperforms fine-tuned multi-modal re-ranking baselines while reducing training and inference complexity. Additional analyses reveal that the improvements arise not only from better entity identification, but also from selecting more informative evidence once correct entity is fixed. Our implementation is made public to ease reproducibility.

</details>


### [97] [VisChronos: Revolutionizing Image Captioning Through Real-Life Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24058)
*Phuc-Tan Nguyen, Hieu Nguyen, Minh-Triet Tran, Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisChronos 利用大语言模型和密集描述模型，从单张图像中识别并描述真实世界历史事件，生成上下文感知的事件级图像描述，并构建了 EventCap 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像描述方法难以捕捉图像中蕴含的上下文叙事和真实世界事件知识，视觉内容与自然语言理解之间存在语义鸿沟。作者希望通过引入历史事件知识来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出 VisChronos 框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）和密集描述模型（dense captioning model），从单张输入图像中自动识别并生成详细的事件描述。同时利用该框架构建了 EventCap 数据集，用于增强模型对复杂事件的识别与理解能力。

Result: 用户研究表明，VisChronos 在生成准确、连贯且聚焦事件的描述方面具有有效性，相比传统方法在描述质量和上下文相关性上有显著提升。

Conclusion: VisChronos 成功将真实世界历史事件知识融入图像描述生成，为事件驱动的图像理解研究开辟了新方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于历史事件增强图像描述（image captioning）的框架 VisChronos，属于视觉问答与图像描述范畴。虽然引入了事件知识，但核心任务仍是图像描述生成，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_vqa_doc（视觉问答与文档理解），该 subtopic 涵盖 VQA 和图像描述。

Importance reason: Focuses on event-level captioning, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: event-centric captioning, dense captioning, dataset construction, not autoregressive or diffusion, no continuous token or world model

Abstract: This paper aims to bridge the semantic gap between visual content and natural language understanding by leveraging historical events in the real world as a source of knowledge for caption generation. We propose VisChronos, a novel framework that utilizes large language models and dense captioning models to identify and describe real-life events from a single input image. Our framework can automatically generate detailed and context-aware event descriptions, enhancing the descriptive quality and contextual relevance of generated captions to address the limitations of traditional methods in capturing contextual narratives. Furthermore, we introduce a new dataset, EventCap ( ), specifically constructed using the proposed framework, designed to enhance the model's ability to identify and understand complex events. The user study demonstrates the efficacy of our solution in generating accurate, coherent, and event-focused descriptions, paving the way for future research in event-centric image understanding.

</details>


### [98] [An LMM for Precisely Grounding Elements in Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24118)
*Yijian Lu, Chuangxin Zhao, Kai Sun, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li, Ji Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (49.29) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: PreciseDoc 是一个专为文档图像中精确元素定位设计的 LMM，通过合成高质量手写文档数据与强化学习联合训练定位与推理，显著提升了文档视觉定位和文档 VQA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LMM 在文本密集的文档图像中定位精度差，难以准确找到关键文档元素以支持可靠推理，缺乏专门针对文档精确元素定位的模型。

Method: 构建两条数据合成管线批量生成带细粒度坐标标注的高质量文档（含相机效果的手写填充文档）；提出视觉定位推理训练范式，用强化学习联合监督定位与推理过程，使定位证据更好地服务于推理。

Result: 在多个文档空间定位和文档理解基准上，PreciseDoc 相比现有方法取得了显著优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的合成数据方法和视觉定位推理训练范式能有效提升 LMM 在文档图像中的精确元素定位能力和文档理解能力。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于文档图像中的精确视觉定位（visual grounding）和文档VQA，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的文档理解范畴，与现有规范子主题"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on document visual grounding, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key directions like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Document visual grounding, LMM for precise element localization, synthetic hand-filled document data, reinforcement learning for grounded reasoning, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Visual grounding in documents is a crucial ability for Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) in areas such as document understanding, deep research and document error detection. However, existing approaches exhibit poor grounding precision in text-rich document images, often failing to accurately locate the critical document elements needed for reliable reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce PreciseDoc, an LMM specifically designed for precise element grounding and can be further optimized for Document VQA tasks. Specifically, to enhance the basic localization capability, we construct challenging training data by two pipelines capable of mass-producing high-quality documents with paired metadata of fine-grained coordinates, including synthetic hand-filled documents with camera effects. The model develops more real-world functions beyond straightforward localization of single text, such as locating personal information from CVs. Furthermore, we introduce a training paradigm for visual grounded reasoning where the grounding and reasoning are supervised jointly with reinforcement learning to improve the contribution of the grounded evidence. A comprehensive evaluation on various benchmarks demonstrates the advantage of the proposed data and methods in document spatial grounding and document understanding.

</details>


### [99] [Ill-Posed by Design: Probing Evidence Use in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24335)
*Boaz Meivar, Shaked Perek, Shani Shvartzman, Eli Schwartz, Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出单目度量物体尺寸估计作为病态诊断任务，构建 Metric VQA 基准（10,813 个查询），通过反事实分析分解六种视觉和语言证据通道，评估 12 个开源 VLM（3B-397B），发现最大模型在野外场景上仍落后于纯文本 LLM，且模型未能有效利用场景几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实分析在良态任务中诊断价值有限，因为多个线索独立支持同一答案时移除一个不会改变预测。需要一种病态（ill-posed）诊断设置来有效研究 VLM 的证据选择行为。

Method: 提出单目度量物体尺寸估计作为病态诊断任务，构建 Metric VQA 基准（来自 Objectron 的 10,813 个维度查询和 331 个野外真实测量场景），对 12 个开源 VLM（3B-397B）进行反事实分析，分解六种证据通道：类别先验、目标外观、局部上下文、图像表观尺寸、场景几何和语言线索。

Result: 最大 VLM（Qwen3-VL-235B、Qwen3.5-397B、InternVL3.5-241B）在野外场景上仍落后于纯文本 LLM；目标身份是最重要的线索；目标像素和局部上下文仅对部分模型有帮助；表观尺寸会改变预测但缺乏方向性解读；全局场景几何基本未被利用；LoRA 微调虽可学习该任务但模型未学会利用场景几何。

Conclusion: 单目度量尺寸估计作为病态诊断任务能有效揭示 VLM 的证据使用模式，当前 VLM 在利用场景几何等关键线索方面存在显著不足，即使通过微调也难以学会利用几何信息。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Metric VQA 基准，研究 VLM 在单目物体尺寸估计这一病态问题中的证据使用，属于视觉问答（VQA）范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Probes VLM evidence use via ill-posed metric estimation, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: VLM diagnostic benchmark, counterfactual analysis, metric size estimation, scene geometry underuse, LoRA fine-tuning intervention

Abstract: Counterfactual analysis is widely used to study evidence use in vision-language models, but its diagnostic value is limited on well-posed tasks: when several cues independently support the same answer, removing one may not change the prediction. We propose monocular metric object-size estimation as an ill-posed diagnostic setting for evidence selection: because physical size cannot be determined from a single uncalibrated image, models must rely on imperfect cues category priors, target appearance, local context, apparent image size, and scene geometry. We assemble Metric VQA ($10{,}813$ dimension queries from Objectron and $331$ tape-measured in-the-wild scenes) and evaluate $12$ open-weight VLMs ($3$--$397$\,B parameters) with counterfactual analysis decomposing six visual and language evidence channels. Even the largest VLMs tested (Qwen3-VL-235B, Qwen3.5-397B, InternVL3.5-241B) trail a text-only frontier LLM on the in-the-wild split. The diagnostic analysis shows: target identity is the most load-bearing cue, target pixels and local context help only some models, apparent size shifts predictions without a directional readout, and global scene geometry is largely unused. We analyze LoRA fine-tuning as an actionable intervention specific to metric estimation: while the task is learnable, the models do not learn to leverage scene geometry.

</details>


### [100] [Open-Vocabulary BEV Segmentation with 3D-Aware Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24353)
*Hojun Choi, Seulbin Hwang, Dae Jung Kim, Kisung Kim, Hyunjung Shim, Jinhan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出OVBEVSeg，一种面向开放词汇BEV分割的几何感知框架，通过3D几何约束将2D VLM语义提升到BEV空间，在nuScenes上未见类别mIoU超越闭集方法15.3，且推理速度比投影法快2.5倍、显存仅0.22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV感知方法局限于闭集场景，无法识别训练集之外的类别，难以应对真实世界的开放环境。将VLM的开放词汇能力引入BEV分割面临2D语义到3D BEV提升过程中的几何不一致性问题。

Method: 提出OVBEVSeg框架，基于高斯泼溅（GS）反投影，通过三个渐进阶段引入3D几何约束：(1) 2D到BEV伪标签生成，利用可靠3D投影实现开放词汇泛化；(2) 联合2D-BEV逐场景优化，引入BEV结构约束保证3D几何一致性；(3) 3D几何蒸馏，实现在线推理效率。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，未见类别mIoU超越闭集方法15.3；即使无未见类标注，仍与使用40%标注的自/半监督基线竞争；推理速度比投影法快2.5倍，显存仅0.22倍。

Conclusion: OVBEVSeg首次实现了高效、实时的开放词汇BEV分割，通过3D几何约束有效解决了2D VLM语义到BEV提升中的几何不一致问题，为自动驾驶中的开放世界感知提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究开放词汇BEV分割，利用VLM进行视觉感知与语义理解，属于多模态视觉问答与场景理解范畴。虽然具体任务是BEV分割，但核心是利用VLM进行开放词汇识别与语义分割，与multimodal_vqa_doc（视觉问答与文档理解）中的视觉理解任务高度相关，且该[CANONICAL]条目可覆盖此类视觉感知与理解工作。

Importance reason: Strong BEV segmentation work but outside priority subtopics and uses negative keywords (autonomous driving, closed-set). Signals: Open-vocabulary BEV segmentation, 3D geometric constraints, Gaussian splatting, nuScenes benchmark, real-time efficiency

Abstract: Bird's-eye view (BEV) perception fuses multi-camera images into a unified top-down representation for autonomous driving. Despite recent progress, state-of-the-art methods remain confined to closed-set scenarios, making them vulnerable to unpredictable real-world environments. In this work, we introduce open-vocabulary BEV segmentation (OVBS), which leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to recognize categories beyond the training set while maintaining precise BEV perception and real-time efficiency. A key challenge in OVBS lies in the 3D geometric inconsistency inherent in the ill-posed lifting of 2D VLM semantics into BEV. To address this, we propose OVBEVSeg, a geometry-aware OVBS framework that enhances efficient Gaussian splatting (GS)-based unprojection by leveraging robust 3D geometric constraints across three progressive stages: (1) 2D-to-BEV pseudo-labeling via reliable 3D projection for OV generalization; (2) joint 2D-BEV per-scene optimization with BEV structural constraints for 3D geometric consistency; and (3) 3D geometric distillation for online efficiency. On the nuScenes dataset, OVBEVSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming closed-set methods by 15.3 mIoU on unseen categories. Remarkably, even with no novel-class ground-truth labels, it remains competitive with self- and semi-supervised baselines trained with up to 40% of ground-truth annotations. Furthermore, it achieves 2.5x faster inference with only 0.22x the memory consumption of projection-based methods. Project page: .

</details>


### [101] [P-MTP: Efficient Document Parsing via Multi-Token Prediction with Progressive Depth Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24447)
*Le Xiang, Chenxi Zhai, Shu Wei, Jingjing Wu, Qunyi Xie, Xiao Tan, Kunbin Chen, Wei He*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (63.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: P-MTP 提出渐进式多token预测框架，通过渐进课程损失和置信门控动态草稿机制，在文档解析任务上实现高达5倍推理加速，且精度损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在文档解析中面临严重的延迟瓶颈，尤其是token密集的文档。多token预测（MTP）虽有加速潜力，但深层预测时存在优化不稳定的问题。本文旨在解决MTP在深层look-ahead下的训练不稳定问题，实现高效文档解析。

Method: 提出P-MTP框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 渐进课程损失（Progressive Curriculum Loss），通过累积路径可靠性和回顾目标一致性自适应重加权不同look-ahead深度的损失，抑制长程预测中的梯度噪声，实现从易到难的优化过渡；(2) 置信门控动态草稿（Confidence-Gated Dynamic Drafting），在推理时根据置信度自适应校准推测长度，最大化有效look-ahead深度和接受率，减少计算浪费。

Result: 在多个基准和架构上，P-MTP实现了高达5倍的速度提升，且精度损失可忽略不计，首次在大规模look-ahead MTP的文档解析领域验证了有效性。

Conclusion: P-MTP通过渐进式训练和动态推理策略，成功将多token预测扩展到深层look-ahead，为VLM文档解析提供了高效的加速方案，具有实际应用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于文档解析（document parsing），属于视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一[CANONICAL]子主题的范畴。论文提出多token预测加速VLM文档解析，核心应用场景是文档理解。

Importance reason: Proposes efficient inference via multi-token prediction, aligning with model efficiency priority but not directly on core continuous multimodal or video generation topics. Signals: Multi-token prediction, inference acceleration, document parsing, progressive curriculum loss, confidence-gated drafting

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have revolutionized document parsing by enabling end-to-end mapping from images to structured text, imposing a significant latency bottleneck, particularly for token-dense documents. While Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating inference, its potential is constrained by optimization instability when scaling to deeper look-ahead depth. In this paper, we propose \textbf{P-MTP}, a framework that leverages \textbf{Progressive Multi-Token Prediction} with a lightweight MTP module to scale the look-ahead depth for high-throughput document parsing. Specifically, we introduce Progressive Curriculum Loss that adaptively re-weights different look-ahead depths using cumulative path reliability and retrospective target consistency. By effectively suppressing gradient noise in long-range predictions, P-MTP, facilitates an automated easy-to-hard optimization transition, enabling the model to master increasingly distant look-ahead depths. Furthermore, we propose Confidence-Gated Dynamic Drafting to maximize the effective look-ahead depth and acceptance rate by adaptively calibrating speculative length during inference, thereby minimizing computational waste and further pushing the boundaries of inference speedup. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks and architectures demonstrate that P-MTP, achieves up to a $5\times$ speedup with negligible loss in accuracy, providing the first successful validation of extensive look-ahead MTP in the document parsing domain.

</details>


### [102] [Boosting Text-Driven Video Segmentation via Geometry-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24464)
*Tianyu Zhu, Yingping Liang, Hesong Li, Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoLaV，一种两阶段框架，通过从单目图像中蒸馏3D几何知识来增强文本引导的视频目标分割（RVOS）。第一阶段利用单目新视角合成进行几何预训练，获得几何一致的视觉表征；第二阶段引入几何感知蒸馏，将3D先验模型的结构知识迁移到视频分割模型中，提升时空一致性和语言定位能力。仅用图像分割数据即可实现零样本RVOS，微调后达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导视频目标分割（RVOS）模型通常在2D图像或视频数据集上使用朴素分割损失训练，忽略了帧间的几何一致性，导致空间理解能力弱。作者希望引入3D几何知识来增强模型对视频中物体空间位置和跨帧一致性的理解。

Method: GeoLaV两阶段框架：第一阶段，在单目图像上进行几何预训练，通过单目新视角合成任务学习几何一致的视觉表征，利用空间对齐损失在大规模单图像数据集上训练；第二阶段，引入几何感知蒸馏，利用通用3D先验模型（如3D感知模型）将3D结构知识迁移到视频分割模型，在视频分割数据集上微调，同时增强时空一致性和语言定位能力。

Result: 仅使用图像分割数据即可在RVOS任务上实现显著的零样本泛化能力；结合几何感知蒸馏在视频上微调后，在多个RVOS基准数据集上取得了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: 通过蒸馏3D几何知识到2D视频分割模型中，可以有效提升文本引导视频目标分割的时空一致性和语言定位能力，验证了几何先验对视频理解任务的重要价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本引导的视频目标分割（Referring Video Object Segmentation），属于视觉问答与细粒度视觉理解范畴。虽然论文侧重分割而非问答，但RVOS是视觉语言理解的重要任务，与multimodal_vqa_doc（视觉问答与文档理解）中的细粒度视觉理解方向高度相关，且该[CANONICAL] subtopic是现有最匹配的类别。

Importance reason: Focuses on video segmentation, not aligned with user's priority on generation, world models, or continuous representations. Signals: Video segmentation, Geometry distillation, Zero-shot RVOS, Not generation, Not continuous model

Abstract: Text-driven Referring Video Object Segmentation (RVOS) aims to locate and segment target objects in videos given natural language. However, existing models are typically trained on 2D image or video datasets with naive segmentation losses, which overlooks the geometric consistency across frames and leads to weak spatial understanding. In this paper, we propose Geometry-enhanced Language-guided Video segmentation (GeoLaV), a two-stage framework that distills 3D geometric knowledge from images to enhance text-driven video segmentation. In the first stage, we perform monocular geometry pretraining with monocular novel-view synthesis, enabling the model to acquire geometry-consistent visual representations via spatial alignment on large-scale single-image datasets. In the second stage, we introduce geometry-aware distillation and fine-tune the model on video segmentation datasets, transferring 3D structural knowledge from a general 3D prior model. This process reinforces 3D awareness and improves both spatiotemporal coherence and language grounding in segmentation. Extensive experiments show that our method using only image segmentation data already provides notable zero-shot generalization in RVOS. When combined with geometry-aware distillation for fine-tuning on videos, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple RVOS benchmarks. The code is available at .

</details>


### [103] [CineCap: Structured Reasoning with Spatio-Temporal Anchors for Cinematographic Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24636)
*Xinyu Mao, Yuhui Zeng, Xiaokun Liu, Wenyu Qin, Meng Wang, Xin Tao, Pengfei Wan, Xiaohan Xing, Max Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CineCap 是一个面向电影摄影视频描述（cinematographic captioning）的框架，通过结构化推理与时空锚点将专业电影语言概念（镜头运动、景别、景深、构图、拍摄角度等）显式地锚定到视觉证据中，并结合强化学习（包含完整性、准确性和门控覆盖奖励）来平衡描述的全面性与事实正确性。同时构建了包含472个手工标注视频-描述对的 CineCap Bench 基准，实验表明该方法显著优于现有闭源和开源基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在电影级视频理解方面能力不足，尤其是缺乏对专业电影语言概念（如镜头运动、景别、景深、构图、拍摄角度等）进行统一开放形式描述的能力。已有的电影理解工作多基于问答形式评估，而电影摄影描述需要模型从细微视觉线索中推断专业概念并生成全面准确的描述，这是一个尚未被充分探索的任务。

Method: CineCap 框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 结构化推理与时空锚点——将专业电影摄影描述显式地锚定到具体的视觉证据中，并将其组织为紧凑的原子推理步骤，用于监督微调；(2) 强化学习——设计包含完整性（comprehensiveness）、准确性（accuracy）和门控覆盖（gated coverage）三种奖励的强化学习策略，以改善描述完整性与事实正确性之间的平衡。此外，还构建了 CineCap Bench 基准数据集（472个手工标注视频-描述对）用于系统评测。

Result: 大量实验表明，CineCap 在电影摄影描述任务上持续优于强大的闭源和开源基线模型，建立了新的最优性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: CineCap 通过结构化推理与时空锚点结合强化学习的方法，有效解决了电影摄影视频描述这一具有挑战性的任务，为细粒度视频理解和可控电影级视频生成提供了重要基础。

Classification reason: 论文研究电影摄影视频描述（cinematographic captioning），属于视频理解与视觉问答的范畴。该任务涉及对视频中镜头运动、景别、景深、构图、拍摄角度等专业电影语言概念进行结构化描述，本质上是一种细粒度的视频问答/描述任务。映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）也可行，但更核心的是视觉问答与视频理解方向。考虑到论文构建了 CineCap Bench 基准并进行了系统评测，同时任务本身是开放形式的视频描述生成，与 VQA/文档理解中的结构化描述任务最为接近，因此选择 multimodal_vqa_doc。

Importance reason: Focuses on niche cinematographic captioning with structured reasoning, but lacks direct alignment with priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Cinematographic captioning, structured reasoning with spatio-temporal anchors, reinforcement learning for comprehensiveness and accuracy, CineCap Bench benchmark, multimodal understanding generation direction

Abstract: Cinematographic captioning aims to describe how a video is filmed using professional film-language concepts such as camera movement, shot size, depth of field, composition, and shooting angle. This capability is important for fine-grained video understanding and controllable movie-quality video generation, yet remains underexplored in existing multimodal large language models. Unlike question-answering-based evaluation of cinematic understanding, cinematographic captioning requires a unified open-form description over multiple cinematographic dimensions. This task is challenging for two main reasons: the model must infer professional cinematographic concepts from subtle visual evidence, and it must generate captions that are both comprehensive and accurate. Accordingly, we propose CineCap, a framework that combines structured reasoning with spatio-temporal anchors and reinforcement learning with comprehensiveness, accuracy, and gated coverage rewards. The former grounds professional cinematographic descriptions in explicit visual evidence and organizes them into compact atomic reasoning for supervised fine-tuning, while the latter improves the balance between descriptive completeness and factual correctness. In addition, we construct CineCap Bench, a benchmark of 472 manually annotated video-caption pairs for systematic evaluation. Extensive experiments show that CineCap consistently outperforms strong proprietary and open-source baselines, establishing a new state of the art for cinematographic captioning. The code, model checkpoint, and benchmark are publicly available in .

</details>


### [104] [ViTexQA: A Multi-Frame Temporal Perception Dataset for Video Text Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24602)
*Zhentao Guo, Chen Duan, Tongkun Guan, Zining Wang, Kai Zhou, Pengfei Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (74.24) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ViTexQA 是一个大规模视频文本问答数据集，要求模型通过跨多帧融合时序分布的文本线索来回答问题。同时提出 FrameThinker 模型，采用两阶段训练（CoT引导的SFT + 时序强化学习）进行显式时序建模，在 ViTexQA 上 ROUGE-L 提升 6.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在视频文本理解上存在局限，尤其是需要跨多帧整合时序分布文本线索才能理解语义的场景。现有数据集大多问题可从单帧回答，无法反映真实视频文本理解需求。

Method: 构建 ViTexQA 数据集，通过质量控制的 CoT 标注流程并加入时序约束，确保所有 QA 对都需要跨帧文本融合才能解答。提出 FrameThinker 模型，采用两阶段训练：CoT-Guided SFT 生成帧感知推理链，然后使用时序强化学习（Temporally-grounded RL）优化多帧连贯性奖励。

Result: 在 ViTexQA 上，FrameThinker 超越 SOTA 基线，ROUGE-L 提升 6.3%。

Conclusion: ViTexQA 填补了视频文本理解中跨帧时序推理数据集的空白，FrameThinker 的两阶段训练策略有效提升了模型在需要多帧文本融合任务上的表现。

Classification reason: 该论文提出视频文本问答数据集 ViTexQA 和模型 FrameThinker，核心任务是视频中的文本理解与跨帧时序推理，属于视觉问答（VQA）范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态理解与生成方向下的 "视觉问答与文档理解" 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on video text QA with temporal fusion, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: Video text QA dataset, Multi-frame temporal reasoning, CoT-guided SFT + RL, ROUGE-L improvement, Not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in multimodal understanding, current MLLMs still exhibit limitations in video text understanding, particularly when semantics emerge through the integration of temporally distributed textual cues across multiple frames. This perception challenge fundamentally differs from static image text understanding, yet existing datasets fail to capture: the vast majority of questions remain answerable from single frames, inadequately reflecting real-world video text comprehension demands. To address this, we present ViTexQA, a large-scale video-text QA dataset, and FrameThinker for robust multi-frame temporal reasoning. We build ViTexQA via a quality-controlled Chain-of-Thought (CoT) annotation pipeline boosted with temporal constraints; all its QA pairs demand cross-frame text fusion to solve, enforcing true temporal reliance. FrameThinker adopts two-stage training for explicit temporal modeling: CoT-Guided Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) generates frame-aware reasoning chains, followed by Temporally-grounded Reinforcement Learning (RL) optimized with multi-frame coherence rewards. Evaluations show our method outperforms SOTA baselines on ViTexQA, lifting ROUGE-L by 6.3%.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-video_understanding_temporal'></div>

### [105] [video-SALMONN-R$^3$: Learning to ReWatch, ReAsk, and ReAnswer for Efficient Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24477)
*Yixuan Li, Guangzhi Sun, Yudong Yang, Wei Li, Zejun MA, Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.SD, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 普通相关 (61.6) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: video-SALMONN-R³ 提出了一种基于强化学习的视频 LLM 两阶段理解范式：先粗看定位相关片段，再以更高时空精度重看这些片段，并引入 re-ask（重问）和 re-answer（重答）机制来提升问答质量，无需昂贵的 CoT 冷启动数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频 LLM 受限于计算和内存预算，通常使用降低的帧率和空间分辨率，可能遗漏关键信息。现有两阶段方法依赖 CoT 冷启动数据标注，成本高且 CoT-SFT 会损害预训练的视频理解能力。

Method: 提出 video-SALMONN-R³，包含三个核心设计：(1) ReWatch——通过强化学习训练模型在粗看后自动定位并重看相关视频片段；(2) ReAsk——在重看时重新注入查询问题，增强问题遵循能力；(3) ReAnswer——模型先在第一轮给出直接答案，重看后再精炼答案，解决推理优先与答案优先行为的不匹配。无需 CoT 冷启动数据。

Result: 实验表明 video-SALMONN-R³ 在多个视频问答基准上一致优于基础模型和 QA-SFT 基线，并超越先前基于重看的方法，同时计算成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习实现端到端的重看机制，结合重问和重答策略，可以有效提升视频 LLM 的理解能力，同时避免昂贵的 CoT 数据标注和 SFT 带来的能力退化。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 video-SALMONN-R³，一种通过强化学习实现"重看"机制的视频 LLM，用于高效视频理解与问答。核心贡献在于视频理解中的时序定位与问答，属于视频 LLM 和视频问答范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视频理解与时序推理" (video_understanding_temporal) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes efficient two-stage video understanding with RL-based re-watch, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or generation. Signals: efficient video understanding, reinforcement learning, re-watch mechanism, video LLM, re-answer strategy

Abstract: Video large language models (LLMs) are often constrained by computation and memory budgets, leading them to use reduced frame rates and spatial resolutions, which may cause them to miss critical information for question answering (QA). A practical and efficient solution is a two-stage paradigm: first perform coarse video understanding to localize relevant segments, and then re-watch these segments at higher temporal or spatial fidelity. In this paper, we present video-SALMONN-R$^3$, the first end-to-end video-LLM that enables re-watch through reinforcement learning without relying on chain-of-thought (CoT) cold-start. This design removes the need for costly CoT data annotations and avoids CoT-based supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which can otherwise degrade the pretrained video understanding abilities. To address the mismatch between the reasoning-first behavior induced by re-watch and the answer-first tendency of pretrained video-LLMs, we propose a re-answer strategy, in which the model first produces a direct answer in the first watch and then refines it after re-watching. Finally, to improve question adherence during re-watching, we propose a re-ask mechanism that re-injects the query when revisiting localized segments. Experimental results show that video-SALMONN-R$^3$ consistently outperforms both the base model and the QA-SFT baseline, while surpassing prior re-watch-based approaches with significantly lower computational cost. Code, models, and data will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>
